	-- Rebuild the hangar plane from scratch: the flight copy carries trails and
	-- exhaust emitters that would keep puffing while parked on the pedestal.
	if planeModel then
		planeModel:Destroy()
		planeModel = nil
	end
	buildMenuPlane()
	buildMenuAllies()
	showHangar()

	State.EmitRunEnded({
		Reason = reason,
		Distance = math.floor(distance),
		Kills = kills,
		Coins = coinsCollected,
		Gems = gemsCollected,
		Combo = comboSteps,
		BestCombo = maxCombo,
	})
end

-- Rebuilds the hangar plane and allies from the current player data. Call this
-- after the player equips a different plane or ally in the menu.
function Flight.RefreshHangar()
	if running then
		return
	end
	buildMenuPlane()
	buildMenuAllies()
end

function Flight.Init()
	if initialized then
		return
	end
	initialized = true

	rememberLightingDefaults()
	buildHangar()
	buildMenuPlane()
	buildMenuAllies()
	showHangar()
	if not hangarIdleConnection then
		hangarIdleConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(updateHangarIdle)
	end

	if player.Character then
		watchCharacter(player.Character)
	end
	if characterAddedConnection then
		characterAddedConnection:Disconnect()
	end
	characterAddedConnection = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
		watchCharacter(character)
		task.defer(function()
			if characterHidden or running then
				hideCharacter()
				setCameraTarget(CFrame.new(planePos), FLIGHT_CAMERA_OFFSET)
			else
				placeCharacterInHangar()
			end
		end)
	end)

	State.OnLoaded(function()
		if not running then
			buildMenuPlane()
			buildMenuAllies()
			showHangar()
		end

		-- Dev hook: when ReplicatedStorage.DevAutoRun is a true BoolValue the
		-- first run starts by itself. Used by automated playtests; absent in a
		-- published place it does nothing.
		local flag = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("DevAutoRun")
		if flag and flag:IsA("BoolValue") and flag.Value and not running then
			task.delay(0.75, function()
				if not running then
					Flight.StartRun()
				end
			end)
		end
	end)

	State.OnUpdate(function(data)
		if running or not data then
			return
		end
		local equipped = data.EquippedHangar or "Cardboard"
		if equipped ~= hangarThemeId then
			showHangar(true)
		end
	end)

	inputBeganConnection = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
		if processed or not running then
			return
		end

		local inputType = input.UserInputType
		if inputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			-- Only the first finger steers and fires; extra fingers are ignored
			-- so lifting one cannot cancel the other.
			if activeTouch then
				return
			end
			activeTouch = input
			lastTouch = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
			fireScreenPos = lastTouch
			fireSources.touch = true
		elseif inputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			fireSources.mouse = true
		elseif inputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and input.KeyCode == keyBindings.Fire then
			fireSources.keyboard = true
		else
			return
		end
		refreshFireState()
	end)

	inputChangedConnection = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if running and activeTouch and input == activeTouch then
			lastTouch = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
			if fireSources.touch then
				fireScreenPos = lastTouch
			end
		end
	end)

	inputEndedConnection = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		local inputType = input.UserInputType
		if inputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			if input ~= activeTouch then
				return
			end
			activeTouch = nil
			lastTouch = nil
			fireSources.touch = false
		elseif inputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			fireSources.mouse = false
		elseif inputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and input.KeyCode == keyBindings.Fire then
			fireSources.keyboard = false
		else
			return
		end
		refreshFireState()
	end)

	-- Releasing a button while the window is unfocused never delivers InputEnded,
	-- which used to leave the weapon firing forever.
	focusReleasedConnection = UserInputService.WindowFocusReleased:Connect(resetPointerInput)
end

local function disconnect(conn: RBXScriptConnection?)
	if conn then
		conn:Disconnect()
	end
end

-- Tears the controller down completely (hot reloads, tests). It does not send
-- RunResult, so a run in progress is abandoned rather than reported.
function Flight.Destroy()
	running = false
	disconnect(connection)
	connection = nil
	disconnect(characterAddedConnection)
	characterAddedConnection = nil
	disconnect(inputBeganConnection)
	inputBeganConnection = nil
	disconnect(inputChangedConnection)
	inputChangedConnection = nil
	disconnect(inputEndedConnection)
	inputEndedConnection = nil
	disconnect(focusReleasedConnection)
	focusReleasedConnection = nil
	disconnect(characterDescendantConnection)
	characterDescendantConnection = nil
	disconnect(hangarIdleConnection)
	hangarIdleConnection = nil

	resetPointerInput()
	clearRunWorld()
	cancelLightingTweens()
	restoreLightingDefaults()
	setDefaultControlsEnabled(true)
	showCharacter()

	-- Hand the avatar and camera back to Roblox's defaults.
	local character = player.Character
	if character then
		local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if root and root:IsA("BasePart") then
			root.Anchored = false
		end
		local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if humanoid then
			humanoid.CameraOffset = Vector3.zero
		end
	end
	local currentCamera = activeCamera()
	if currentCamera then
		currentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
	end

	destroyAllyUnits()
	if planeModel then
		planeModel:Destroy()
		planeModel = nil
	end
	if hangar then
		hangar:Destroy()
		hangar = nil
	end
	hangarThemeId = nil
	hangarEggs = {}

	initialized = false
end

return Flight

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/client/init.client.luau
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
--!nonstrict
-- Client bootstrap for Paper Wings.
-- This LocalScript is the "Client" container; its siblings are client modules.

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")
local Remotes = require(Shared:WaitForChild("Remotes"))

local State = require(script:WaitForChild("State"))
local Flight = require(script:WaitForChild("Flight"))
local UI = require(script:WaitForChild("UI"))

Remotes.Get(Remotes.DataLoaded).OnClientEvent:Connect(function(data)
	State.ApplyLoaded(data)
end)

Remotes.Get(Remotes.DataUpdate).OnClientEvent:Connect(function(data)
	State.ApplyUpdate(data)
end)

Remotes.Get(Remotes.Notify).OnClientEvent:Connect(function(text)
	State.EmitNotify(tostring(text))
end)

-- Ask for the snapshot now that the handlers above are connected. The server may
-- have pushed it before this script finished loading, so retry until it lands.
task.spawn(function()
	for _ = 1, 10 do
		if State.Data then
			return
		end
		Remotes.Get(Remotes.RequestData):FireServer()
		task.wait(1)
	end
	warn("[PaperWings] no data after 10s")
end)

Flight.Init()
UI.Init()

-- Live handles. Rojo re-creates module instances when it re-syncs, so anything
-- that needs the *running* state (tests, debug tools) must go through this
-- instead of requiring the modules again.
_G.PaperWings = { State = State, Flight = Flight, UI = UI, Remotes = Remotes }

print("[PaperWings] Client ready")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/client/PlaneBuilder.luau

-- ============================================================================
--!nonstrict
-- PlaneBuilder: builds the player's plane, allies, bosses and pets from parts.
--
-- Shape language: everything is folded paper. Panels are thin wedges layered at
-- slightly different angles, fold lines are thin accent strips, and engines are
-- the only neon elements so the eye reads motion first.
--
-- Forward is -Z. Every plane exposes named parts so Flight can animate:
--   "AileronL" / "AileronR"  bank with steering
--   "Propeller"              spins while flying
--   "Glow"                   engine/exhaust emitter anchor

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")
local GameConfig = require(Shared:WaitForChild("GameConfig"))

local PlaneBuilder = {}

local PAPER = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
local FOIL = Enum.Material.Foil
local GLASS = Enum.Material.Glass
local NEON = Enum.Material.Neon
local WOOD = Enum.Material.Wood

local function makePart(parent, className, name, size, cframe, color, material): BasePart
	local p = Instance.new(className)
	p.Name = name
	p.Size = size
	p.CFrame = cframe
	p.Color = color
	p.Material = material or PAPER
	p.Anchored = true
	p.CanCollide = false
	p.CanTouch = false
	p.CanQuery = false
	p.CastShadow = true
	p.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
	p.BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
	p.Parent = parent
	return p
end

-- Axis-aligned block at (x, y, z), optionally rotated (degrees).
local function block(parent, name, size, x, y, z, color, material, rx, ry, rz)
	local cf = CFrame.new(x, y, z) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(rx or 0), math.rad(ry or 0), math.rad(rz or 0))
	return makePart(parent, "Part", name, size, cf, color, material)
end

local function wedge(parent, name, size, x, y, z, color, material, rx, ry, rz)
	local cf = CFrame.new(x, y, z) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(rx or 0), math.rad(ry or 0), math.rad(rz or 0))
	return makePart(parent, "WedgePart", name, size, cf, color, material)
end

local function ball(parent, name, diameter, x, y, z, color, material)
	local p = makePart(
		parent,
		"Part",
		name,
		Vector3.new(diameter, diameter, diameter),
		CFrame.new(x, y, z),
		color,
		material
	)
	p.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
	return p
end

local function ellipsoid(parent, name, size, x, y, z, color, material, rx, ry, rz)
	local cf = CFrame.new(x, y, z) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(rx or 0), math.rad(ry or 0), math.rad(rz or 0))
	local p = makePart(parent, "Part", name, size, cf, color, material)
	p.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
	return p
end

local function cylinder(parent, name, size, x, y, z, color, material, rx, ry, rz)
	local cf = CFrame.new(x, y, z) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(rx or 0), math.rad(ry or 0), math.rad(rz or 0))
	local p = makePart(parent, "Part", name, size, cf, color, material)
	p.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
	return p
end

local function trim(parent, name, size, x, y, z, color, rx, ry, rz)
	return block(parent, name, size, x, y, z, color, NEON, rx, ry, rz)
end

-- A wing made of three tapering panels. Returns the aileron part.
local function wing(parent, side: number, opts)
	local color = opts.color
	local accent = opts.accent
	local rootX = opts.rootX or 1.2
	local span = opts.span or 3.0
	local sweep = opts.sweep or 10
	local dihedral = opts.dihedral or 4
	local thickness = opts.thickness or 0.16
	local chord = opts.chord or 3.4
	local y = opts.y or 0.1
	local z = opts.z or 0.2

	local x1 = side * (rootX + span * 0.5)
	local x2 = side * (rootX + span * 0.9)
	local x3 = side * (rootX + span * 1.25)

	local root = wedge(
		parent,
		"WingRoot",
		Vector3.new(span, thickness, chord),
		x1,
		y,
		z,
		color,
		PAPER,
		0,
		side * sweep * 0.35,
		side * dihedral
	)
	local mid = wedge(
		parent,
		"WingMid",
		Vector3.new(span * 0.8, thickness * 0.9, chord * 0.78),
		x2,
		y + 0.06,
		z + 0.25,
		color,
		PAPER,
		0,
		side * sweep * 0.7,
		side * dihedral * 1.15
	)
	wedge(
		parent,
		"WingTip",
		Vector3.new(span * 0.5, thickness * 0.8, chord * 0.55),
		x3,
		y + 0.12,
		z + 0.5,
		accent,
		PAPER,
		0,
		side * sweep,
		side * dihedral * 1.4
	)

	-- Fold lines: two thin strips that catch the light along the paper crease.
	block(parent, "FoldLine", Vector3.new(span * 0.95, 0.05, 0.08), x1, y + thickness * 0.55, z + 0.9, accent, PAPER)
	block(
		parent,
		"FoldLine2",
		Vector3.new(span * 0.7, 0.05, 0.08),
		x2,
		y + 0.1,
		z + 1.0,
		accent,
		PAPER,
		0,
		side * sweep * 0.7,
		side * dihedral * 1.15
	)

	-- Wingtip light.
	local light = block(parent, "WingLight", Vector3.new(0.28, 0.28, 0.28), x3, y + 0.2, z + 0.55, accent, NEON)

	-- Aileron sits behind the trailing edge and banks with the plane.
	local aileron = block(
		parent,
		if side > 0 then "AileronR" else "AileronL",
		Vector3.new(span * 0.85, 0.1, 0.75),
		x1,
		y,
		z + chord * 0.55,
		accent,
		PAPER,
		0,
		side * sweep * 0.35,
		side * dihedral
	)

	root.CanCollide = false
	return aileron, light
end

local function propeller(parent, x, y, z, color)
	local hub = block(parent, "PropHub", Vector3.new(0.3, 0.3, 0.3), x, y, z, color, FOIL)
	local disc = block(parent, "Propeller", Vector3.new(2.6, 2.6, 0.08), x, y, z - 0.12, color, GLASS, 0, 0, 0)
	disc.Transparency = 0.72
	disc.CastShadow = false
	return hub, disc
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- planes ---
local builders = {}

builders.Classic = function(config)
	local model = Instance.new("Model")
	model.Name = "Plane_Classic"
	local c, a = config.Color, config.Accent

	-- Folded dart fuselage: three stacked wedges give a proper keel silhouette.
	local body = wedge(model, "Body", Vector3.new(1.9, 1.0, 5.6), 0, 0, 0, c, PAPER)
	wedge(model, "BodyTop", Vector3.new(1.5, 0.7, 4.2), 0, 0.45, 0.2, c, PAPER)
	wedge(model, "Nose", Vector3.new(1.1, 0.55, 2.0), 0, -0.05, -3.4, a, PAPER)
	block(model, "Keel", Vector3.new(0.12, 1.3, 3.4), 0, -0.55, 0.4, a, PAPER)

	local aileronL = wing(model, -1, { color = c, accent = a, span = 3.2, chord = 3.6, sweep = 12, dihedral = 5, z = 0.3 })
	local aileronR = wing(model, 1, { color = c, accent = a, span = 3.2, chord = 3.6, sweep = 12, dihedral = 5, z = 0.3 })

	-- Tail assembly.
	wedge(model, "TailFin", Vector3.new(0.16, 1.7, 1.7), 0, 0.9, 2.5, c, PAPER)
	block(model, "TailFinTrim", Vector3.new(0.2, 0.25, 1.5), 0, 1.6, 2.6, a, NEON)
	wedge(model, "StabilizerL", Vector3.new(1.6, 0.12, 1.0), -1.5, 0.35, 2.5, c, PAPER, 0, -8, 0)
	wedge(model, "StabilizerR", Vector3.new(1.6, 0.12, 1.0), 1.5, 0.35, 2.5, c, PAPER, 0, 8, 0)

	-- Canopy and cockpit.
	local canopy = ellipsoid(model, "Canopy", Vector3.new(1.15, 0.8, 1.7), 0, 0.8, -0.6, Color3.fromRGB(170, 220, 255), GLASS)
	canopy.Transparency = 0.35
	block(model, "CockpitRim", Vector3.new(1.15, 0.12, 1.6), 0, 0.55, -0.6, a, PAPER)

	-- Paper pilot: two parts, just enough to read as a person at speed.
	ellipsoid(model, "PilotBody", Vector3.new(0.55, 0.7, 0.5), 0, 0.95, -0.15, Color3.fromRGB(60, 110, 200), PAPER)
	ball(model, "PilotHead", 0.44, 0, 1.42, -0.15, Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 148), PAPER)

	-- Nose tip light and engine glow.
	block(model, "Glow", Vector3.new(0.5, 0.3, 0.3), 0, 0.05, -4.35, a, NEON)
	block(model, "EngineGlow", Vector3.new(0.7, 0.25, 0.7), 0, 0.1, 2.95, a, NEON)

	model.PrimaryPart = body
	return model, aileronL, aileronR
end

builders.Glider = function(config)
	local model = Instance.new("Model")
	model.Name = "Plane_Glider"
	local c, a = config.Color, config.Accent

	-- No fuselage: a keel and a huge delta wing, like a paper hang glider.
	local body = block(model, "Body", Vector3.new(0.8, 0.5, 4.6), 0, 0, 0.2, c, PAPER)
	wedge(model, "Keel", Vector3.new(0.24, 1.1, 3.8), 0, -0.45, 0.4, a, PAPER)
	block(model, "Nose", Vector3.new(0.5, 0.3, 1.2), 0, 0, -2.4, a, PAPER)

	local aileronL = wing(model, -1, { color = c, accent = a, span = 4.4, chord = 4.6, sweep = 6, dihedral = 7, rootX = 0.8 })
	local aileronR = wing(model, 1, { color = c, accent = a, span = 4.4, chord = 4.6, sweep = 6, dihedral = 7, rootX = 0.8 })

	-- Crossbar and hang strap.
	block(model, "Crossbar", Vector3.new(9.4, 0.16, 0.16), 0, 0.35, 0.3, a, FOIL)
	block(model, "StrapA", Vector3.new(0.1, 1.0, 0.1), -0.35, -0.35, -0.4, Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 80), PAPER, 0, 0, 8)
	block(model, "StrapB", Vector3.new(0.1, 1.0, 0.1), 0.35, -0.35, -0.4, Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 80), PAPER, 0, 0, -8)

	-- Tiny harness pilot.
	ellipsoid(model, "PilotBody", Vector3.new(0.5, 0.8, 0.45), 0, -0.9, -0.15, Color3.fromRGB(90, 160, 120), PAPER)
	ball(model, "PilotHead", 0.42, 0, -0.35, -0.15, Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 148), PAPER)

	-- Tail ribbons.
	wedge(model, "Tail", Vector3.new(0.14, 1.1, 1.8), 0, 0.6, 2.6, a, PAPER)
	block(model, "Glow", Vector3.new(0.6, 0.2, 0.2), 0, 0.05, 2.9, a, NEON)

	model.PrimaryPart = body
	return model, aileronL, aileronR
end

builders.Bomber = function(config)
	local model = Instance.new("Model")
	model.Name = "Plane_Bomber"
	local c, a = config.Color, config.Accent

	-- Boxy cardboard fuselage with visible corrugation strips.
	local body = block(model, "Body", Vector3.new(2.6, 2.0, 6.4), 0, 0, 0, c, PAPER)
	block(model, "Nose", Vector3.new(2.2, 1.6, 1.6), 0, -0.1, -3.6, a, PAPER)
	for i = -1, 1 do
		block(model, "Rib" .. i, Vector3.new(2.7, 0.12, 0.3), 0, 1.02, i * 1.8, Color3.fromRGB(110, 78, 50), PAPER)
	end
	block(model, "Belly", Vector3.new(2.0, 0.6, 4.6), 0, -1.05, 0.2, Color3.fromRGB(120, 85, 55), PAPER)

	local aileronL = wing(model, -1, { color = c, accent = a, span = 3.6, chord = 3.2, sweep = 4, dihedral = 2, thickness = 0.3, rootX = 1.4 })
	local aileronR = wing(model, 1, { color = c, accent = a, span = 3.6, chord = 3.2, sweep = 4, dihedral = 2, thickness = 0.3, rootX = 1.4 })

	-- Twin engine nacelles with props.
	for i = -1, 1, 2 do
		cylinder(model, "Nacelle", Vector3.new(2.2, 0.95, 0.95), i * 2.6, 0.05, 0.4, a, FOIL, 0, 90, 0)
		propeller(model, i * 2.6, 0.05, -0.8, Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 66))
	end

	-- Tail: big vertical fin plus stabilizers.
	wedge(model, "TailFin", Vector3.new(0.2, 2.1, 1.9), 0, 1.1, 2.9, c, PAPER)
	block(model, "TailFinTrim", Vector3.new(0.24, 0.3, 1.7), 0, 2.0, 3.0, a, NEON)
	block(model, "Stabilizer", Vector3.new(4.2, 0.18, 1.3), 0, 0.6, 3.0, c, PAPER)

	-- Cockpit glass and gun turret.
	local canopy = ellipsoid(model, "Canopy", Vector3.new(1.7, 0.95, 1.7), 0, 1.15, -1.7, Color3.fromRGB(170, 220, 255), GLASS)
	canopy.Transparency = 0.4
	ball(model, "Turret", 0.8, 0, -1.35, -1.4, a, FOIL)
	block(model, "Glow", Vector3.new(0.8, 0.3, 0.3), 0, 0, -4.45, a, NEON)

	model.PrimaryPart = body
	return model, aileronL, aileronR
end

builders.OrigamiCrane = function(config)
	local model = Instance.new("Model")
	model.Name = "Plane_OrigamiCrane"
	local c, a = config.Color, config.Accent

	-- Long folded neck and swept crane wings.
	local body = wedge(model, "Body", Vector3.new(1.2, 0.9, 3.6), 0, 0, 0.4, c, PAPER)
	block(model, "Neck", Vector3.new(0.42, 2.0, 0.42), 0, 0.9, -2.2, c, PAPER, 14, 0, 0)
	block(model, "NeckTop", Vector3.new(0.38, 0.9, 0.38), 0, 1.9, -2.5, c, PAPER, 8, 0, 0)
	ellipsoid(model, "Head", Vector3.new(0.65, 0.6, 1.0), 0, 2.35, -2.8, a, PAPER)
	wedge(model, "Beak", Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.9), 0, 2.3, -3.4, Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 90), PAPER)
	ball(model, "Eye", 0.18, -0.2, 2.45, -2.95, Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 40), NEON)
	ball(model, "Eye2", 0.18, 0.2, 2.45, -2.95, Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 40), NEON)

	local aileronL = wing(model, -1, { color = c, accent = a, span = 3.8, chord = 3.0, sweep = 18, dihedral = 8, rootX = 0.8, z = 0.6 })
	local aileronR = wing(model, 1, { color = c, accent = a, span = 3.8, chord = 3.0, sweep = 18, dihedral = 8, rootX = 0.8, z = 0.6 })

	-- Fan tail.
	wedge(model, "TailFanL", Vector3.new(1.4, 0.1, 1.6), -0.8, 0.3, 2.6, a, PAPER, 0, -20, 0)
	wedge(model, "TailFanR", Vector3.new(1.4, 0.1, 1.6), 0.8, 0.3, 2.6, a, PAPER, 0, 20, 0)
	block(model, "Glow", Vector3.new(0.5, 0.25, 0.5), 0, 0.1, 2.9, a, NEON)

	model.PrimaryPart = body
	return model, aileronL, aileronR
end

builders.Jetstream = function(config)
	local model = Instance.new("Model")
	model.Name = "Plane_Jetstream"
	local c, a = config.Color, config.Accent

	-- Sleek swept delta; the only plane with an obvious jet intake and nozzle.
	local body = wedge(model, "Body", Vector3.new(1.7, 0.9, 6.2), 0, 0, 0, c, PAPER)
	wedge(model, "Spine", Vector3.new(1.0, 0.5, 4.4), 0, 0.6, 0.4, c, PAPER)
	wedge(model, "Nose", Vector3.new(0.6, 0.6, 2.4), 0, 0, -3.9, a, FOIL)
	block(model, "Intake", Vector3.new(1.1, 0.5, 0.6), 0, -0.5, -2.6, Color3.fromRGB(40, 44, 52), FOIL)

	local aileronL = wing(model, -1, { color = c, accent = a, span = 3.4, chord = 3.0, sweep = 26, dihedral = 3, rootX = 1.0 })
	local aileronR = wing(model, 1, { color = c, accent = a, span = 3.4, chord = 3.0, sweep = 26, dihedral = 3, rootX = 1.0 })

	-- Twin canted tails.
	wedge(model, "TailL", Vector3.new(0.14, 1.5, 1.5), -0.7, 0.9, 2.7, c, PAPER, 0, 0, 18)
	wedge(model, "TailR", Vector3.new(0.14, 1.5, 1.5), 0.7, 0.9, 2.7, c, PAPER, 0, 0, -18)
	block(model, "TailTrim", Vector3.new(1.7, 0.18, 0.2), 0, 1.55, 2.8, a, NEON)

	-- Canopy.
	local canopy = ellipsoid(model, "Canopy", Vector3.new(1.0, 0.68, 2.1), 0, 0.62, -1.2, Color3.fromRGB(120, 210, 255), GLASS)
	canopy.Transparency = 0.25

	-- Nozzle + afterburner glow.
	cylinder(model, "Nozzle", Vector3.new(0.5, 0.95, 0.95), 0, 0, 3.15, Color3.fromRGB(50, 52, 60), FOIL, 0, 90, 0)
	local glow = ball(model, "Glow", 0.75, 0, 0, 3.55, a, NEON)
	glow.Transparency = 0.15
	block(model, "Glow2", Vector3.new(0.45, 0.45, 1.2), 0, 0, 4.1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), NEON)

	model.PrimaryPart = body
	return model, aileronL, aileronR
end

builders.GoldenFold = function(config)
	local model = Instance.new("Model")
	model.Name = "Plane_GoldenFold"
	local c, a = config.Color, config.Accent

	-- Layered golden folds: three stacked wing layers, each slightly offset.
	local body = wedge(model, "Body", Vector3.new(1.8, 1.0, 5.8), 0, 0, 0, c, FOIL)
	wedge(model, "BodyTop", Vector3.new(1.4, 0.7, 4.6), 0, 0.5, 0, a, FOIL)

	local aileronL = wing(model, -1, { color = c, accent = a, span = 3.6, chord = 3.8, sweep = 14, dihedral = 6 })
	local aileronR = wing(model, 1, { color = c, accent = a, span = 3.6, chord = 3.8, sweep = 14, dihedral = 6 })

	-- Second and third wing layers for a feathered look.
	for layer = 1, 2 do
		local y = 0.18 + layer * 0.22
		local spread = 0.55 * layer
		wedge(model, "LayerL" .. layer, Vector3.new(2.4, 0.1, 2.6), -2.4 - spread, y, 0.9, a, FOIL, 0, 10, -4)
		wedge(model, "LayerR" .. layer, Vector3.new(2.4, 0.1, 2.6), 2.4 + spread, y, 0.9, a, FOIL, 0, -10, 4)
	end

	-- Crown tail.
	wedge(model, "TailFin", Vector3.new(0.18, 2.0, 1.9), 0, 1.1, 2.6, c, FOIL)
	block(model, "Crown", Vector3.new(1.1, 0.35, 0.35), 0, 2.05, 2.7, a, NEON)
	block(model, "CrownL", Vector3.new(0.3, 0.7, 0.3), -0.5, 2.2, 2.7, a, NEON, 0, 0, 22)
	block(model, "CrownR", Vector3.new(0.3, 0.7, 0.3), 0.5, 2.2, 2.7, a, NEON, 0, 0, -22)

	local canopy = ellipsoid(model, "Canopy", Vector3.new(1.05, 0.68, 1.6), 0, 0.75, -0.7, Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 190), GLASS)
	canopy.Transparency = 0.3

	block(model, "Glow", Vector3.new(1.0, 0.4, 0.9), 0, 0, 2.95, a, NEON)
	block(model, "Glow2", Vector3.new(0.4, 0.4, 2.0), 0, 0, -3.2, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), NEON)

	model.PrimaryPart = body
	return model, aileronL, aileronR
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------- finish details ---
-- Consistent small-scale aircraft details make each procedural silhouette read
-- as a finished vehicle rather than a stack of plain wedges. These details are
-- intentionally non-colliding and remain part of the model pivot.
local function addAircraftFinish(model: Model, config, planeId: string)
	local accent = config.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(220, 230, 245)
	local darkMetal = Color3.fromRGB(48, 54, 66)
	local lightMetal = Color3.fromRGB(176, 188, 204)

	-- Fold/paint bands on both wings and small insignia discs.
	for side = -1, 1, 2 do
		local stripe = block(model, "WingFinishStripe", Vector3.new(0.34, 0.075, 2.15), side * 3.35, 0.22, 0.05, accent, FOIL, 0, side * 8, side * 2)
		stripe.Transparency = 0.04
		local insignia = ellipsoid(model, "WingInsignia", Vector3.new(0.72, 0.12, 0.72), side * 4.05, 0.24, -0.45, accent, FOIL)
		insignia.Transparency = 0.08
		block(model, "WingTipMark", Vector3.new(0.42, 0.12, 0.62), side * 5.0, 0.25, 0.65, Color3.fromRGB(248, 246, 232), PAPER)
	end

	-- Reinforced belly rails and two runway wheels make the aircraft feel grounded.
	for side = -1, 1, 2 do
		block(model, "LandingStrut", Vector3.new(0.16, 0.9, 0.16), side * 0.82, -0.78, 0.78, lightMetal, FOIL, 0, 0, side * -8)
		local wheel = cylinder(model, "LandingWheel", Vector3.new(0.42, 0.72, 0.72), side * 0.82, -1.18, 0.78, darkMetal, FOIL)
		wheel.CanCollide = false
		cylinder(model, "WheelHub", Vector3.new(0.44, 0.24, 0.24), side * 0.82, -1.18, 0.78, lightMetal, FOIL)
	end

	-- Small navigation lamps and panel seams, color-coded by wing side.
	block(model, "NavigationLightL", Vector3.new(0.3, 0.24, 0.42), -5.05, 0.22, 0.65, Color3.fromRGB(255, 78, 78), NEON)
	block(model, "NavigationLightR", Vector3.new(0.3, 0.24, 0.42), 5.05, 0.22, 0.65, Color3.fromRGB(90, 220, 255), NEON)
	block(model, "RearPanelSeam", Vector3.new(0.12, 0.06, 1.2), 0, 0.5, 1.65, accent, FOIL)
	block(model, "NosePanelSeam", Vector3.new(0.08, 0.07, 1.1), 0, 0.48, -2.4, accent, FOIL)

	-- A second tail marker provides a readable silhouette from behind.
	local tail = model:FindFirstChild("TailFin", true)
	if tail and tail:IsA("BasePart") then
		block(model, "TailMark", Vector3.new(0.22, 0.35, 1.1), 0, 1.15, 2.7, accent, FOIL)
	end

	model:SetAttribute("FinishProfile", "PaperWings_Refined_01")
	model:SetAttribute("PlaneId", planeId)
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- public ---
function PlaneBuilder.BuildPlane(planeId: string): Model
	local config = GameConfig.Planes[planeId] or GameConfig.Planes.Classic
	local build = builders[planeId] or builders.Classic
	local model, aileronL, aileronR = build(config)
	model.Name = "Plane_" .. planeId
	addAircraftFinish(model, config, planeId)

	-- Flight animates these; missing ones simply never move.
	if aileronL then
		aileronL:SetAttribute("BaseAngle", 0)
	end
	if aileronR then
		aileronR:SetAttribute("BaseAngle", 0)
	end
	model:SetAttribute("PlaneId", planeId)
	return model
end

allyBuilders.InkSpirit = function(config)

			model,
			"Drop" .. i,
			0.5,
			math.cos(angle) * 1.0,
			math.sin(angle * 1.3) * 0.5,
			math.sin(angle) * 1.0,
			config.Accent,
			NEON
		)

	end
	ball(model, "EyeL", 0.36, -0.4, 0.25, -0.72, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), NEON)
	ball(model, "EyeR", 0.36, 0.4, 0.25, -0.72, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), NEON)
	model.PrimaryPart = body
	return model
end

function PlaneBuilder.BuildAlly(allyId: string): Model
	local config = GameConfig.Allies[allyId]
	local build = allyBuilders[allyId]
	if not config or not build then
		return nil :: any
	end
	local model = build(config)
	model.Name = "Ally_" .. allyId
	return model
end

return PlaneBuilder

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/client/State.luau
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
--!nonstrict
-- Client-side player state for Paper Wings.
--
-- The server always sends a complete snapshot, but updates are merged over the
-- previous table anyway: a partial payload must never be able to blank out
-- collection data (that bug wiped UnlockedPlanes and reset the shop).

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")
local Stats = require(Shared:WaitForChild("Stats"))

local State = {
	Data = nil,
	Stats = nil,
}

local updateEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
local loadedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
local runUpdateEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
local runEndedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
local notifyEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")

function State.OnUpdate(callback)
	return updateEvent.Event:Connect(callback)
end

function State.OnLoaded(callback)
	-- Data can arrive before a late subscriber (Flight/UI init after the first
	-- DataLoaded), so replay the loaded event for anyone who asks afterwards.
	if State.Data then
		task.defer(callback, State.Data, State.Stats)
	end
	return loadedEvent.Event:Connect(callback)
end

function State.OnRunUpdate(callback)
	return runUpdateEvent.Event:Connect(callback)
end

function State.OnRunEnded(callback)
	return runEndedEvent.Event:Connect(callback)
end

function State.OnNotify(callback)
	return notifyEvent.Event:Connect(callback)
end

function State.EmitRunUpdate(...)
	runUpdateEvent:Fire(...)
end

function State.EmitRunEnded(result)
	runEndedEvent:Fire(result)
end

function State.EmitNotify(text)
	notifyEvent:Fire(text)
end

local function merge(base, incoming)
	local merged = table.clone(base or {})
	for key, value in pairs(incoming) do
		merged[key] = value
	end
	return merged
end

local function apply(data, isFull: boolean)
	if type(data) ~= "table" then
		return
	end
	State.Data = merge(State.Data, data)
	State.Stats = Stats.Compute(State.Data)
	if isFull then
		loadedEvent:Fire(State.Data, State.Stats)
	else
		updateEvent:Fire(State.Data, State.Stats)
	end
end

function State.ApplyUpdate(data)
	apply(data, false)
end

function State.ApplyLoaded(data)
	apply(data, true)
end

-- Convenience accessors used by the UI.
function State.Coins(): number
	return (State.Data and State.Data.Coins) or 0
end

function State.Gems(): number
	return (State.Data and State.Data.Gems) or 0
end

function State.Level(): number
	return (State.Stats and State.Stats.Level) or 1
end

return State

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/client/UI.luau
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- ============================================================================
--!nonstrict
-- PaperWings UI: hangar, HUD, shop, quests, daily rewards, codes and results.
--
-- One ScreenGui, five layers:
--   menu    the hangar overlay (before a run)
--   hud     in-flight readouts
--   shop    tabbed store (upgrades, planes, weapons, pets, shields, eggs, passes)
--   results end-of-run summary
--   modal   quests / codes / pass details, with a shared backdrop
--
-- Everything is built from code so the place has no UI assets to keep in sync.

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local playerGui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")
local GameConfig = require(Shared:WaitForChild("GameConfig"))
local Monetization = require(Shared:WaitForChild("Monetization"))
local Quests = require(Shared:WaitForChild("Quests"))
local Remotes = require(Shared:WaitForChild("Remotes"))
local Utility = require(Shared:WaitForChild("Utility"))

local State = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("State"))
local Flight = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("Flight"))
local Effects = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("Effects"))

local UI = {}

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- palette ---
local C = {
	Ink = Color3.fromRGB(30, 34, 46),
	InkSoft = Color3.fromRGB(74, 82, 100),
	Paper = Color3.fromRGB(250, 247, 238),
	PaperDim = Color3.fromRGB(228, 225, 214),
	Sun = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 74),
	Sky = Color3.fromRGB(72, 158, 222),
	Mint = Color3.fromRGB(88, 202, 132),
	Coral = Color3.fromRGB(240, 96, 96),
	Grape = Color3.fromRGB(170, 110, 226),
	White = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
	Muted = Color3.fromRGB(128, 136, 150),
}

local FONT = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
local FONT_BOLD = Enum.Font.GothamBold
local FONT_REG = Enum.Font.Gotham

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------ state ---
local gui: ScreenGui
local scaler: UIScale
local menu: Frame
local hud: Frame
local shopFrame: Frame
local resultsFrame: Frame
local modal: Frame
local modalTitle: TextLabel
local modalBody: ScrollingFrame

local shopContent: ScrollingFrame
local shopTabs: { [string]: TextButton } = {}
local currentTab = "Upgrades"
local shopOpen = false
local modalOpen = false

local hudRefs = {}
local menuRefs = {}
local resultsRefs = {}
local toastBox: Frame
local toastLabel: TextLabel
local toastToken = 0

local lastZoneName: string? = nil
local lastMilestone = -1
local lastComboShown = 0
local lastServerResult: any = nil
local bossShown = false
local leaderboardData: any = nil
local leaderboardOpen = false
local lastHealth = 0
local lastStreakTier = 0
local bindingCaptureAction: string? = nil
local bindingButtons: { [string]: TextButton } = {}

local STREAKS = {
	[2] = "DOUBLE KILL",
	[3] = "TRIPLE KILL",
	[5] = "RAMPAGE",
	[8] = "UNSTOPPABLE",
	[12] = "PAPER LEGEND",
}

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- helpers ---
local function new(className: string, props, parent: Instance?): any
	local obj = Instance.new(className)
	for key, value in pairs(props or {}) do
		obj[key] = value
	end
	if parent then
		obj.Parent = parent
	end
	return obj
end

local function corner(obj: Instance, radius: number?)
	new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius or 12) }, obj)
end

local function outline(obj: Instance, color: Color3?, thickness: number?, transparency: number?)
	new("UIStroke", {
		Color = color or C.Ink,
		Thickness = thickness or 2,
		ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
		Transparency = transparency or 0,
	}, obj)
end

local function gradient(obj: Instance, top: Color3, bottom: Color3, rotation: number?)
	new("UIGradient", {
		Color = ColorSequence.new(top, bottom),
		Rotation = rotation or 90,
	}, obj)
end

local function text(parent: Instance, props): TextLabel
	local base = {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Font = FONT_BOLD,
		TextColor3 = C.White,
		TextSize = 16,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
	}
	for key, value in pairs(props) do
		base[key] = value
	end
	return new("TextLabel", base, parent)
end

local function button(parent: Instance, props, onClick): TextButton
	local base = {
		Font = FONT,
		TextSize = 18,
		TextColor3 = C.White,
		BackgroundColor3 = C.Sky,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
	}
	for key, value in pairs(props) do
		base[key] = value
	end
	local b = new("TextButton", base, parent)
	corner(b, 12)
	outline(b, C.Ink, 2)
	local scale = new("UIScale", { Scale = 1 }, b)
	local function tweenScale(value: number)
		TweenService:Create(scale, TweenInfo.new(0.09, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Scale = value }):Play()
	end

	local baseColor = b.BackgroundColor3
	b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		if b:GetAttribute("Disabled") then
			return
		end
		tweenScale(1.035)
		TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.1), { BackgroundColor3 = baseColor:Lerp(C.White, 0.16) }):Play()
	end)
	b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		tweenScale(1)
		TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.1), { BackgroundColor3 = baseColor }):Play()
	end)
	b.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
		if b:GetAttribute("Disabled") then
			return
		end
		tweenScale(0.95)
	end)
	b.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
		tweenScale(if b:GetAttribute("Disabled") then 1 else 1.035)
	end)
	b.Activated:Connect(function()
		if b:GetAttribute("Disabled") then
			return
		end
		Effects.Play("Click", nil, 0.25)
		onClick()
	end)
	return b
end

local function setDisabled(b: TextButton, disabled: boolean, tint: Color3?)
	b:SetAttribute("Disabled", disabled)
	b.BackgroundColor3 = if disabled then C.PaperDim else (tint or C.Sky)
	b.TextColor3 = if disabled then C.Muted else C.White
end

local function card(parent: Instance, props)
	local base = {
		BackgroundColor3 = C.Paper,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
	}
	for key, value in pairs(props) do
		base[key] = value
	end
	local f = new("Frame", base, parent)
	corner(f, 18)
	outline(f, C.Ink, 3)
	return f
end

-- Thin rounded bar; returns the fill frame so callers can size it.
local function bar(parent: Instance, props)
	local track = new("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = C.Ink,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
	}, parent)
	for key, value in pairs(props or {}) do
		track[key] = value
	end
	corner(track, 8)
	outline(track, C.White, 1, 0.6)
	local fill = new("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = C.Mint,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
	}, track)
	corner(fill, 7)
	return track, fill
end

local function currencyIcon(parent: Instance, name: string, y: number, color: Color3, diamond: boolean?)
	local icon = new("Frame", {
		Name = name,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, y),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14),
		BackgroundColor3 = color,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Rotation = if diamond then 45 else 0,
	}, parent)
	corner(icon, if diamond then 3 else 7)
	outline(icon, C.Ink, 1.5, 0.15)
	return icon
end

local function fmt(n): string
	return Utility.FormatNumber(tonumber(n) or 0)
end

local function toast(message: string)
	toastToken += 1
	local token = toastToken
	toastLabel.Text = tostring(message)
	toastBox.Visible = true
	toastBox.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
	TweenService:Create(toastBox, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 24) }):Play()
	task.delay(2.6, function()
		if token ~= toastToken then
			return
		end
		TweenService:Create(toastBox, TweenInfo.new(0.25), { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 8) }):Play()
		task.delay(0.3, function()
			if token == toastToken then
				toastBox.Visible = false
			end
		end)
	end)
end

UI.Toast = toast

-- --------------------------------------------------------------- screens ---
local function buildHud()

	})
	hudRefs.zone = text(score, {
		Name = "Zone",
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 50),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26),
		Text = "PAPER TOWN",
		Font = FONT,
		TextSize = 14,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
		TextColor3 = C.Sun,
	})

	-- Wallet, top right.
	local wallet = card(hud, {

			end)

			end

		boostButton.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then

local function showBanner(message: string)
	local label = hudRefs.banner
	if not label then
		return
	end
	label.Text = message
	label.TextTransparency = 0
	label.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.3, 0)
	TweenService:Create(label, TweenInfo.new(0.9, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.26, 0),
	}):Play()
	task.delay(1.3, function()
		if label.Text == message then
			TweenService:Create(label, TweenInfo.new(0.5), { TextTransparency = 1 }):Play()
		end
	end)
end

local function buildMenu()
	-- Fully transparent: the hangar itself is the menu backdrop.
	menu = new("Frame", {

		Size = UDim2.new(0, 520, 0, 24),
		Text = "FLY FARTHER.  HIT HARDER.  COME BACK STRONGER.",
		Font = FONT,

		TextColor3 = C.Sun,

		TextStrokeColor3 = C.Ink,
	})

	menuRefs.hangar = text(menu, {
		Name = "HangarName",
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 42, 0, 252),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 620, 0, 24),

		TextStrokeTransparency = 0.45,
		TextStrokeColor3 = C.Ink,
	})
	menuRefs.stats = text(menu, {
		Name = "HomeStats",
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 42, 0, 278),

		Font = FONT_REG,
		TextSize = 13,
		TextColor3 = C.White,
		TextTransparency = 0.12,
		TextStrokeTransparency = 0.7,
		TextStrokeColor3 = C.Ink,
	})

	-- Slow paper confetti, behind every control.
	local decor = new("Frame", {
		Name = "PaperBits",
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		ZIndex = 0,
	}, menu)
	for index = 1, 14 do
		local bit = new("Frame", {
			Name = "Bit" .. index,
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(6 + (index % 4) * 3, 12 + (index % 3) * 5),
			Position = UDim2.fromScale((index * 0.071) % 1, math.random() * 90 / 100),
			Rotation = math.random(-40, 40),
			BackgroundColor3 = ({ C.Sun, C.Mint, C.Sky, C.Coral, C.Grape })[(index % 5) + 1],
			BackgroundTransparency = 0.45,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			ZIndex = 0,
		}, decor)
		corner(bit, 2)
		task.spawn(function()
			while bit.Parent do
				bit.Rotation = math.random(-40, 40)
				local tween = TweenService:Create(bit, TweenInfo.new(6 + (index % 5) * 1.7, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {
					Position = UDim2.fromScale(bit.Position.X.Scale, 1.1),
					Rotation = bit.Rotation + 220,
				})
				tween:Play()
				tween.Completed:Wait()
				bit.Position = UDim2.fromScale((index * 0.071) % 1, -0.12)
				task.wait(0.2)
			end
		end)
	end

	-- Live-ops event badge (weekend rush, midweek gems, ...).
	menuRefs.event = text(menu, {
		Name = "Event",
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 42, 0, 222),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 560, 0, 26),

	-- Stat plates, top right.
	local levelPlate = card(menu, {

		BackgroundColor3 = C.Ink,
	})
	menuRefs.level = text(levelPlate, {
		Name = "Level",
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),

	menuRefs.gems = text(wallet, {

	})
	currencyIcon(wallet, "GemsIcon", 45, C.Sky, true)

	-- Personal best strip.
	menuRefs.best = text(menu, {
		Name = "Best",
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1),
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -168),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 520, 0, 28),
		Text = "PERSONAL BEST  0 m",
		Font = FONT,
		TextSize = 17,

	-- Primary actions.
	local actions = new("Frame", {

	menuRefs.actions = actions

		Size = UDim2.new(0, 268, 1, 0),
		Text = "FLY",
		TextSize = 30,
		BackgroundColor3 = C.Mint,
	}, function()
    print("[PaperWings] FLY BUTTON CLICKED")
    print("[PaperWings] Data loaded:", State.Data ~= nil)
    print("[PaperWings] Stats loaded:", State.Stats ~= nil)

    local ok, err = xpcall(function()
        Flight.StartRun()
    end, debug.traceback)

    if not ok then
        warn("[PaperWings] STARTUP ERROR:\n" .. tostring(err))
    end

    print("[PaperWings] IsRunning:", Flight.IsRunning())
end)
	outline(fly, C.Ink, 3)
	local shop = button(actions, {
		Name = "Shop",
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -268, 0, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 268, 1, 0),
		Text = "SHOP",
		TextSize = 30,
		BackgroundColor3 = C.Sky,
	}, function()
		UI.OpenShop()
	end)

	-- Secondary actions.
	local secondary = new("Frame", {
		Name = "Secondary",
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1),
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -26),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 796, 0, 48),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
	}, menu)
	local secondaryDefs = {
		{ Name = "Quests", Label = "QUESTS", Color = C.Grape, Action = function()
			UI.OpenQuests()
		end },
		{ Name = "Daily", Label = "DAILY", Color = C.Sun, Action = function()
			Remotes.Get(Remotes.ClaimDaily):FireServer()
		end },
		{ Name = "Rankings", Label = "RANKINGS", Color = C.Sky, Action = function()
			UI.OpenLeaderboard()
		end },
		{ Name = "Codes", Label = "CODES", Color = C.InkSoft, Action = function()
			UI.OpenCodes()
		end },
		{ Name = "Rebirth", Label = "REBIRTH", Color = C.Coral, Action = function()
			Remotes.Get(Remotes.Rebirth):FireServer()
		end },
		{ Name = "Settings", Label = "SETTINGS", Color = C.InkSoft, Action = function()
			UI.OpenSettings()
		end },
	}
	for index, def in ipairs(secondaryDefs) do
		local b = button(secondary, {
			Name = def.Name,
			Position = UDim2.new(0, (index - 1) * 132, 0, 0),
			Size = UDim2.new(0, 126, 0, 46),
			Text = def.Label,
			TextSize = 13,
			BackgroundColor3 = def.Color,
		}, def.Action)
		menuRefs[def.Name] = b
	end

	text(menu, {
		Name = "Controls",
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 40, 1, -26),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 560, 0, 22),
		Text = "WASD MOVE  •  Q/E ALTITUDE  •  MOUSE AIM + HOLD TO FIRE  •  SHIFT BOOST",
		Font = FONT,
		TextSize = 12,
		TextColor3 = C.White,
		TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5,
		TextStrokeColor3 = C.Ink,
	})
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------ shop ---
local renderers = {}

local function addRow(titleText: string, descText: string, actionText: string, color: Color3, enabled: boolean, onClick)
	local row = card(shopContent, {
		Name = titleText,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 84),
		BackgroundColor3 = C.Paper,
	})
	local stripe = new("Frame", {
		Name = "Stripe",
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 8, 1, 0),
		BackgroundColor3 = color,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
	}, row)
	corner(stripe, 6)
	text(row, {
		Name = "Title",
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 8),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -210, 0, 28),
		Text = titleText,
		Font = FONT,
		TextSize = 18,
		TextColor3 = C.Ink,
	})
	text(row, {
		Name = "Desc",
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 38),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -210, 0, 38),
		Text = descText,
		Font = FONT_REG,
		TextSize = 13,
		TextColor3 = C.InkSoft,
		TextWrapped = true,
		TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
	})
	local action = button(row, {
		Name = "Action",
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 178, 0, 46),
		Text = actionText,
		TextSize = 14,
		BackgroundColor3 = color,
	}, function()
		if not enabled then
			return
		end
		onClick()
	end)
	if not enabled then
		setDisabled(action, true)
	end
	return row, action
end

renderers.Upgrades = function()
	for _, kind in ipairs(GameConfig.UpgradeOrder) do
		local cfg = GameConfig.Upgrades[kind]
		local level = (State.Data.Upgrades[kind]) or 1
		local cost = GameConfig.UpgradeCost(kind, level)
		local canAfford = State.Coins() >= cost
		local maxLevel = if cfg.Max == math.huge then math.huge else math.floor(cfg.Max / cfg.PerLevel) + 1
		local actionText = if level >= maxLevel then "MAXED" else "UPGRADE  " .. fmt(cost)
		addRow(
			string.format("%s  •  LV %d", string.upper(cfg.Name), level),
			string.format("%s  (+%d%% per level)", cfg.Desc, math.floor(cfg.PerLevel * 100)),
			actionText,
			if level >= maxLevel then C.Muted else (canAfford and C.Mint or C.Coral),
			level < maxLevel and canAfford,
			function()
				Remotes.Get(Remotes.Purchase):FireServer({ Action = "upgrade", Id = kind })
			end
		)
	end
end

renderers.Planes = function()
	local ids = {}
	for id in pairs(GameConfig.Planes) do
		table.insert(ids, id)
	end
	table.sort(ids)
	for _, id in ipairs(ids) do
		local plane = GameConfig.Planes[id]
		local unlocked = State.Data.UnlockedPlanes[id]
		local equipped = State.Data.EquippedPlane == id
		local levelOk = State.Level() >= (plane.UnlockLevel or 0)
		local actionText
		local enabled = true
		if equipped then
			actionText = "EQUIPPED"
			enabled = false
		elseif unlocked then
			actionText = "EQUIP"
		elseif plane.RequiresPass then
			actionText = "GAMEPASS"
			enabled = false
		elseif not levelOk then
			actionText = "LV " .. plane.UnlockLevel
			enabled = false
		else
			actionText = "BUY  " .. plane.Cost .. " GEMS"
			enabled = State.Gems() >= plane.Cost
		end
		addRow(plane.Name, string.format("%s   SPD %.2f • HP %.2f • DMG %.2f", plane.Desc, plane.SpeedMult, plane.HealthMult, plane.DamageMult), actionText, plane.Color, enabled, function()
			Remotes.Get(Remotes.Purchase):FireServer({ Action = "plane", Id = id })
		end)
	end
end

renderers.Weapons = function()
	local ids = {}
	for id in pairs(GameConfig.Weapons) do
		table.insert(ids, id)
	end
	table.sort(ids)
	for _, id in ipairs(ids) do
		local weapon = GameConfig.Weapons[id]
		local unlocked = State.Data.UnlockedWeapons[id]
		local equipped = State.Data.EquippedWeapon == id
		local actionText
		local enabled = true
		if equipped then
			actionText = "EQUIPPED"
			enabled = false
		elseif unlocked then
			actionText = "EQUIP"
		elseif weapon.RequiresPass then
			actionText = "GAMEPASS"
			enabled = false
		else
			actionText = "BUY  " .. weapon.Cost .. " GEMS"
			enabled = State.Gems() >= weapon.Cost
		end
		local shots = math.max(1, math.floor(weapon.Projectiles or 1))
		local shotText = if shots == 1 then "1 SHOT" else (shots .. " SHOTS")
		addRow(weapon.Name, string.format("%s   DMG x%.2f • RATE %.2fs • %s", weapon.Desc, weapon.DamageMult, weapon.FireInterval, shotText), actionText, weapon.Color, enabled, function()
			Remotes.Get(Remotes.Purchase):FireServer({ Action = "weapon", Id = id })
		end)
	end
end

renderers.Pets = function()
	local ids = {}
	for id in pairs(GameConfig.Allies) do
		table.insert(ids, id)
	end
	table.sort(ids)
	local limit = State.Data.AllyLimit or GameConfig.MaxAllies
	for _, id in ipairs(ids) do
		local ally = GameConfig.Allies[id]
		local owned = State.Data.UnlockedAllies[id]
		local equipped = table.find(State.Data.Allies, id) ~= nil
		local actionText
		local enabled = true
		if equipped then
			actionText = "IN SQUAD"
			enabled = false
		elseif owned then
			actionText = "ADD TO SQUAD"
			enabled = #State.Data.Allies < limit
		else
			actionText = "BUY  " .. ally.Cost .. " GEMS"
			enabled = State.Gems() >= ally.Cost and #State.Data.Allies < limit
		end
		addRow(ally.Name, string.format("%s   DMG %d • RATE %.1fs%s", ally.Desc, ally.Damage, ally.FireInterval, ally.Splash and " • SPLASH" or ""), actionText, ally.Color, enabled, function()
			Remotes.Get(Remotes.Purchase):FireServer({ Action = "ally", Id = id })
		end)
	end
	text(shopContent, {
		Name = "SquadInfo",
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 30),
		Text = string.format("SQUAD %d / %d   •   BUY MORE SLOTS WITH THE ALLY SQUAD PASS", #State.Data.Allies, limit),
		Font = FONT,
		TextSize = 12,
		TextColor3 = C.Muted,
	})
end

renderers.Shields = function()
	local ids = {}
	for id in pairs(GameConfig.Shields) do
		table.insert(ids, id)
	end
	table.sort(ids)
	for _, id in ipairs(ids) do
		local shield = GameConfig.Shields[id]
		local unlocked = State.Data.UnlockedShields[id]
		local equipped = State.Data.EquippedShield == id
		local actionText
		local enabled = true
		if equipped then
			actionText = "EQUIPPED"
			enabled = false
		elseif unlocked then
			actionText = "EQUIP"
		else
			actionText = "BUY  " .. shield.Cost .. " GEMS"
			enabled = State.Gems() >= shield.Cost
		end
		local reduction = math.floor((1 - 1 / shield.Mult) * 100 + 0.5)
		local desc = if id == "None"
			then shield.Desc
			else string.format("%s   TAKES %d%% LESS DAMAGE", shield.Desc, reduction)
		addRow(shield.Name, desc, actionText, C.Sky, enabled, function()
			Remotes.Get(Remotes.Purchase):FireServer({ Action = "shield", Id = id })
		end)
	end
end

renderers.Eggs = function()
	local ids = {}
	for id in pairs(GameConfig.Eggs) do
		table.insert(ids, id)
	end
	table.sort(ids)
	for _, id in ipairs(ids) do
		local egg = GameConfig.Eggs[id]
		addRow(egg.Name, "HATCH A RANDOM PET  •  LUCK IMPROVES THE ODDS", "HATCH  " .. egg.Cost .. " GEMS", egg.Color or C.Grape, State.Gems() >= egg.Cost, function()
			Remotes.Get(Remotes.Purchase):FireServer({ Action = "egg", Id = id })
		end)
	end
end

renderers.Hangars = function()
	for _, id in ipairs(GameConfig.HangarOrder) do
		local hangar = GameConfig.Hangars[id]
		local equipped = State.Data.EquippedHangar == id
		addRow(
			hangar.Name,
			string.format("%s  •  FEATURED EGG: %s  •  HOME ZONE: %s", hangar.Tagline, hangar.Egg, hangar.Zone),
			if equipped then "EQUIPPED" else "MAKE HOME",
			hangar.Accent or C.Grape,
			not equipped,
			function()
				Remotes.Get(Remotes.Purchase):FireServer({ Action = "hangar", Id = id })
			end
		)
	end
end

renderers.Prestige = function()
	for _, kind in ipairs(GameConfig.PrestigeOrder) do
		local entry = GameConfig.Prestige[kind]
		local rank = (State.Data.Prestige and State.Data.Prestige[kind]) or 0
		local maxed = rank >= entry.Max
		local cost = GameConfig.PrestigeCost(kind, rank)
		addRow(
			string.format("%s  •  RANK %d/%d", entry.Name, rank, entry.Max),
			entry.Desc .. "  •  PERMANENT (SURVIVES REBIRTH)",
			if maxed then "MAXED" else ("BUY  " .. cost .. " GEMS"),
			C.Grape,
			not maxed and State.Gems() >= cost,
			function()
				Remotes.Get(Remotes.Purchase):FireServer({ Action = "prestige", Id = kind })
			end
		)
	end
end

renderers.Passes = function()
	local owned = State.Data.OwnedPasses or {}
	for _, key in ipairs(Monetization.GamepassOrder) do
		local pass = Monetization.Gamepasses[key]
		local has = owned[key] == true
		addRow(
			pass.Name,
			table.concat(pass.PerkLines, "   •   "),
			if has then "OWNED" else (Monetization.IsConfigured(pass.Id) and ("BUY  " .. pass.Price .. " R$") or "COMING SOON"),
			pass.Color,
			not has and Monetization.IsConfigured(pass.Id),
			function()
				Remotes.Get(Remotes.PromptPass):FireServer(key)
			end
		)
	end

	local header = text(shopContent, {
		Name = "ProductHeader",
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 34),
		Text = "COIN AND GEM PACKS",
		Font = FONT,
		TextSize = 15,
		TextColor3 = C.Ink,
	})
	header.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

	for _, key in ipairs(Monetization.ProductOrder) do
		local product = Monetization.Products[key]
		addRow(
			product.Name,
			product.Description,
			if Monetization.IsConfigured(product.Id) then ("BUY  " .. product.Price .. " R$") else "COMING SOON",
			C.PaperDim,
			Monetization.IsConfigured(product.Id),
			function()
				Remotes.Get(Remotes.PromptProduct):FireServer(key)
			end
		)
	end
end

local function clearShop()
	for _, child in ipairs(shopContent:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("Frame") or child:IsA("TextLabel") then
			child:Destroy()
		end
	end
end

function UI.RefreshShop()
	if not shopOpen or not State.Data then
		return
	end
	clearShop()
	local render = renderers[currentTab]
	if render then
		render()
	end
end

function UI.SelectTab(tab: string)
	currentTab = tab
	for name, tabButton in pairs(shopTabs) do
		local active = name == tab
		tabButton.BackgroundColor3 = if active then C.Sky else C.PaperDim
		tabButton.TextColor3 = if active then C.White else C.InkSoft
	end
	UI.RefreshShop()
end

local function buildShop()
	shopFrame = card(gui, {
		Name = "Shop",
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 900, 0, 540),
		BackgroundColor3 = C.Paper,
		Visible = false,
	})
	shopFrame.ZIndex = 10

	text(shopFrame, {
		Name = "Title",
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 12),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -120, 0, 40),
		Text = "FLIGHT SHOP",
		Font = FONT,
		TextSize = 26,
		TextColor3 = C.Ink,
	})
	text(shopFrame, {
		Name = "Subtitle",
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 50),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0, 24),
		Text = "Spend coins on power, gems on style.",
		Font = FONT_REG,
		TextSize = 12,
		TextColor3 = C.Muted,
	})
	local close = button(shopFrame, {
		Name = "Close",
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, 12),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 40),
		Text = "X",
		TextSize = 18,
		BackgroundColor3 = C.Coral,
	}, function()
		UI.CloseShop()
	end)

	local tabBar = new("Frame", {
		Name = "Tabs",
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 82),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 42),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
	}, shopFrame)
	local tabs = { "Upgrades", "Prestige", "Planes", "Weapons", "Pets", "Shields", "Eggs", "Hangars", "Passes" }
	for index, name in ipairs(tabs) do
		local width = 92
		local b = button(tabBar, {
			Name = name,
			Position = UDim2.new(0, (index - 1) * (width + 4), 0, 0),
			Size = UDim2.new(0, width, 1, 0),
			Text = name,
			TextSize = 12,
			BackgroundColor3 = if name == currentTab then C.Sky else C.PaperDim,
		}, function()
			UI.SelectTab(name)
		end)
		shopTabs[name] = b
	end

	shopContent = new("ScrollingFrame", {
		Name = "Content",
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 132),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 1, -146),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ScrollBarThickness = 6,
		ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.Sky,
		ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y,
		AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
	}, shopFrame)
	new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 8), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, shopContent)
	new("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 4) }, shopContent)
end

-- --------------------------------------------------------------- results ---
local function buildResults()
	resultsFrame = card(gui, {
		Name = "Results",
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),

		BackgroundColor3 = C.Paper,

	})
	resultsFrame.ZIndex = 12

	text(resultsFrame, {
		Name = "Title",
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 14),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44),
		Text = "FLIGHT OVER",
		Font = FONT,
		TextSize = 30,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
		TextColor3 = C.Ink,
	})

	local stats = new("Frame", {
		Name = "Stats",
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 68),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0, 240),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
	}, resultsFrame)
	local rows = {
		{ Key = "Distance", Label = "DISTANCE", Color = C.Sky },
		{ Key = "Kills", Label = "TARGETS", Color = C.Ink },
		{ Key = "Coins", Label = "COINS", Color = C.Sun },
		{ Key = "Gems", Label = "GEMS", Color = C.Grape },
		{ Key = "Combo", Label = "BEST COMBO", Color = C.Mint },

	for index, row in ipairs(rows) do

	end

	local again = button(resultsFrame, {
		Name = "Again",
