		BackgroundColor3 = C.Mint,
	}, function()
		UI.CloseResults()
		Flight.StartRun()
	end)

	local shop = button(resultsFrame, {
		Name = "Shop",
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -225, 1, -78),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 205, 0, 56),
		Text = "UPGRADE",
		TextSize = 20,
		BackgroundColor3 = C.Sky,
	}, function()
		UI.CloseResults()
		menu.Visible = true
		UI.OpenShop()
	end)

	resultsRefs.subtitle = text(resultsFrame, {
		Name = "Subtitle",
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 52),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18),
		Text = "",
		Font = FONT,
		TextSize = 12,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
		TextColor3 = C.Muted,
	})
end

-- ----------------------------------------------------------------- modal ---
local function buildModal()
	modal = new("Frame", {
		Name = "Modal",
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		BackgroundColor3 = C.Ink,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.45,
		Visible = false,
	}, gui)
	modal.ZIndex = 20

	local panel = card(modal, {
		Name = "Panel",
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 560, 0, 470),
		BackgroundColor3 = C.Paper,
	})
	panel.ZIndex = 21

	modalTitle = text(panel, {
		Name = "Title",
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 14),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -90, 0, 40),
		Text = "TITLE",
		Font = FONT,
		TextSize = 24,
		TextColor3 = C.Ink,
	})
	modalTitle.ZIndex = 22

	local close = button(panel, {
		Name = "Close",
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, 14),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 42, 0, 38),
		Text = "X",
		TextSize = 17,
		BackgroundColor3 = C.Coral,
	}, function()
		UI.CloseModal()
	end)
	close.ZIndex = 23

	modalBody = new("ScrollingFrame", {
		Name = "Body",
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 64),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 1, -80),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ScrollBarThickness = 6,
		AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
	}, panel)
	modalBody.ZIndex = 22
	new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 8), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, modalBody)
end

local function openModal(titleText: string, build)
	if not modal then
		return
	end
	modalOpen = true
	modal.Visible = true
	modalTitle.Text = titleText
	for _, child in ipairs(modalBody:GetChildren()) do
		if not child:IsA("UIListLayout") then
			child:Destroy()
		end
	end
	build(modalBody)
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------- hud ---
local function updateHud(hp, maxHp, dist, coins, gems, kills, zoneName, bossFrac, comboSteps, comboMult, boost, revives, boosting)
	if not hud.Visible then
		lastHealth = hp
		lastStreakTier = 0
	end
	hud.Visible = true
	menu.Visible = false
	resultsFrame.Visible = false

	local fraction = if maxHp > 0 then math.clamp(hp / maxHp, 0, 1) else 0
	hudRefs.healthFill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(fraction, 1)
	hudRefs.healthFill.BackgroundColor3 = C.Coral:Lerp(C.Mint, fraction)
	hudRefs.healthText.Text = string.format("%d / %d", math.ceil(hp), math.ceil(maxHp))

	-- Damage flash, and a pulsing vignette when the run is nearly over.
	if lastHealth > 0 and hp < lastHealth - 0.5 then
		hudRefs.flash.BackgroundTransparency = 0.72
		TweenService:Create(hudRefs.flash, TweenInfo.new(0.35), { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
	end
	lastHealth = hp

	if fraction < 0.3 and hp > 0 then
		hudRefs.vignette.Visible = true
		hudRefs.vignetteStroke.Transparency = 0.5 + 0.25 * math.abs(math.sin(os.clock() * 3))
	else
		hudRefs.vignette.Visible = false
	end
	hudRefs.distance.Text = fmt(dist) .. " m"
	hudRefs.zone.Text = string.upper(zoneName or "PAPER TOWN")
	hudRefs.coins.Text = fmt(coins)
	hudRefs.gems.Text = fmt(gems)

	if bossFrac and bossFrac > 0 then
		if not bossShown then
			bossShown = true
			showBanner("BOSS INCOMING")
		end
		hudRefs.bossBar.Visible = true
		hudRefs.bossFill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(math.clamp(bossFrac, 0, 1), 1)
	else
		bossShown = false
		hudRefs.bossBar.Visible = false
	end

	local steps = comboSteps or 0
	if steps >= 2 then
		hudRefs.comboBox.Visible = true
		hudRefs.comboText.Text = string.format("x%.1f", comboMult or 1)
		if steps ~= lastComboShown then
			lastComboShown = steps
			hudRefs.comboBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 142, 0, 66)
			TweenService:Create(hudRefs.comboBox, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Back), { Size = UDim2.new(0, 132, 0, 62) }):Play()
		end
	else
		hudRefs.comboBox.Visible = false
		lastComboShown = 0
		lastStreakTier = 0
	end

	-- Kill-streak callouts ("TRIPLE KILL", "RAMPAGE", ...).
	local streakText = STREAKS[steps]
	if streakText and steps > lastStreakTier then
		lastStreakTier = steps
		local label = hudRefs.streak
		label.Text = streakText
		label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0.5, 52)
		TweenService:Create(label, TweenInfo.new(0.18), { TextTransparency = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0.5, 44) }):Play()
		task.delay(0.9, function()
			if label.Text == streakText then
				TweenService:Create(label, TweenInfo.new(0.3), { TextTransparency = 1 }):Play()
			end
		end)
	end

	local charge = math.clamp(boost or 0, 0, GameConfig.Boost.ChargeMax)
	hudRefs.boostFill.Size = UDim2.new(charge / GameConfig.Boost.ChargeMax, 0, 1, 0)
	hudRefs.boostFill.BackgroundColor3 = if boosting then C.Sun else C.Sky
	hudRefs.boostLabel.Text = if boosting then "BOOSTING" else "BOOST  SHIFT"

	if (revives or 0) > 0 then
		hudRefs.revives.Text = "REVIVES  " .. tostring(revives)
	else
		hudRefs.revives.Text = ""
	end

	if zoneName and zoneName ~= lastZoneName then
		lastZoneName = zoneName
		showBanner(string.upper(zoneName))
	end

	local milestone = math.floor((dist or 0) / 1000)
	if milestone > lastMilestone then
		lastMilestone = milestone
		if milestone > 0 then
			showBanner(fmt(milestone * 1000) .. " m")
		end
	end
end

local function updateResults(result)
	if not result then
		return
	end
	resultsFrame.Visible = true
	menu.Visible = false
	hud.Visible = false
	resultsRefs.Distance.Text = "DISTANCE  " .. fmt(result.Distance) .. " m"
	resultsRefs.Kills.Text = "TARGETS  " .. tostring(result.Kills or 0)
	resultsRefs.Coins.Text = "COINS  " .. fmt(result.Coins) .. (lastServerResult and "  (saved)" or "")
	resultsRefs.Gems.Text = "GEMS  " .. fmt(result.Gems)
	resultsRefs.Combo.Text = "BEST COMBO  x" .. tostring(result.BestCombo or 0)
	if result.Boss then
		resultsRefs.subtitle.Text = "BOSS DEFEATED  •  " .. tostring(result.Boss.Name)
	else
		resultsRefs.subtitle.Text = if result.Reason == "death" then "SHOT DOWN  •  TRY AGAIN" else "RUN COMPLETE"
	end
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- refresh ---
function UI.RefreshTop()
	local data = State.Data
	if not data or not menuRefs.level then
		return
	end
	menuRefs.coins.Text = fmt(data.Coins)
	menuRefs.gems.Text = fmt(data.Gems)
	local streak = tonumber(data.DailyStreak) or 0
	menuRefs.level.Text = string.format(
		"LEVEL %d   •   FLIGHTS %d%s",
		State.Level(),
		data.Runs or 0,
		if streak > 0 then string.format("   •   STREAK %d", streak) else ""
	)
	menuRefs.best.Text = "PERSONAL BEST  " .. fmt(data.BestDistance) .. " m"
	if menuRefs.tutorial then
		menuRefs.tutorial.Visible = (tonumber(data.Runs) or 0) == 0
	end
	local hangar = GameConfig.Hangars[data.EquippedHangar or "Cardboard"] or GameConfig.Hangars.Cardboard
	if menuRefs.hangar then
		menuRefs.hangar.Text = string.format("%s  •  %s", hangar.Name, string.upper(hangar.Egg))
		menuRefs.hangar.TextColor3 = hangar.Accent
	end
	if menuRefs.stats then
		local petCount = 0
		for _ in pairs(data.UnlockedAllies or {}) do
			petCount += 1
		end
		local bossCount = 0
		for _ in pairs(data.BossesBeaten or {}) do
			bossCount += 1
		end
		menuRefs.stats.Text = string.format("PETS %d  •  BOSSES %d  •  FLIGHTS %d", petCount, bossCount, tonumber(data.Runs) or 0)
	end
	if menuRefs.event then
		local event = data.Event
		if event and event.Name then
			menuRefs.event.Text = "EVENT  •  " .. tostring(event.Name) .. "  —  " .. tostring(event.Desc or "")
			menuRefs.event.Visible = true
		else
			menuRefs.event.Visible = false
		end
	end
	if menuRefs.Rebirth then
		menuRefs.Rebirth.Visible = data.CanRebirth == true
	end
end

function UI.OpenShop()
	if not State.Data then
		return
	end
	shopOpen = true
	shopFrame.Visible = true
	menu.Visible = true
	if menuRefs.actions then
		menuRefs.actions.Visible = false
	end
	UI.SelectTab(currentTab)
end

function UI.CloseShop()
	shopOpen = false
	if shopFrame then
		shopFrame.Visible = false
	end
	if menuRefs.actions then
		menuRefs.actions.Visible = true
	end
end

function UI.OpenQuests()
	openModal("DAILY QUESTS", function(parent)
		local data = State.Data
		if not data or not data.Quests then
			return
		end
		for index, entry in ipairs(data.Quests.List) do
			local template = nil
			for _, candidate in ipairs(Quests.Templates) do
				if candidate.Id == entry.Id then
					template = candidate
					break
				end
			end
			if template then
				local row = card(parent, {
					Name = template.Id,
					Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 92),
					BackgroundColor3 = C.PaperDim,
				})
				text(row, {
					Name = "Text",
					Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 8),
					Size = UDim2.new(1, -160, 0, 26),
					Text = Quests.QuestText(template),
					Font = FONT,
					TextSize = 16,
					TextColor3 = C.Ink,
				})
				text(row, {
					Name = "Progress",
					Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 34),
					Size = UDim2.new(1, -160, 0, 20),
					Text = string.format("%d / %d   •   +%d coins, +%d gems", entry.Progress, template.Amount, template.Coins, template.Gems),
					Font = FONT_REG,
					TextSize = 12,
					TextColor3 = C.InkSoft,
				})
				local track, fill = bar(row, {
					Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 62),
					Size = UDim2.new(1, -190, 0, 14),
				})
				fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(math.clamp(entry.Progress / template.Amount, 0, 1), 1)
				fill.BackgroundColor3 = if entry.Claimed then C.Muted else C.Mint

				local complete = entry.Progress >= template.Amount
				local claim
				claim = button(row, {
					Name = "Claim",
					AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
					Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0.5, 0),
					Size = UDim2.new(0, 150, 0, 46),
					Text = if entry.Claimed then "CLAIMED" else (complete and "CLAIM" or "IN PROGRESS"),
					TextSize = 14,
					BackgroundColor3 = if complete and not entry.Claimed then C.Mint else C.PaperDim,
				}, function()
					Remotes.Get(Remotes.ClaimQuest):FireServer(index)
					claim.Text = "CLAIMED"
					fill.BackgroundColor3 = C.Muted
					setDisabled(claim, true)
				end)
				if entry.Claimed or not complete then
					setDisabled(claim, true)
				end
			end
		end

		text(parent, {
			Name = "AchievementHeader",
			Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 36),
			Text = "ACHIEVEMENTS",
			Font = FONT,
			TextSize = 15,
			TextColor3 = C.Ink,
		})

		for _, achievement in ipairs(Quests.Achievements) do
			local unlocked = data.Achievements and data.Achievements[achievement.Id] == true
			local row = card(parent, {
				Name = achievement.Id,
				Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 56),
				BackgroundColor3 = if unlocked then Color3.fromRGB(226, 246, 232) else C.PaperDim,
			})
			text(row, {
				Name = "Name",
				Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 6),
				Size = UDim2.new(0.65, 0, 0, 24),
				Text = (if unlocked then "✓  " else "") .. achievement.Name,
				Font = FONT,
				TextSize = 15,
				TextColor3 = if unlocked then C.Mint else C.Ink,
			})
			text(row, {
				Name = "Reward",
				Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 30),
				Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 20),
				Text = string.format("+%d coins, +%d gems", achievement.Coins or 0, achievement.Gems or 0),
				Font = FONT_REG,
				TextSize = 12,
				TextColor3 = C.InkSoft,
			})
		end
	end)
end

function UI.OpenSettings()
	bindingCaptureAction = nil
	table.clear(bindingButtons)
	local labels = {
		{ "Forward", "MOVE FORWARD" },
		{ "Backward", "MOVE BACKWARD" },
		{ "Left", "STRAFE LEFT" },
		{ "Right", "STRAFE RIGHT" },
		{ "Descend", "DESCEND" },
		{ "Ascend", "ASCEND" },
		{ "Fire", "KEYBOARD FIRE (MOUSE 1 ALWAYS WORKS)" },
		{ "Boost", "BOOST" },
	}
	local function refreshBindingButton(action: string)
		local b = bindingButtons[action]
		if b and b.Parent then
			local key = Flight.GetBindings()[action]
			b.Text = key and key.Name or "UNBOUND"
		end
	end

	openModal("FLIGHT SETTINGS", function(parent)
		text(parent, {
			Name = "Intro",
			Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 42),
			Text = "Select a binding, then press the new keyboard key. Mouse 1 remains the primary fire control.",
			Font = FONT_REG,
			TextSize = 13,
			TextColor3 = C.InkSoft,
			TextWrapped = true,
			TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
		})
		for _, definition in ipairs(labels) do
			local action, labelText = definition[1], definition[2]
			local row = card(parent, {
				Name = "Binding_" .. action,
				Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 48),
				BackgroundColor3 = C.PaperDim,
			})
			text(row, {
				Name = "Label",
				Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0),
				Size = UDim2.new(1, -160, 1, 0),
				Text = labelText,
				Font = FONT_BOLD,
				TextSize = 11,
				TextColor3 = C.Ink,
				TextWrapped = true,
			})
			local keyButton: TextButton
			keyButton = button(row, {
				Name = "Key",
				AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
				Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0),
				Size = UDim2.new(0, 128, 0, 36),
				Text = Flight.GetBindings()[action].Name,
				TextSize = 13,
				BackgroundColor3 = C.Sky,
			}, function()
				bindingCaptureAction = action
				keyButton.Text = "PRESS A KEY..."
			end)
			bindingButtons[action] = keyButton
		end

		button(parent, {
			Name = "ResetBindings",
			Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 46),
			Text = "RESET DEFAULT CONTROLS",
			TextSize = 14,
			BackgroundColor3 = C.Coral,
		}, function()
			Flight.ResetBindings()
			bindingCaptureAction = nil
			for action in pairs(bindingButtons) do refreshBindingButton(action) end
			toast("Default controls restored for this session")
		end)

		text(parent, {
			Name = "PersistenceNote",
			Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 34),
			Text = "Control changes currently last for this play session. Account-level saving requires the server settings handler.",
			Font = FONT_REG,
			TextSize = 11,
			TextColor3 = C.Muted,
			TextWrapped = true,
		})
	end)
end

function UI.OpenCodes()
	openModal("REDEEM A CODE", function(parent)
		local box = new("TextBox", {
			Name = "Input",
			Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 56),
			BackgroundColor3 = C.White,
			BorderSizePixel = 2,
			BorderColor3 = C.Ink,
			Font = FONT,
			TextSize = 22,
			PlaceholderText = "TYPE CODE",
			Text = "",
			ClearTextOnFocus = false,
			TextColor3 = C.Ink,
		}, parent)
		box.PlaceholderColor3 = C.Muted
		corner(box, 10)

		button(parent, {
			Name = "Redeem",
			Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 54),
			Text = "REDEEM",
			TextSize = 20,
			BackgroundColor3 = C.Mint,
		}, function()
			Remotes.Get(Remotes.RedeemCode):FireServer(box.Text)
			box.Text = ""
		end)

		text(parent, {
			Name = "Hint",
			Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 40),
			Text = "Codes come from the group wall and update videos.",
			Font = FONT_REG,
			TextSize = 13,
			TextColor3 = C.Muted,
			TextWrapped = true,
		})
	end)
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------ leaderboard ---
local MEDALS = {
	Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 80),
	Color3.fromRGB(206, 212, 222),
	Color3.fromRGB(206, 150, 96),
}

local function renderLeaderboard(parent)
	if not leaderboardData then
		text(parent, {
			Name = "Loading",
			Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 60),
			Text = "LOADING RANKINGS...",
			Font = FONT,
			TextSize = 16,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
			TextColor3 = C.Muted,
		})
		return
	end

	local entries = leaderboardData.Entries or {}
	if #entries == 0 then
		text(parent, {
			Name = "Empty",
			Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 80),
			Text = "NO FLIGHTS RECORDED YET.\nBE THE FIRST TO SET A RECORD.",
			Font = FONT,
			TextSize = 15,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
			TextColor3 = C.Muted,
			TextWrapped = true,
		})
	end

	for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
		local rank = tonumber(entry.Rank) or 0
		local row = card(parent, {
			Name = "Rank" .. rank,
			Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 54),
			BackgroundColor3 = if rank <= 3 then C.PaperDim else C.Paper,
		})
		text(row, {
			Name = "Rank",
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0),
			Size = UDim2.new(0, 54, 1, 0),
			Text = "#" .. tostring(rank),
			Font = FONT,
			TextSize = 18,
			TextColor3 = MEDALS[rank] or C.InkSoft,
		})
		text(row, {
			Name = "Name",
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 64, 0, 0),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, -220, 1, 0),
			Text = tostring(entry.Name or "Pilot"),
			Font = FONT_BOLD,
			TextSize = 16,
			TextColor3 = C.Ink,
			TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
		})
		text(row, {
			Name = "Distance",
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
			Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 0),
			Size = UDim2.new(0, 150, 1, 0),
			Text = fmt(entry.Distance) .. " m",
			Font = FONT,
			TextSize = 17,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
			TextColor3 = C.Sky,
		})
	end

	text(parent, {
		Name = "Personal",
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 46),
		Text = "YOUR BEST   " .. fmt(leaderboardData.Personal or 0) .. " m",
		Font = FONT,
		TextSize = 15,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
		TextColor3 = C.Ink,
	})
end

function UI.OpenLeaderboard()
	leaderboardOpen = true
	openModal("TOP FLIGHTS", renderLeaderboard)
	Remotes.Get(Remotes.RequestLeaderboard):FireServer()
end

function UI.CloseModal()
	modalOpen = false
	leaderboardOpen = false
	bindingCaptureAction = nil
	if modal then
		modal.Visible = false
	end
end

function UI.CloseResults()
	if resultsFrame then
		resultsFrame.Visible = false
	end
	if menu then
		menu.Visible = true
	end
	lastServerResult = nil
end

function UI.CloseAll()
	UI.CloseShop()
	UI.CloseModal()
	if resultsFrame then
		resultsFrame.Visible = false
	end
end

-- Exercises every tab, modal and HUD path once. Wired to the DevSelfTest flag;
-- prints a PASS/FAIL line so an automated playtest can catch UI errors that
-- only happen on a screen nobody opened.
function UI.SelfTest(): string
	local results = {}
	local function check(name: string, fn)
		local ok, err = pcall(fn)
		table.insert(results, name .. (ok and "=ok" or ("=FAIL:" .. tostring(err))))
	end

	check("menu", function()
		menu.Visible = true
	end)
	check("shop", function()
		UI.OpenShop()
	end)
	for _, tab in ipairs({ "Upgrades", "Prestige", "Planes", "Weapons", "Pets", "Shields", "Eggs", "Hangars", "Passes" }) do
		check(tab, function()
			UI.SelectTab(tab)
		end)
	end
	check("closeShop", function()
		UI.CloseShop()
	end)
	check("quests", function()
		UI.OpenQuests()
	end)
	check("closeModal", function()
		UI.CloseModal()
	end)
	check("codes", function()
		UI.OpenCodes()
	end)
	check("closeModal2", function()
		UI.CloseModal()
	end)
	check("settings", function()
		UI.OpenSettings()
	end)
	check("closeSettings", function()
		UI.CloseModal()
	end)
	check("leaderboard", function()
		UI.OpenLeaderboard()
	end)
	check("closeModal3", function()
		UI.CloseModal()
	end)
	check("hud", function()
		updateHud(80, 100, 1234, 500, 5, 12, "PAPER TOWN", 0.4, 5, 1.2, 50, 1, true)
	end)
	check("results", function()
		updateResults({ Distance = 1234, Kills = 12, Coins = 500, Gems = 5, BestCombo = 5, Reason = "death" })
	end)
	check("closeResults", function()
		UI.CloseResults()
	end)

	return table.concat(results, " | ")
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------- init ---
function UI.Init()
	if gui and gui.Parent then
		gui:Destroy()
	end

	gui = new("ScreenGui", {
		Name = "PaperWingsUI",
		ResetOnSpawn = false,
		IgnoreGuiInset = true,
		DisplayOrder = 10,
		ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
		Parent = playerGui,
	})

	scaler = new("UIScale", { Scale = 1 }, gui)
	local function fit()
		local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if camera then
			scaler.Scale = math.clamp(math.min(camera.ViewportSize.X / 1200, camera.ViewportSize.Y / 760), 0.55, 1)
		end
	end
	fit()
	if workspace.CurrentCamera then
		workspace.CurrentCamera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(fit)
	end

	toastBox = card(gui, {
		Name = "Toast",
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 8),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 420, 0, 46),
		BackgroundColor3 = C.Ink,
		Visible = false,
	})
	toastBox.ZIndex = 40
	toastLabel = text(toastBox, {
		Name = "Text",
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		Text = "",
		Font = FONT,
		TextSize = 14,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
	})

	buildMenu()
	buildHud()
	buildShop()
	buildResults()
	buildModal()

	State.OnLoaded(function()
		UI.RefreshTop()
		UI.RefreshShop()
	end)
	State.OnUpdate(function()
		UI.RefreshTop()
		UI.RefreshShop()
	end)
	State.OnRunUpdate(function(...)
		updateHud(...)
	end)
	State.OnRunEnded(function(result)
		lastServerResult = nil
		updateResults(result)
		UI.RefreshTop()
	end)
	State.OnNotify(function(message)
		toast(message)
	end)

	Remotes.Get(Remotes.LeaderboardData).OnClientEvent:Connect(function(payload)
		leaderboardData = payload
		if leaderboardOpen and modalOpen then
			openModal("TOP FLIGHTS", renderLeaderboard)
		end
	end)

	Remotes.Get(Remotes.ShopSync).OnClientEvent:Connect(function(success, message)
		if success then
			Effects.Play("Upgrade", nil, 0.4)
			if message and message ~= "" then
				toast(message)
			end
		elseif message and message ~= "" then
			toast(message)
		end
		UI.RefreshTop()
		UI.RefreshShop()
	end)

	Remotes.Get(Remotes.RunEvent).OnClientEvent:Connect(function(kind, payload)
		if kind == "Ended" then
			lastServerResult = payload
			-- The results screen opens with the client's local estimate (which
			-- includes coin pickups the server does not pay). Once the
			-- authoritative payout arrives, rewrite the numbers so the player
			-- never sees a figure that is not what actually hit their balance.
			if payload and resultsFrame.Visible then
				resultsRefs.Distance.Text = "DISTANCE  " .. fmt(payload.Distance) .. " m"
				resultsRefs.Kills.Text = "TARGETS  " .. tostring(payload.Kills or 0)
				resultsRefs.Coins.Text = "COINS  " .. fmt(payload.Coins)
				resultsRefs.Gems.Text = "GEMS  " .. fmt(payload.Gems)
				resultsRefs.subtitle.Text = "REWARDS SAVED"
			end
			UI.RefreshTop()
			UI.RefreshShop()
		elseif kind == "Rebirth" then
			Effects.Play("Upgrade", nil, 0.6)
			showBanner("REBIRTH " .. tostring(payload and payload.Rebirths or ""))
		end
	end)

	UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
		if bindingCaptureAction and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
			local action = bindingCaptureAction
			bindingCaptureAction = nil
			local ok, message = Flight.SetBinding(action, input.KeyCode)
			if ok then
				local b = bindingButtons[action]
				if b then b.Text = input.KeyCode.Name end
				toast("Updated " .. action .. " binding")
			else
				local b = bindingButtons[action]
				if b then b.Text = Flight.GetBindings()[action].Name end
				toast(message)
			end
			return
		end
		if processed then
			return
		end
		if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
			if modalOpen then
				UI.CloseModal()
			elseif shopOpen then
				UI.CloseShop()
			end
		end
	end)

	UI.RefreshTop()

	-- Dev hook: run the UI smoke test once when ReplicatedStorage.DevSelfTest
	-- exists. Absent in a published place.
	if ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("DevSelfTest") then
		task.delay(3, function()
			print("[UI] selftest " .. UI.SelfTest())
		end)
	end
end

return UI

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/client/WorldBuilder.luau
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- ============================================================================
--!nonstrict
-- WorldBuilder: procedural paper-craft scenery, enemies, bosses and pickups.
--
-- Each zone has its own recipe (`Style`) so the world changes character every
-- few thousand metres instead of only changing colour. Everything is built from
-- parts, so it streams in instantly and costs nothing to download.

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")
local GameConfig = require(Shared:WaitForChild("GameConfig"))

local WorldBuilder = {}

local PAPER = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
local FOIL = Enum.Material.Foil
local GLASS = Enum.Material.Glass
local NEON = Enum.Material.Neon
local WOOD = Enum.Material.Wood
local FABRIC = Enum.Material.Fabric
local GRASS = Enum.Material.Grass
local METAL = Enum.Material.CorrodedMetal

-- ------------------------------------------------------------- primitives ---
local function makePart(parent, className, name, size, cframe, color, material): BasePart
	local p = Instance.new(className)
	p.Name = name
	p.Size = size
	p.CFrame = cframe
	p.Color = color
	p.Material = material or PAPER
	p.Anchored = true
	p.CanCollide = false
	p.CanTouch = false
	p.CanQuery = false
	p.CastShadow = true
	p.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
	p.BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
	p.Parent = parent
	return p
end

local function part(parent, name, size, pos, color, material, rx, ry, rz)
	local cf = CFrame.new(pos) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(rx or 0), math.rad(ry or 0), math.rad(rz or 0))
	return makePart(parent, "Part", name, size, cf, color, material)
end

local function wedge(parent, name, size, pos, color, material, rx, ry, rz)
	local cf = CFrame.new(pos) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(rx or 0), math.rad(ry or 0), math.rad(rz or 0))
	return makePart(parent, "WedgePart", name, size, cf, color, material)
end

local function ball(parent, name, diameter, pos, color, material)
	return makePart(parent, "Part", name, Vector3.new(diameter, diameter, diameter), CFrame.new(pos), color, material)
end

local function ellipsoid(parent, name, size, pos, color, material, rx, ry, rz)
	local p = makePart(
		parent,
		"Part",
		name,
		size,
		CFrame.new(pos) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(rx or 0), math.rad(ry or 0), math.rad(rz or 0)),
		color,
		material
	)
	p.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
	return p
end

local function cylinder(parent, name, size, pos, color, material, rx, ry, rz)
	local p = makePart(
		parent,
		"Part",
		name,
		size,
		CFrame.new(pos) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(rx or 0), math.rad(ry or 0), math.rad(rz or 0)),
		color,
		material
	)
	p.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
	return p
end

-- Uniformly scale a finished model around its primary part.
local function scaleModel(model: Model, scale: number)
	if scale == 1 or not model.PrimaryPart then
		return
	end
	local origin = model.PrimaryPart.CFrame
	for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
		if d:IsA("BasePart") and d ~= model.PrimaryPart then
			local offset = origin:PointToObjectSpace(d.CFrame.Position)
			d.CFrame = origin * CFrame.new(offset * scale) * (d.CFrame - d.CFrame.Position)
			d.Size = d.Size * scale
		end
	end
	model.PrimaryPart.Size = model.PrimaryPart.Size * scale
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- clouds ---
function WorldBuilder.BuildCloud(position: Vector3, scale: number): Model
	local model = Instance.new("Model")
	model.Name = "Cloud"
	local white = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	local shade = Color3.fromRGB(224, 233, 244)
	local offsets = {
		{ Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), 4.6 },
		{ Vector3.new(3.6, 0.4, 0.5), 3.4 },
		{ Vector3.new(-3.7, 0.2, -0.4), 3.2 },
		{ Vector3.new(1.6, 1.0, 0.7), 2.8 },
		{ Vector3.new(-1.8, 0.9, -0.6), 2.6 },
		{ Vector3.new(0.2, -0.7, 0.2), 3.8 },
	}
	for i, entry in ipairs(offsets) do
		local size = entry[2] * scale
		local puff = ball(model, "Puff" .. i, size, position + entry[1] * scale, if i % 2 == 0 then shade else white, PAPER)
		puff.Size = Vector3.new(size, size * 0.62, size)
		puff.CastShadow = false
	end
	return model
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------ zone pieces ---
local function groundStrip(parent, pathColor, groundColor, halfLength, centerZ, pathWidth)
	part(parent, "Path", Vector3.new(pathWidth, 1, halfLength * 2), Vector3.new(0, 0.2, centerZ), pathColor, PAPER)
	part(parent, "Ground", Vector3.new(420, 2, halfLength * 2), Vector3.new(0, -1, centerZ), groundColor, GRASS)
end

local function buildHouse(parent, position, wallColor, roofColor, rng)
	local house = Instance.new("Model")
	house.Name = "House"
	local w = 11 + rng:NextNumber() * 5
	local d = 10 + rng:NextNumber() * 4
	local h = 8 + rng:NextNumber() * 5

	part(house, "Walls", Vector3.new(w, h, d), position + Vector3.new(0, h / 2, 0), wallColor, PAPER)

	-- Low gable roof: two sloped panels with a ridge and eave trim. The edges
	-- are crisp paper folds, not tubes or cylinders.
	local roofHeight = h * 0.42
	local halfSpan = w / 2 + 1.2
	local slopeLength = math.sqrt(halfSpan * halfSpan + roofHeight * roofHeight)
	local roofAngle = math.atan2(roofHeight, halfSpan)
	for side = -1, 1, 2 do
		local roofCenter = position + Vector3.new(side * halfSpan / 2, h + roofHeight / 2, 0)
		local slab = part(house, "Roof", Vector3.new(slopeLength, 0.45, d + 2.4), roofCenter, roofColor, PAPER)
		slab.CFrame = CFrame.new(roofCenter) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, -side * roofAngle)
		part(
			house,
			"EaveTrim",
			Vector3.new(0.45, 0.32, d + 2.6),
			position + Vector3.new(side * halfSpan, h + 0.16, 0),
			roofColor:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), 0.18),
			PAPER
		)
	end
	part(house, "RoofCap", Vector3.new(0.9, 0.42, d + 2.6), position + Vector3.new(0, h + roofHeight, 0), roofColor:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), 0.12), PAPER)
	for _, side in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
		part(
			house,
			"Gable",
			Vector3.new(w * 0.62, roofHeight * 0.58, 0.5),
			position + Vector3.new(0, h + roofHeight * 0.29, side * (d / 2 + 0.05)),
			wallColor,
			PAPER
		)
	end

	local doorColor = Color3.fromRGB(120, 82, 52)
	part(house, "Door", Vector3.new(2.6, 4.2, 0.4), position + Vector3.new(0, 2.1, d / 2 + 0.2), doorColor, WOOD)
	part(house, "DoorLintel", Vector3.new(3.2, 0.35, 0.6), position + Vector3.new(0, 4.3, d / 2 + 0.22), doorColor, WOOD)
	for _, side in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
		part(house, "DoorTrim", Vector3.new(0.28, 4.4, 0.6), position + Vector3.new(side * 1.45, 2.2, d / 2 + 0.22), doorColor, WOOD)
	end

	local glass = Color3.fromRGB(160, 214, 244)
	for _, side in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
		local windowPos = position + Vector3.new(side * w * 0.3, h * 0.62, d / 2 + 0.14)
		part(house, "WindowFrame" .. side, Vector3.new(3.2, 3.1, 0.3), windowPos, Color3.fromRGB(242, 238, 228), PAPER)
		part(house, "Window" .. side, Vector3.new(2.5, 2.4, 0.36), windowPos + Vector3.new(0, 0, 0.08), glass, GLASS)
		part(house, "WindowMullionV" .. side, Vector3.new(0.16, 2.4, 0.4), windowPos + Vector3.new(0, 0, 0.12), Color3.fromRGB(242, 238, 228), PAPER)
		part(house, "WindowMullionH" .. side, Vector3.new(2.5, 0.16, 0.4), windowPos + Vector3.new(0, 0, 0.12), Color3.fromRGB(242, 238, 228), PAPER)
		part(house, "WindowSill" .. side, Vector3.new(3.5, 0.25, 0.7), windowPos + Vector3.new(0, -1.7, 0.16), Color3.fromRGB(242, 238, 228), PAPER)
	end

	local chimneyTop = h + roofHeight * 0.62 + 1.55
	part(
		house,
		"Chimney",
		Vector3.new(1.5, 3.4, 1.5),
		position + Vector3.new(w * 0.28, chimneyTop - 1.7, -d * 0.18),
		Color3.fromRGB(170, 92, 78),
		PAPER
	)
	part(
		house,
		"ChimneyCap",
		Vector3.new(1.9, 0.3, 1.9),
		position + Vector3.new(w * 0.28, chimneyTop, -d * 0.18),
		Color3.fromRGB(140, 76, 66),
		PAPER
	)

	-- Paper-strip fence in front.
	local fenceColor = Color3.fromRGB(230, 224, 210)
	for i = -2, 2 do
		part(
			house,
			"FencePost",
			Vector3.new(0.28, 2.0, 0.28),
			position + Vector3.new(i * 2.4, 1.0, d / 2 + 2.2),
			fenceColor,
			PAPER
		)
	end
	for _, y in ipairs({ 0.65, 1.5 }) do
		part(
			house,
			"FenceRail",
			Vector3.new(w * 0.8, 0.22, 0.22),
			position + Vector3.new(0, y, d / 2 + 2.2),
			fenceColor,
			PAPER
		)
	end

	house.Parent = parent
	return house
end

local function buildTree(parent, position, leafColor, rng)
	local tree = Instance.new("Model")
	tree.Name = "Tree"
	local height = 5 + rng:NextNumber() * 3
	cylinder(tree, "Trunk", Vector3.new(height, 1.2, 1.2), position + Vector3.new(0, height / 2, 0), Color3.fromRGB(122, 84, 52), WOOD, 0, 0, 90)
	local crownY = height + 0.9
	ellipsoid(tree, "Canopy", Vector3.new(7.2, 6.0, 7.2), position + Vector3.new(0, crownY, 0), leafColor, GRASS)
	local side = if rng:NextNumber() > 0.5 then 1 else -1
	ellipsoid(
		tree,
		"CanopySide",
		Vector3.new(4.8, 4.2, 4.8),
		position + Vector3.new(side * 1.9, crownY + 0.7, 0.5),
		leafColor:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(40, 70, 40), 0.08),
		GRASS
	)
	ellipsoid(
		tree,
		"CanopyTop",
		Vector3.new(4.2, 3.6, 4.2),
		position + Vector3.new(-side * 0.9, crownY + 1.7, -0.4),
		leafColor:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), 0.06),
		GRASS
	)
	tree.Parent = parent
	return tree
end

local function buildPencil(parent, position, color, height)
	local pencil = Instance.new("Model")
	pencil.Name = "Pencil"
	cylinder(pencil, "Body", Vector3.new(height, 2.4, 2.4), position + Vector3.new(0, height / 2, 0), color, PAPER, 0, 0, 90)
	cylinder(pencil, "Tip", Vector3.new(1.5, 2.6, 2.6), position + Vector3.new(0, height + 0.7, 0), Color3.fromRGB(240, 205, 130), PAPER, 0, 0, 90)
	cylinder(pencil, "Lead", Vector3.new(1.0, 1.2, 1.2), position + Vector3.new(0, height + 1.7, 0), Color3.fromRGB(70, 68, 76), PAPER, 0, 0, 90)
	pencil.Parent = parent
	return pencil
end

local function buildNotebook(parent, position, color, size, rng)
	local nb = Instance.new("Model")
	nb.Name = "Notebook"
	part(nb, "Cover", size, position, color, PAPER)
	part(
		nb,
		"Pages",
		size * Vector3.new(0.94, 1.0, 0.94) + Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0),
		position + Vector3.new(0, 0.55, 0),
		Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
		PAPER
	)
	-- Spiral binding along one edge.
	local count = math.clamp(math.floor(size.Z / 2.2), 3, 12)
	for i = 1, count do
		local z = position.Z - size.Z / 2 + (i - 0.5) * (size.Z / count)
		cylinder(nb, "Ring" .. i, Vector3.new(1.4, 0.3, 0.3), Vector3.new(position.X - size.X / 2 - 0.2, position.Y + size.Y / 2 + 0.2, z), Color3.fromRGB(150, 156, 170), METAL, 0, 0, 90)
	end
	nb.Parent = parent
	return nb
end

local function buildScissor(parent, position, scale, rng)
	local sc = Instance.new("Model")
	sc.Name = "Scissor"
	local metal = Color3.fromRGB(198, 202, 212)
	local handle = Color3.fromRGB(90, 150, 210)
	local blade = 9 * scale
	wedge(sc, "BladeA", Vector3.new(0.5 * scale, blade, 1.2 * scale), position + Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), metal, FOIL, 0, 0, 22)
	wedge(sc, "BladeB", Vector3.new(0.5 * scale, blade, 1.2 * scale), position + Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), metal, FOIL, 0, 0, -22)
	part(sc, "Pivot", Vector3.new(1.6, 1.6, 1.6) * scale, position + Vector3.new(0, -blade * 0.5, 0), Color3.fromRGB(120, 124, 134), METAL)
	cylinder(sc, "HandleL", Vector3.new(0.7, 3.4, 3.4) * scale, position + Vector3.new(-1.4, -blade * 0.85, 0) * scale, handle, PAPER, 0, 0, 90)
	cylinder(sc, "HandleR", Vector3.new(0.7, 3.4, 3.4) * scale, position + Vector3.new(1.4, -blade * 0.85, 0) * scale, handle, PAPER, 0, 0, 90)
	sc.Parent = parent
	return sc
end

local function buildInkwell(parent, position, scale)
	local ink = Instance.new("Model")
	ink.Name = "Inkwell"
	local dark = Color3.fromRGB(26, 22, 52)
	part(ink, "Bottle", Vector3.new(12, 14, 12) * scale, position + Vector3.new(0, 7 * scale, 0), dark, GLASS)
	part(ink, "Neck", Vector3.new(6, 5, 6) * scale, position + Vector3.new(0, 16 * scale, 0), dark, GLASS)
	cylinder(ink, "Cap", Vector3.new(2, 8, 8) * scale, position + Vector3.new(0, 19 * scale, 0), Color3.fromRGB(190, 170, 120), PAPER)
	part(ink, "Label", Vector3.new(8, 5, 0.3) * scale, position + Vector3.new(0, 8 * scale, 6.1 * scale), Color3.fromRGB(240, 236, 220), PAPER)
	ink.Parent = parent
	return ink
end

-- --------------------------------------------------------------- recipes ---
local recipes = {}

recipes.town = function(parent, zone, centerZ, halfLength, rng)
	groundStrip(parent, zone.Path, zone.Ground, halfLength, centerZ, 34)

	-- Gentle rolling hills behind the houses.
	for i = 1, 4 do
		local side = if i % 2 == 0 then 1 else -1
		ellipsoid(
			parent,
			"Hill" .. i,
			Vector3.new(130, 44, 96),
			Vector3.new(side * (150 + rng:NextInteger(0, 60)), -12, centerZ + rng:NextNumber(-halfLength, halfLength)),
			Color3.fromRGB(112, 178, 96),
			GRASS,
			0,
			rng:NextNumber(0, 90),
			0
		)
	end

	for i = 1, 7 do
		local side = if i % 2 == 0 then 1 else -1
		local x = side * (34 + rng:NextInteger(0, 42))
		local z = centerZ - halfLength + (i + rng:NextNumber()) * (halfLength * 2 / 8)
		buildHouse(
			parent,
			Vector3.new(x, 0, z),
			Color3.fromRGB(226, 196, 150),
			Color3.fromHSV(rng:NextNumber(), 0.55, 0.8),
			rng
		)
	end

	for i = 1, 10 do
		local side = if i % 2 == 0 then 1 else -1
		local x = side * (24 + rng:NextInteger(6, 34))
		local z = centerZ + rng:NextNumber(-halfLength, halfLength)
		buildTree(parent, Vector3.new(x, 0, z), Color3.fromRGB(92, 166, 86), rng)
	end

	-- Hanging cranes and clouds in the sky.
	for i = 1, 3 do
		local x = rng:NextNumber(-55, 55)
		local y = 42 + rng:NextNumber(0, 26)
		local z = centerZ + rng:NextNumber(-halfLength, halfLength)
		local crane = part(parent, "Crane", Vector3.new(9, 0.8, 9), Vector3.new(x, y, z), Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), PAPER, 12, rng:NextNumber(0, 360), -10)
		crane.Name = "Crane"
	end
end

recipes.desk = function(parent, zone, centerZ, halfLength, rng)
	groundStrip(parent, zone.Path, zone.Ground, halfLength, centerZ, 38)

	-- Giant pencils sticking up like pillars, stacked notebooks, scissors and inkwells.
	local pencilColors = { Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 50), Color3.fromRGB(80, 160, 240), Color3.fromRGB(235, 90, 90) }
	for i = 1, 8 do
		local side = if i % 2 == 0 then 1 else -1
		local x = side * (32 + rng:NextInteger(0, 48))
		local z = centerZ - halfLength + (i + rng:NextNumber()) * (halfLength * 2 / 9)
		local c = pencilColors[((i - 1) % #pencilColors) + 1]
		buildPencil(parent, Vector3.new(x, 0, z), c, 18 + rng:NextNumber() * 18)
	end

	for i = 1, 6 do
		local side = if i % 2 == 0 then 1 else -1
		local x = side * (30 + rng:NextInteger(0, 36))
		local z = centerZ + rng:NextNumber(-halfLength, halfLength)
		buildNotebook(parent, Vector3.new(x, 2, z), Color3.fromHSV(rng:NextNumber(), 0.5, 0.85), Vector3.new(18, 2.5, 24), rng)
	end

	for i = 1, 3 do
		local side = if i % 2 == 0 then 1 else -1
		buildScissor(parent, Vector3.new(side * (42 + i * 8), 8 + i * 4, centerZ + (i - 2) * 50), 1.4, rng)
	end

	for i = 1, 3 do
		local side = if i % 2 == 0 then 1 else -1
		buildInkwell(parent, Vector3.new(side * 46, 0, centerZ + rng:NextNumber(-halfLength, halfLength)), 1.3)
	end
end

recipes.canyon = function(parent, zone, centerZ, halfLength, rng)
	groundStrip(parent, zone.Path, zone.Ground, halfLength, centerZ, 34)

	-- Red-rock mesas and folded arches framing the corridor.
	local rock = Color3.fromRGB(204, 118, 76)
	local rockDark = Color3.fromRGB(168, 86, 54)
	for i = 1, 6 do
		local side = if i % 2 == 0 then 1 else -1
		local x = side * (44 + rng:NextInteger(0, 44))
		local z = centerZ - halfLength + (i + rng:NextNumber()) * (halfLength * 2 / 7)
		local h = 24 + rng:NextNumber() * 26
		part(parent, "Mesa", Vector3.new(28, h, 32), Vector3.new(x, h / 2, z), rock, PAPER)
		part(parent, "MesaCap", Vector3.new(30, 3, 34), Vector3.new(x, h + 1.5, z), rockDark, PAPER)
	end

	-- Overhead folded ribbon arch.
	local arch = part(parent, "RibbonArch", Vector3.new(110, 4, 12), Vector3.new(0, 56, centerZ), Color3.fromRGB(240, 178, 120), PAPER, 0, 0, 0)
	arch.Name = "RibbonArch"
end

recipes.city = function(parent, zone, centerZ, halfLength, rng)
	groundStrip(parent, zone.Path, zone.Ground, halfLength, centerZ, 40)

	-- Tall paper towers with glowing trim bands.
	for i = 1, 8 do
		local side = if i % 2 == 0 then 1 else -1
		local x = side * (36 + rng:NextInteger(0, 42))
		local z = centerZ - halfLength + (i + rng:NextNumber()) * (halfLength * 2 / 9)
		local h = 38 + rng:NextNumber() * 54
		part(parent, "Tower", Vector3.new(18, h, 18), Vector3.new(x, h / 2, z), Color3.fromRGB(220, 232, 248), PAPER)
		part(parent, "NeonBand", Vector3.new(18.6, 1.5, 18.6), Vector3.new(x, h * 0.75, z), zone.Accent, NEON)
	end
end

recipes.void = function(parent, zone, centerZ, halfLength, rng)
	-- Floating paper islands and neon rings in dark space; no ground.
	for i = 1, 10 do
		local side = if i % 2 == 0 then 1 else -1
		local x = side * (24 + rng:NextInteger(0, 68))
		local y = 8 + rng:NextNumber() * 44
		local z = centerZ + rng:NextNumber(-halfLength, halfLength)
		part(parent, "Island", Vector3.new(16, 4, 16), Vector3.new(x, y, z), Color3.fromRGB(54, 48, 86), PAPER, rng:NextNumber(-15, 15), rng:NextNumber(0, 360), rng:NextNumber(-15, 15))
		part(parent, "GlowRing", Vector3.new(18, 0.6, 18), Vector3.new(x, y + 2.2, z), zone.Accent, NEON)
	end
end

recipes.ocean = function(parent, zone, centerZ, halfLength, rng)
	groundStrip(parent, zone.Path, zone.Ground, halfLength, centerZ, 44)
	local deep = Color3.fromRGB(23, 38, 78)
	-- Layered islands, sea stacks and luminous tide markers give this zone a
	-- recognizable silhouette without putting scenery in the flight lane.
	for i = 1, 9 do
		local side = if i % 2 == 0 then 1 else -1
		local x = side * (42 + rng:NextInteger(0, 32))
		local z = centerZ + rng:NextNumber(-halfLength, halfLength)
		local width = rng:NextNumber(16, 28)
		local height = rng:NextNumber(10, 24)
		part(parent, "InkSeaStack", Vector3.new(width, height, width * 1.2), Vector3.new(x, height * 0.5 - 1, z), deep, FOIL, rng:NextNumber(-8, 8), rng:NextNumber(0, 360), rng:NextNumber(-8, 8))
		part(parent, "SeaFoamCap", Vector3.new(width * 1.08, 1.2, width * 1.28), Vector3.new(x, height + 0.1, z), zone.Accent, NEON)
	end
	for i = 1, 5 do
		local x = (if i % 2 == 0 then -1 else 1) * (38 + i * 5)
		local z = centerZ + rng:NextNumber(-halfLength, halfLength)
		local column = cylinder(parent, "TideBeacon", Vector3.new(20, 2.2, 2.2), Vector3.new(x, 10, z), Color3.fromRGB(62, 72, 112), FOIL, 0, 0, 90)
		part(parent, "BeaconLight", Vector3.new(2.8, 2.8, 2.8), Vector3.new(x, 21, z), zone.Accent, NEON)
		column.Name = "TideBeacon"
	end
	for i = 1, 4 do
		local z = centerZ + rng:NextNumber(-halfLength, halfLength)
		local x = rng:NextNumber(-30, 30)
		local ring = cylinder(parent, "CurrentRing", Vector3.new(0.6, 18, 18), Vector3.new(x, 26 + rng:NextNumber(0, 10), z), zone.Accent, NEON, 0, 0, 90)
		ring.Transparency = 0.35
	end
end

recipes.storm = function(parent, zone, centerZ, halfLength, rng)
	groundStrip(parent, zone.Path, zone.Ground, halfLength, centerZ, 42)
	local stormDark = Color3.fromRGB(36, 38, 54)
	for i = 1, 8 do
		local side = if i % 2 == 0 then 1 else -1
		local x = side * (42 + rng:NextInteger(0, 42))
		local z = centerZ + rng:NextNumber(-halfLength, halfLength)
		local h = rng:NextNumber(28, 58)
		part(parent, "StormSpire", Vector3.new(rng:NextNumber(10, 18), h, 16), Vector3.new(x, h * 0.5, z), stormDark, FOIL, rng:NextNumber(-12, 12), rng:NextNumber(-20, 20), rng:NextNumber(-12, 12))
		part(parent, "StormCap", Vector3.new(18, 1.1, 18), Vector3.new(x, h + 0.5, z), zone.Accent, NEON)
	end
	for i = 1, 5 do
		local x = rng:NextNumber(-48, 48)
		local y = rng:NextNumber(42, 62)
		local z = centerZ + rng:NextNumber(-halfLength, halfLength)
		local cloud = WorldBuilder.BuildCloud(Vector3.new(x, y, z), rng:NextNumber(1.5, 2.6))
		for _, d in ipairs(cloud:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("BasePart") then d.Color = stormDark end
		end
		cloud.Parent = parent
	end
	-- Angular lightning-bolt landmarks, offset from the central flight lane.
	for side = -1, 1, 2 do
		local x = side * rng:NextNumber(32, 48)
		local y = rng:NextNumber(30, 48)
		local z = centerZ + rng:NextNumber(-halfLength * 0.7, halfLength * 0.7)
		for segment = 1, 3 do
			local p = part(parent, "LightningShard", Vector3.new(1.3, 1.3, rng:NextNumber(7, 12)), Vector3.new(x, y - segment * 5, z + segment * 5), zone.Accent, NEON, 0, 0, side * 24)
			p.Transparency = 0.18
		end
	end
end

recipes.clouds = function(parent, zone, centerZ, halfLength, rng)
	groundStrip(parent, zone.Path, zone.Ground, halfLength, centerZ, 44)
	local gold = Color3.fromRGB(255, 188, 100)
	for i = 1, 10 do
		local side = if i % 2 == 0 then 1 else -1
		local x = side * (36 + rng:NextInteger(0, 54))
		local z = centerZ + rng:NextNumber(-halfLength, halfLength)
		local y = rng:NextNumber(4, 18)
		local island = part(parent, "CloudIsland", Vector3.new(rng:NextNumber(18, 30), 5, rng:NextNumber(22, 38)), Vector3.new(x, y, z), Color3.fromRGB(238, 222, 204), PAPER, rng:NextNumber(-8, 8), rng:NextNumber(0, 360), rng:NextNumber(-8, 8))
		part(parent, "GoldSeam", Vector3.new(island.Size.X * 0.88, 0.35, 0.6), island.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2.7, 0), gold, NEON)
	end
	for i = 1, 5 do
		local x = (i - 3) * 30 + rng:NextNumber(-8, 8)
		local y = rng:NextNumber(44, 60)
		local z = centerZ + rng:NextNumber(-halfLength, halfLength)
		local cloud = WorldBuilder.BuildCloud(Vector3.new(x, y, z), rng:NextNumber(1.5, 2.2))
		cloud.Parent = parent
	end
	for i = 1, 3 do
		local x = (i - 2) * 34
		local z = centerZ + rng:NextNumber(-halfLength * 0.7, halfLength * 0.7)
		local arch = part(parent, "SunRing", Vector3.new(2, 32, 32), Vector3.new(x, 28, z), gold, NEON, 0, 0, 90)
		arch.Transparency = 0.3
	end
end

recipes.fold = function(parent, zone, centerZ, halfLength, rng)
	groundStrip(parent, zone.Path, zone.Ground, halfLength, centerZ, 46)
	local dark = Color3.fromRGB(26, 24, 44)
	local gold = Color3.fromRGB(255, 202, 92)
	for i = 1, 12 do
		local side = if i % 2 == 0 then 1 else -1
		local x = side * (34 + rng:NextInteger(0, 60))
		local z = centerZ + rng:NextNumber(-halfLength, halfLength)
		local h = rng:NextNumber(22, 52)
		local width = rng:NextNumber(12, 24)
		local fold = wedge(parent, "FoldedMonolith", Vector3.new(width, h, width * 0.8), Vector3.new(x, h * 0.5, z), dark, FOIL, rng:NextNumber(-10, 10), rng:NextNumber(0, 360), rng:NextNumber(-18, 18))
		part(parent, "FoldCrease", Vector3.new(width * 0.88, 0.5, 1), fold.Position + Vector3.new(0, h * 0.18, 0), gold, NEON, 0, 0, rng:NextNumber(-20, 20))
	end
	for i = 1, 4 do
		local z = centerZ + (i - 2.5) * 62
		local arch = part(parent, "OrigamiGate", Vector3.new(3, 44, 42), Vector3.new(0, 24, z), dark, FOIL, 0, 0, 90)
		arch.Transparency = 0.12
		part(parent, "GateCrest", Vector3.new(44, 2, 3), Vector3.new(0, 46, z), gold, NEON)
	end
	for i = 1, 16 do
		local angle = i / 16 * math.pi * 2
		local radius = rng:NextNumber(52, 76)
		local star = part(parent, "FoldStar", Vector3.new(1.2, 1.2, 1.2), Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * radius, rng:NextNumber(12, 54), centerZ + math.sin(angle) * radius), gold, NEON)
		star.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
	end
end

recipes.prism = function(parent, zone, centerZ, halfLength, rng)
	groundStrip(parent, zone.Path, zone.Ground, halfLength, centerZ, 40)

	-- Rainbow faceted pillars and floating foil shards.
	for i = 1, 8 do
		local side = if i % 2 == 0 then 1 else -1
		local x = side * (34 + rng:NextInteger(0, 44))
		local z = centerZ - halfLength + (i + rng:NextNumber()) * (halfLength * 2 / 9)
		local h = 28 + rng:NextNumber() * 42
		local col = Color3.fromHSV((i * 0.14 + rng:NextNumber() * 0.1) % 1, 0.75, 0.95)
		part(parent, "PrismPillar", Vector3.new(12, h, 12), Vector3.new(x, h / 2, z), col, FOIL, 0, rng:NextNumber(0, 45), 0)
	end
end

-- -------------------------------------------------------- flight barriers ---
local function buildElectricalBoundaries(parent, zone, centerZ, halfLength, rng)
	local corridor = GameConfig.Corridor
	local width = corridor.HalfWidth
	local top = corridor.MaxHeight
	local length = halfLength * 2
	local cyan = Color3.fromRGB(110, 220, 255)

	for _, side in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
		local wall = part(parent, "ElectricalWall", Vector3.new(0.5, top, length), Vector3.new(side * width, top * 0.5, centerZ), cyan, Enum.Material.ForceField)
		wall.Transparency = 0.91
		wall.Reflectance = 0.05
		wall.CanCollide = false
		local edge = part(parent, "ElectricalWallEdge", Vector3.new(0.65, 0.65, length), Vector3.new(side * width, top, centerZ), zone.Accent, NEON)
		edge.Transparency = 0.18
		local lower = part(parent, "ElectricalWallEdge", Vector3.new(0.45, 0.45, length), Vector3.new(side * width, 1.2, centerZ), cyan, NEON)
		lower.Transparency = 0.32

		-- Static jagged discharges make the barrier legible from a distance.
		for boltIndex = 1, 3 do
			local boltZ = centerZ + rng:NextNumber(-halfLength * 0.85, halfLength * 0.85)
			local boltY = rng:NextNumber(8, top - 8)
			local previous = Vector3.new(side * (width - 0.4), boltY, boltZ)
			for segmentIndex = 1, 3 do
				local nextPoint = previous + Vector3.new(side * rng:NextNumber(-1.2, 1.2), rng:NextNumber(-4, 4), rng:NextNumber(3, 7))
				local delta = nextPoint - previous
				local arc = part(parent, "ElectricalArc", Vector3.new(0.16, 0.16, math.max(0.1, delta.Magnitude)), (previous + nextPoint) * 0.5, cyan, NEON)
				arc.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(arc.Position, nextPoint)
				arc.Transparency = 0.12
				previous = nextPoint
			end
		end
	end

	local ceiling = part(parent, "ElectricalCeiling", Vector3.new(width * 2, 0.45, length), Vector3.new(0, top, centerZ), cyan, Enum.Material.ForceField)
	ceiling.Transparency = 0.93
	ceiling.CanCollide = false
	local ceilingEdge = part(parent, "ElectricalCeilingEdge", Vector3.new(width * 2, 0.35, 0.5), Vector3.new(0, top, centerZ - halfLength), zone.Accent, NEON)
	ceilingEdge.Transparency = 0.22
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------ chunks ---
function WorldBuilder.BuildChunk(zoneId: string, centerZ: number, halfLength: number, seed: number): Model
	local zone = GameConfig.ZonesById[zoneId] or GameConfig.Zones[1]
	local rng = Random.new(seed)
	local model = Instance.new("Model")
	model.Name = "Chunk_" .. zone.Id

	local recipe = recipes[zone.Style]
	if not recipe then
		warn(string.format("WorldBuilder: missing scenery recipe '%s' for zone '%s'", tostring(zone.Style), tostring(zone.Id)))
		model:Destroy()
		error("Missing WorldBuilder recipe: " .. tostring(zone.Style))
	end
	recipe(model, zone, centerZ, halfLength, rng)
	buildElectricalBoundaries(model, zone, centerZ, halfLength, rng)

	-- Floating clouds above the corridor in every zone except the void.
	if zone.Style == "town" or zone.Style == "canyon" then
		for i = 1, 5 do
			local x = (i - 3) * 34 + rng:NextNumber(-10, 10)
			local y = 52 + rng:NextNumber(0, 22)
			local z = centerZ + rng:NextNumber(-halfLength, halfLength)
			local cloud = WorldBuilder.BuildCloud(Vector3.new(x, y, z), 1.0 + rng:NextNumber() * 0.8)
			cloud.Parent = model
		end
	end

	return model
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- enemies ---
local enemyBuilders = {}

enemyBuilders.InkBlob = function(config, scale)
	local m = Instance.new("Model")
	m.Name = "Enemy_InkBlob"
	local c = config.Color

	ellipsoid(m, "Top", Vector3.new(4.2, 3.2, 4.2), Vector3.new(0, 2.2, 0), c, PAPER)
	for i = 1, 4 do
		local a = (i / 4) * math.pi * 2
		ball(m, "Drop" .. i, 1.8, Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * 2.8, -1.2, math.sin(a) * 2.8), c, PAPER)
	end
	ball(m, "EyeL", 1.1, Vector3.new(-1.1, 0.6, 2.2), Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), NEON)
	ball(m, "EyeR", 1.1, Vector3.new(1.1, 0.6, 2.2), Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), NEON)
	ball(m, "PupilL", 0.5, Vector3.new(-1.1, 0.6, 2.7), Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 30), PAPER)
	ball(m, "PupilR", 0.5, Vector3.new(1.1, 0.6, 2.7), Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 30), PAPER)
	m.PrimaryPart = body
	scaleModel(m, scale)
	return m
end
