function WorldBuilder.BuildEnemy(enemyId: string, scale: number?): Model
	local config = GameConfig.Enemies[enemyId] or GameConfig.Enemies.InkBlob
	local build = enemyBuilders[enemyId] or enemyBuilders.InkBlob
	return build(config, scale or config.Scale or 1)
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------ bosses ---
function WorldBuilder.BuildBoss(bossDef): Model
	local m = Instance.new("Model")
	m.Name = "Boss_" .. (bossDef.Name or "Boss")
	local main = bossDef.Color or Color3.fromRGB(240, 140, 160)
	local accent = bossDef.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 120)

	-- Giant folded golem / titan: wide shoulders, horned crown, glowing core.
	local torso = part(m, "Torso", Vector3.new(15, 16, 11), Vector3.zero, main, PAPER)
	part(m, "ChestBand", Vector3.new(15.6, 2.2, 11.6), Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), accent, NEON)
	part(m, "Head", Vector3.new(10, 9, 9), Vector3.new(0, 12.5, 0), main, PAPER)
	wedge(m, "HornL", Vector3.new(3, 8, 3), Vector3.new(-5.5, 18.5, 0), accent, PAPER, 0, 0, 24)
	wedge(m, "HornR", Vector3.new(3, 8, 3), Vector3.new(5.5, 18.5, 0), accent, PAPER, 0, 0, -24)
	part(m, "ArmL", Vector3.new(6, 15, 6), Vector3.new(-11, -1, 0), main, PAPER, 0, 0, 12)
	part(m, "ArmR", Vector3.new(6, 15, 6), Vector3.new(11, -1, 0), main, PAPER, 0, 0, -12)
	ball(m, "EyeL", 2.2, Vector3.new(-2.4, 13, 4.6), Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60), NEON)
	ball(m, "EyeR", 2.2, Vector3.new(2.4, 13, 4.6), Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60), NEON)
	m.PrimaryPart = torso
	return m
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------ pickups ---
function WorldBuilder.BuildCoin(): Model
	local m = Instance.new("Model")
	m.Name = "Coin"
	local disc = cylinder(m, "Disc", Vector3.new(0.6, 3.2, 3.2), Vector3.zero, Color3.fromRGB(255, 204, 64), FOIL)
	cylinder(m, "Rim", Vector3.new(0.75, 2.5, 2.5), Vector3.zero, Color3.fromRGB(255, 232, 130), NEON)
	m.PrimaryPart = disc
	return m
end

return WorldBuilder

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/server/DataService.luau
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
--!nonstrict
-- DataService: persistence with a Studio-safe fallback.
--
-- In Studio (or when the DataStore API is unavailable) every request falls
-- through to an in-memory store, so joins are instant and the console stays
-- clean instead of blocking on three retries of a request that cannot succeed.

local DataStoreService = game:GetService("DataStoreService")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local DataService = {}

local STORE_NAME = "PaperWings_v2"
local RETRIES = 3
local RETRY_BASE = 1

local isStudio = RunService:IsStudio()
local memory: { [string]: any } = {}

local store = nil
if not isStudio then
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return DataStoreService:GetDataStore(STORE_NAME)
	end)
	store = if ok then result else nil
end

function DataService.IsPersistent(): boolean
	return store ~= nil
end

local function safeRequest(action, fallback)
	local lastErr
	for attempt = 1, RETRIES do
		local ok, result = pcall(action)
		if ok then
			return result
		end
		lastErr = result
		if attempt < RETRIES then
			task.wait(RETRY_BASE * attempt)
		end
	end
	warn("[DataService] request failed:", lastErr)
	return fallback
end

function DataService.Load(key: string)
	if not store then
		local cached = memory[key]
		return if cached then table.clone(cached) else nil
	end
	return safeRequest(function()
		return store:GetAsync(key)
	end, nil)
end

function DataService.Save(key: string, data): boolean
	if not store then
		memory[key] = table.clone(data)
		return true
	end
	return safeRequest(function()
		store:SetAsync(key, data)
		return true
	end, false)
end

-- UpdateAsync variant used for the global leaderboard so concurrent writes from
-- several servers cannot lose entries.
function DataService.Update(key: string, transform)
	if not store then
		return nil
	end
	return safeRequest(function()
		return store:UpdateAsync(key, transform)
	end, nil)
end

function DataService.GetOrderedStore(name: string)
	if not store then
		return nil
	end
	local ok, ordered = pcall(function()
		return DataStoreService:GetOrderedDataStore(name)
	end)
	return if ok then ordered else nil
end

return DataService

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/server/GameService.luau
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
--!nonstrict
-- GameService: the authoritative server for Paper Wings.
--
-- The client simulates flight for responsiveness, but it never decides a payout.
-- A run must be started through RequestStart and reported once through
-- RunResult; the reward is derived from the *validated* distance and kill count,
-- clamped against the wall-clock time the run actually took.

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")
local Remotes = require(Shared:WaitForChild("Remotes"))
local GameConfig = require(Shared:WaitForChild("GameConfig"))
local Monetization = require(Shared:WaitForChild("Monetization"))
local Quests = require(Shared:WaitForChild("Quests"))

local DataService = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("DataService"))
local PlayerData = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("PlayerData"))
local MonetizationService = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("MonetizationService"))
local LeaderboardService = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("LeaderboardService"))

local GameService = {}

-- Anti-cheat ceilings. A legitimate run can never exceed these: top speed with
-- every multiplier and boost is ~62 m/s, and kills are bounded by spawn rate.
local MAX_METERS_PER_SECOND = 72
local MAX_KILLS_PER_SECOND = 12
local MIN_RUN_SECONDS = 2
local SAVE_INTERVAL = 45

local dataByPlayer: { [Player]: any } = {}
local runByPlayer: { [Player]: any } = {}
local dirty: { [Player]: boolean } = {}

local remotes = {}

local function keyFor(player: Player): string
	return "Player_" .. player.UserId
end

local function now(): number
	return Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- helpers ---
local function getData(player: Player)
	return dataByPlayer[player]
end

local function notify(player: Player, text: string)
	if remotes.Notify then
		remotes.Notify:FireClient(player, text)
	end
end

local function pushSnapshot(player: Player)
	local data = dataByPlayer[player]
	if not data then
		return
	end
	local snapshot = PlayerData.Snapshot(data, MonetizationService.GetPerks(player), MonetizationService.GetOwnedKeys(player))
	if remotes.DataUpdate then
		remotes.DataUpdate:FireClient(player, snapshot)
	end
	local stats = player:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
	if stats then
		local distance = stats:FindFirstChild("Distance")
		if distance then
			distance.Value = data.BestDistance
		end
		local rebirths = stats:FindFirstChild("Rebirths")
		if rebirths then
			rebirths.Value = data.Rebirths
		end
		local level = stats:FindFirstChild("Level")
		if level then
			level.Value = PlayerData.GetLevel(data)
		end
	end
end

local function markDirty(player: Player)
	dirty[player] = true
end

local function saveNow(player: Player)
	local data = dataByPlayer[player]
	if not data then
		return
	end
	data.LastSeen = os.time()
	dirty[player] = false
	task.spawn(function()
		DataService.Save(keyFor(player), data)
	end)
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------ quests ------
local function addQuestProgress(data, kind: string, amount: number)
	if amount <= 0 then
		return
	end
	PlayerData.EnsureDailyQuests(data)
	for _, entry in ipairs(data.Quests.List) do
		local template = PlayerData.FindQuestTemplate(entry.Id)
		if template and template.Type == kind and not entry.Claimed then
			entry.Progress = math.min(template.Amount, entry.Progress + amount)
		end
	end
end

local ACHIEVEMENT_VALUES = {
	Runs = function(data)
		return data.Runs
	end,
	TotalDistance = function(data)
		return data.TotalDistance
	end,
	TotalKills = function(data)
		return data.TotalKills
	end,
	Rebirths = function(data)
		return data.Rebirths
	end,
	Bosses = function(data)
		local count = 0
		for _ in pairs(data.BossesBeaten) do
			count += 1
		end
		return count
	end,
}

local function checkAchievements(player: Player, data)
	for _, achievement in ipairs(Quests.Achievements) do
		if not data.Achievements[achievement.Id] then
			local getter = ACHIEVEMENT_VALUES[achievement.Type]
			if getter and getter(data) >= achievement.Amount then
				data.Achievements[achievement.Id] = true
				data.Coins += achievement.Coins or 0
				data.Gems += achievement.Gems or 0
				notify(
					player,
					string.format("Achievement unlocked: %s  (+%d coins)", achievement.Name, achievement.Coins or 0)
				)
			end
		end
	end
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------- rewards ----
local function computeReward(data, perks, distance: number, kills: number, comboSteps: number, bossZone: string?)
	local stats = PlayerData.GetStats(data, perks)

	local comboBonus = math.min(GameConfig.Combo.MaxBonus, comboSteps * GameConfig.Combo.BonusPerKill)
	local coinMult = (stats.CoinMult or 1) * (1 + comboBonus)
	local gemMult = stats.GemMult or 1

	-- Deeper zones pay more per metre, so pushing distance always beats farming
	-- the first zone. The multiplier is based on how far the run actually got.
	local zoneCoinMult = GameConfig.GetZoneForDistance(distance).CoinMult or 1

	-- Live-ops bonus (weekend rush, midweek gems, ...).
	local event = GameConfig.GetActiveEvent()
	local eventCoinMult = (event and event.CoinMult) or 1
	local eventGemMult = (event and event.GemMult) or 1

	local coins = distance * GameConfig.CoinPerMeter * zoneCoinMult * coinMult * eventCoinMult
	coins += kills * GameConfig.KillCoinFlat * zoneCoinMult * coinMult * eventCoinMult
	local gems = distance * GameConfig.GemPerMeter * zoneCoinMult * gemMult * eventGemMult

	local bossReward = nil
	if bossZone then
		local boss = GameConfig.GetBossForZone(bossZone)
		if boss then
			coins += boss.Coins * (stats.CoinMult or 1)
			gems += boss.Gems * gemMult
			bossReward = { Zone = bossZone, Name = boss.Name, Coins = boss.Coins, Gems = boss.Gems }
		end
	end

	return math.floor(coins + 0.5), math.floor(gems + 0.5), bossReward, comboBonus
end

-- -------------------------------------------------------------- runs ------
local function handleRequestStart(player: Player)
	local data = dataByPlayer[player]
	if not data then
		return
	end
	local state = runByPlayer[player]
	if state and state.active then
		-- A previous run never reported back (client error, reconnect, abandoned
		-- run). Rather than locking the player out of flying forever, close that
		-- run out silently and let the new one start. No reward is granted for
		-- the abandoned attempt.
		runByPlayer[player] = { active = false, endedAt = now() }
	end
	-- No cooldown: a start carries no reward, and dropping a legitimate start
	-- (for example "Fly Again" clicked instantly) would silently void the run.
	runByPlayer[player] = { active = true, startedAt = now() }
	if remotes.DataLoaded then
		remotes.DataLoaded:FireClient(player, PlayerData.Snapshot(data, MonetizationService.GetPerks(player), MonetizationService.GetOwnedKeys(player)))
	end
end

local function handleRunResult(player: Player, payload: any)
	local data = dataByPlayer[player]
	local state = runByPlayer[player]
	if not data or not state or not state.active then
		return
	end
	state.active = false
	state.endedAt = now()

	local elapsed = math.max(0, now() - (state.startedAt or now()))
	if type(payload) ~= "table" or elapsed < MIN_RUN_SECONDS then
		runByPlayer[player] = { active = false, endedAt = now() }
		return
	end

	-- Clamp everything the client claims to what the clock allows.
	local maxDistance = elapsed * MAX_METERS_PER_SECOND + 150
	local claimedDistance = math.floor(tonumber(payload.Distance) or 0)
	local distance = math.clamp(claimedDistance, 0, math.min(maxDistance, 1e6))

	local maxKills = elapsed * MAX_KILLS_PER_SECOND + 30
	local claimedKills = math.floor(tonumber(payload.Kills) or 0)
	local kills = math.clamp(claimedKills, 0, math.min(maxKills, 1e6))

	local comboSteps = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(payload.Combo) or 0), 0, GameConfig.Combo.MaxSteps)

	local bossZone = nil
	if type(payload.BossZone) == "string" then
		local boss = GameConfig.GetBossForZone(payload.BossZone)
		if boss and distance >= boss.Distance then
			bossZone = boss.Zone
		end
	end

	local perks = MonetizationService.GetPerks(player)
	local coins, gems, bossReward, comboBonus = computeReward(data, perks, distance, kills, comboSteps, bossZone)

	data.Coins += coins
	data.Gems += gems
	data.TotalKills += kills
	data.TotalDistance += distance
	data.TotalCoinsEarned += coins
	data.TotalGemsEarned += gems
	data.Runs += 1
	data.BestDistance = math.max(data.BestDistance, distance)
	data.BestKills = math.max(data.BestKills, kills)
	if bossZone then
		data.BossesBeaten[bossZone] = true
	end

	addQuestProgress(data, "Kills", kills)
	addQuestProgress(data, "Distance", distance)
	addQuestProgress(data, "Coins", coins)
	addQuestProgress(data, "Gems", gems)
	addQuestProgress(data, "Runs", 1)
	if bossZone then
		addQuestProgress(data, "Bosses", 1)
	end

	checkAchievements(player, data)
	LeaderboardService.Submit(player, data.BestDistance)

	if claimedDistance > maxDistance + 1 or claimedKills > maxKills + 1 then
		warn(
			string.format(
				"[GameService] clamped suspicious run from %s: claimed %d m / %d kills in %.1fs",
				player.Name,
				claimedDistance,
				claimedKills,
				elapsed
			)
		)
	end

	pushSnapshot(player)
	markDirty(player)

	if remotes.RunEvent then
		remotes.RunEvent:FireClient(player, "Ended", {
			Distance = distance,
			Kills = kills,
			Coins = coins,
			Gems = gems,
			Combo = comboBonus,
			Boss = bossReward,
			Level = PlayerData.GetLevel(data),
		})
	end

	runByPlayer[player] = { active = false, endedAt = now() }
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------- purchases -----
local function maxUpgradeLevel(kind: string): number
	local upgrade = GameConfig.Upgrades[kind]
	if not upgrade or upgrade.Max == math.huge then
		return math.huge
	end
	return math.floor(upgrade.Max / upgrade.PerLevel) + 1
end

local function handleUpgrade(player: Player, data, id: string, result)
	local upgrade = GameConfig.Upgrades[id]
	if not upgrade then
		result.message = "Unknown upgrade"
		return
	end
	local level = PlayerData.UpgradeLevel(data, id)
	if level >= maxUpgradeLevel(id) then
		result.message = "Already maxed"
		return
	end
	local cost = PlayerData.UpgradeCost(id, level)
	if data.Coins < cost then
		result.message = "Not enough coins"
		return
	end
	data.Coins -= cost
	data.Upgrades[id] = level + 1
	result.success = true
end

local function handlePlane(player: Player, data, id: string, perks, result)
	local plane = GameConfig.Planes[id]
	if not plane then
		result.message = "Unknown plane"
		return
	end
	if plane.RequiresPass and not MonetizationService.OwnsPass(player, plane.RequiresPass) then
		result.message = "Requires the " .. (Monetization.Gamepasses[plane.RequiresPass]
			and Monetization.Gamepasses[plane.RequiresPass].Name or "gamepass")
		return
	end
	if data.UnlockedPlanes[id] then
		data.EquippedPlane = id
		result.success = true
		return
	end
	if PlayerData.GetLevel(data) < (plane.UnlockLevel or 0) then
		result.message = "Reach level " .. plane.UnlockLevel
		return
	end
	if data.Gems < plane.Cost then
		result.message = "Not enough gems"
		return
	end
	data.Gems -= plane.Cost
	data.UnlockedPlanes[id] = true
	data.EquippedPlane = id
	result.success = true
end

local function handleWeapon(player: Player, data, id: string, result)
	local weapon = GameConfig.Weapons[id]
	if not weapon then
		result.message = "Unknown weapon"
		return
	end
	if weapon.RequiresPass and not MonetizationService.OwnsPass(player, weapon.RequiresPass) then
		result.message = "Gamepass exclusive"
		return
	end
	if data.UnlockedWeapons[id] then
		data.EquippedWeapon = id
		result.success = true
		return
	end
	if data.Gems < weapon.Cost then
		result.message = "Not enough gems"
		return
	end
	data.Gems -= weapon.Cost
	data.UnlockedWeapons[id] = true
	data.EquippedWeapon = id
	result.success = true
end

local function handleShield(player: Player, data, id: string, result)
	local shield = GameConfig.Shields[id]
	if not shield then
		result.message = "Unknown shield"
		return
	end
	if data.UnlockedShields[id] then
		data.EquippedShield = id
		result.success = true
		return
	end
	if data.Gems < shield.Cost then
		result.message = "Not enough gems"
		return
	end
	data.Gems -= shield.Cost
	data.UnlockedShields[id] = true
	data.EquippedShield = id
	result.success = true
end

local function handleAlly(player: Player, data, id: string, perks, result)
	local ally = GameConfig.Allies[id]
	if not ally then
		result.message = "Unknown ally"
		return
	end

	local limit = PlayerData.AllyLimit(data, perks)
	for _, equipped in ipairs(data.Allies) do
		if equipped == id then
			result.message = "Already in your squad"
			return
		end
	end

	if data.UnlockedAllies[id] then
		if #data.Allies >= limit then
			result.message = "Squad is full"
			return
		end
		table.insert(data.Allies, id)
		result.success = true
		return
	end

	if #data.Allies >= limit then
		result.message = "Squad is full"
		return
	end
	if data.Gems < ally.Cost then
		result.message = "Not enough gems"
		return
	end
	data.Gems -= ally.Cost
	data.UnlockedAllies[id] = true
	table.insert(data.Allies, id)
	result.success = true
end

local function handleEgg(player: Player, data, id: string, perks, result)
	local egg = GameConfig.Eggs[id]
	if not egg then
		result.message = "Unknown egg"
		return
	end
	if data.Gems < egg.Cost then
		result.message = "Not enough gems"
		return
	end
	if #egg.Pets == 0 then
		result.message = "This egg is empty"
		return
	end

	data.Gems -= egg.Cost
	local luck = 1 + ((perks.LuckMult or 1) - 1) + (data.Upgrades.Luck - 1) * GameConfig.Upgrades.Luck.PerLevel

	-- The last entry in each egg's pool is the rare one; luck biases toward it.
	local index = math.random(1, #egg.Pets)
	if luck > 1 and math.random() < math.min(0.6, (luck - 1) * 0.35) then
		index = #egg.Pets
	end
	local petId = egg.Pets[index]
	local petName = GameConfig.Allies[petId] and GameConfig.Allies[petId].Name or petId

	data.UnlockedAllies[petId] = true
	table.insert(data.Eggs, id)
	if #data.Eggs > 50 then
		table.remove(data.Eggs, 1)
	end

	local limit = PlayerData.AllyLimit(data, perks)
	if #data.Allies < limit then
		table.insert(data.Allies, petId)
		result.message = "Hatched " .. petName .. "!"
	else
		result.message = "Hatched " .. petName .. " (squad full)"
	end
	result.success = true
end

local function handlePrestige(data, id: string, result)
	local entry = GameConfig.Prestige[id]
	if not entry then
		result.message = "Unknown prestige"
		return
	end
	local rank = math.max(0, math.floor(tonumber(data.Prestige[id]) or 0))
	if rank >= entry.Max then
		result.message = "Already maxed"
		return
	end
	local cost = GameConfig.PrestigeCost(id, rank)
	if data.Gems < cost then
		result.message = "Not enough gems"
		return
	end
	data.Gems -= cost
	data.Prestige[id] = rank + 1
	result.success = true
end

local function handlePurchase(player: Player, payload: any)
	local data = dataByPlayer[player]
	if not data or type(payload) ~= "table" then
		return
	end

	local action = payload.Action
	local id = payload.Id
	local perks = MonetizationService.GetPerks(player)
	local result = { success = false, message = "" }

	if action == "upgrade" then
		handleUpgrade(player, data, tostring(id), result)
	elseif action == "plane" then
		handlePlane(player, data, tostring(id), perks, result)
	elseif action == "weapon" then
		handleWeapon(player, data, tostring(id), result)
	elseif action == "shield" then
		handleShield(player, data, tostring(id), result)
	elseif action == "ally" then
		handleAlly(player, data, tostring(id), perks, result)
	elseif action == "egg" then
		handleEgg(player, data, tostring(id), perks, result)
	elseif action == "prestige" then
		handlePrestige(data, tostring(id), result)
	elseif action == "hangar" then
		local hangar = GameConfig.Hangars[tostring(id)]
		if hangar then
			data.EquippedHangar = tostring(id)
			result.success = true
			result.message = "Home set to " .. hangar.Name
		else
			result.message = "Unknown hangar"
		end
	elseif action == "removeAlly" then
		for index, equipped in ipairs(data.Allies) do
			if equipped == id then
				table.remove(data.Allies, index)
				result.success = true
				break
			end
		end
	else
		result.message = "Unknown action"
	end

	if result.success then
		pushSnapshot(player)
		markDirty(player)
	end

	if remotes.ShopSync then
		remotes.ShopSync:FireClient(player, result.success, result.message)
	end
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------- rebirth ----
local function handleRebirth(player: Player)
	local data = dataByPlayer[player]
	if not data then
		return
	end
	if not PlayerData.CanRebirth(data) then
		if remotes.ShopSync then
			remotes.ShopSync:FireClient(player, false, "Rebirth needs level " .. GameConfig.Rebirth.RequiredLevel)
		end
		return
	end

	data.Coins = 0
	data.Upgrades = { Damage = 1, Health = 1, Speed = 1, Magnet = 1, Luck = 1 }
	data.Rebirths += 1

	checkAchievements(player, data)
	pushSnapshot(player)
	markDirty(player)

	if remotes.ShopSync then
		remotes.ShopSync:FireClient(player, true, "Rebirth " .. data.Rebirths .. "!")
	end
	if remotes.RunEvent then
		remotes.RunEvent:FireClient(player, "Rebirth", { Rebirths = data.Rebirths })
	end
end

-- --------------------------------------------------------------- daily ----
local function handleClaimDaily(player: Player)
	local data = dataByPlayer[player]
	if not data then
		return
	end

	local day = Quests.UtcDay()
	if data.LastDailyDay == day then
		notify(player, "Already claimed today")
		return
	end

	data.DailyStreak = if data.LastDailyDay == day - 1 then math.min(365, data.DailyStreak + 1) else 1
	data.LastDailyDay = day

	local rewards = GameConfig.DailyRewards
	local entry = rewards[math.min(data.DailyStreak, #rewards)]
	data.Coins += entry.Coins or 0
	data.Gems += entry.Gems or 0

	pushSnapshot(player)
	markDirty(player)
	notify(player, string.format("Day %d reward: +%d coins, +%d gems", data.DailyStreak, entry.Coins or 0, entry.Gems or 0))
end

local function handleClaimQuest(player: Player, index: any)
	local data = dataByPlayer[player]
	if not data then
		return
	end
	PlayerData.EnsureDailyQuests(data)

	local slot = math.floor(tonumber(index) or 0)
	local entry = data.Quests.List[slot]
	if not entry then
		return
	end
	if entry.Claimed then
		notify(player, "Already claimed")
		return
	end
	local template = PlayerData.FindQuestTemplate(entry.Id)
	if not template or entry.Progress < template.Amount then
		notify(player, "Quest not complete")
		return
	end

	entry.Claimed = true
	data.Coins += template.Coins or 0
	data.Gems += template.Gems or 0

	pushSnapshot(player)
	markDirty(player)
	notify(player, string.format("Quest complete: +%d coins, +%d gems", template.Coins or 0, template.Gems or 0))
end

local function handleRedeemCode(player: Player, rawCode: any)
	local data = dataByPlayer[player]
	if not data or type(rawCode) ~= "string" then
		return
	end
	local code = string.upper(string.gsub(rawCode, "%s", ""))
	if #code == 0 or #code > 32 then
		return
	end
	local reward = Quests.Codes[code]
	if not reward then
		notify(player, "Invalid code")
		return
	end
	if data.CodesRedeemed[code] then
		notify(player, "Code already used")
		return
	end

	data.CodesRedeemed[code] = true
	data.Coins += reward.Coins or 0
	data.Gems += reward.Gems or 0

	pushSnapshot(player)
	markDirty(player)
	notify(player, string.format("Code %s: +%d coins, +%d gems", code, reward.Coins or 0, reward.Gems or 0))
end

-- --------------------------------------------------------------- joins ----
local function buildLeaderstats(player: Player, data)
	local leaderstats = Instance.new("Folder")
	leaderstats.Name = "leaderstats"

	local distance = Instance.new("IntValue")
	distance.Name = "Distance"
	distance.Value = data.BestDistance
	distance.Parent = leaderstats

	local rebirths = Instance.new("IntValue")
	rebirths.Name = "Rebirths"
	rebirths.Value = data.Rebirths
	rebirths.Parent = leaderstats

	local level = Instance.new("IntValue")
	level.Name = "Level"
	level.Value = PlayerData.GetLevel(data)
	level.Parent = leaderstats

	leaderstats.Parent = player
end

local function onPlayerAdded(player: Player)
	task.spawn(function()
		local loaded = DataService.Load(keyFor(player))
		if not player.Parent then
			return
		end

		local data = PlayerData.Sanitize(loaded)
		dataByPlayer[player] = data
		runByPlayer[player] = { active = false }
		PlayerData.EnsureDailyQuests(data)

		MonetizationService.Refresh(player)
		-- A pass that unlocks a plane/weapon also grants it permanently.
		local perks = MonetizationService.GetPerks(player)
		for planeId in pairs(perks.UnlockPlanes) do
			data.UnlockedPlanes[planeId] = true
		end
		for weaponId in pairs(perks.UnlockWeapons) do
			data.UnlockedWeapons[weaponId] = true
		end

		if not player.Parent then
			dataByPlayer[player] = nil
			return
		end

		buildLeaderstats(player, data)

		if remotes.DataLoaded then
			remotes.DataLoaded:FireClient(player, PlayerData.Snapshot(data, perks, MonetizationService.GetOwnedKeys(player)))
		end
		pushSnapshot(player)

		print(string.format("[GameService] loaded %s (level %d, %d coins)", player.Name, PlayerData.GetLevel(data), data.Coins))
	end)
end

local function onPlayerRemoving(player: Player)
	runByPlayer[player] = nil
	if dataByPlayer[player] then
		saveNow(player)
	end
	dataByPlayer[player] = nil
	dirty[player] = nil
	MonetizationService.Forget(player)
	LeaderboardService.Forget(player)
end

-- ----------------------------------------------------------------- boot ---
local function buildRemotes()
	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
	folder.Name = Remotes.Folder

	local function make(name: string)
		local remote = Instance.new("RemoteEvent")
		remote.Name = name
		remote.Parent = folder
		return remote
	end

	remotes.RunResult = make(Remotes.RunResult)
	remotes.Purchase = make(Remotes.Purchase)
	remotes.Rebirth = make(Remotes.Rebirth)
	remotes.RequestStart = make(Remotes.RequestStart)
	remotes.RequestData = make(Remotes.RequestData)
	remotes.PromptPass = make(Remotes.PromptPass)
	remotes.PromptProduct = make(Remotes.PromptProduct)
	remotes.ClaimQuest = make(Remotes.ClaimQuest)
	remotes.ClaimDaily = make(Remotes.ClaimDaily)
	remotes.RedeemCode = make(Remotes.RedeemCode)
	remotes.RequestLeaderboard = make(Remotes.RequestLeaderboard)
	remotes.UseRevive = make(Remotes.UseRevive)
	remotes.DataLoaded = make(Remotes.DataLoaded)
	remotes.DataUpdate = make(Remotes.DataUpdate)
	remotes.RunEvent = make(Remotes.RunEvent)
	remotes.ShopSync = make(Remotes.ShopSync)
	remotes.Notify = make(Remotes.Notify)
	remotes.LeaderboardData = make(Remotes.LeaderboardData)

	folder.Parent = ReplicatedStorage
end

local function handleProduct(player: Player, key: string, product): boolean
	local data = dataByPlayer[player]
	if not data then
		return false
	end

	if product.Coins and product.Coins > 0 then
		data.Coins += product.Coins
	end
	if product.Gems and product.Gems > 0 then
		data.Gems += product.Gems
	end
	if product.Revive then
		data.ReviveTokens = (data.ReviveTokens or 0) + 1
	end

	pushSnapshot(player)
	markDirty(player)
	notify(player, product.Name .. " delivered!")
	return true
end

function GameService.Start()
	buildRemotes()

	MonetizationService.SetProductHandler(handleProduct)
	MonetizationService.OnPerksChanged = function(player)
		pushSnapshot(player)
	end
	MonetizationService.OnPassPurchased = function(player, _key)
		local data = dataByPlayer[player]
		if not data then
			return
		end
		local perks = MonetizationService.GetPerks(player)
		for planeId in pairs(perks.UnlockPlanes) do
			data.UnlockedPlanes[planeId] = true
		end
		for weaponId in pairs(perks.UnlockWeapons) do
			data.UnlockedWeapons[weaponId] = true
		end
		pushSnapshot(player)
		markDirty(player)
	end
	MonetizationService.Start()

	remotes.RunResult.OnServerEvent:Connect(handleRunResult)
	remotes.Purchase.OnServerEvent:Connect(handlePurchase)
	remotes.Rebirth.OnServerEvent:Connect(handleRebirth)
	remotes.RequestStart.OnServerEvent:Connect(handleRequestStart)
	-- The client connects its handlers after the server may have already sent
	-- the join snapshot, so it asks again once it is ready.
	remotes.RequestData.OnServerEvent:Connect(function(player)
		local data = dataByPlayer[player]
		if not data then
			return
		end
		if remotes.DataLoaded then
			remotes.DataLoaded:FireClient(
				player,
				PlayerData.Snapshot(data, MonetizationService.GetPerks(player), MonetizationService.GetOwnedKeys(player))
			)
		end
	end)
	remotes.PromptPass.OnServerEvent:Connect(function(player, key)
		if type(key) ~= "string" then
			return
		end
		local ok, message = MonetizationService.PromptPass(player, key)
		if not ok then
			notify(player, message)
		end
	end)
	remotes.PromptProduct.OnServerEvent:Connect(function(player, key)
		if type(key) ~= "string" then
			return
		end
		local ok, message = MonetizationService.PromptProduct(player, key)
		if not ok then
			notify(player, message)
		end
	end)
	remotes.ClaimQuest.OnServerEvent:Connect(handleClaimQuest)
	remotes.ClaimDaily.OnServerEvent:Connect(handleClaimDaily)
	remotes.RedeemCode.OnServerEvent:Connect(handleRedeemCode)
	remotes.RequestLeaderboard.OnServerEvent:Connect(function(player)
		local data = dataByPlayer[player]
		if remotes.LeaderboardData then
			remotes.LeaderboardData:FireClient(player, {
				Entries = LeaderboardService.GetTop(10),
				Personal = if data then data.BestDistance else 0,
			})
		end
	end)
	remotes.UseRevive.OnServerEvent:Connect(function(player)
		local data = dataByPlayer[player]
		if not data then
			return
		end
		if (data.ReviveTokens or 0) <= 0 then
			return
		end
		data.ReviveTokens -= 1
		pushSnapshot(player)
		markDirty(player)
	end)

	Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(onPlayerAdded)
	Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(onPlayerRemoving)
	for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		onPlayerAdded(player)
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		while true do
			task.wait(SAVE_INTERVAL)
			for player in pairs(dirty) do
				if player.Parent and dataByPlayer[player] then
					saveNow(player)
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	game:BindToClose(function()
		for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			local data = dataByPlayer[player]
			if data then
				data.LastSeen = os.time()
				DataService.Save(keyFor(player), data)
			end
		end
	end)

	print("[GameService] Started")
end

return GameService

FILE: src/server/init.server.luau

--!strict
-- Server bootstrap for Paper Wings.
-- This script is the "Server" container; its siblings are the server modules.

local GameService = require(script:WaitForChild("GameService"))

GameService.Start()

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/server/LeaderboardService.luau
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
--!nonstrict
-- LeaderboardService: global "furthest flight" rankings via OrderedDataStore.
--
-- Writes are throttled per player and only happen when a personal best improves.
-- The top list is cached for a minute so a busy server does not hammer the
-- DataStore. In Studio (no API access) it falls back to session-only entries so
-- the UI still has something real to show.

local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local DataService = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("DataService"))

local LeaderboardService = {}

local STORE_NAME = "PaperWings_Distance_v2"
local CACHE_SECONDS = 60
local MAX_ENTRIES = 25

local submitted: { [Player]: number } = {}
local sessionBest: { [number]: number } = {}
local nameCache: { [number]: string } = {}
local cache = { entries = {}, at = 0 }

local function ordered()
	return DataService.GetOrderedStore(STORE_NAME)
end

local function nameFor(userId: number): string
	local cached = nameCache[userId]
	if cached then
		return cached
	end
	local ok, name = pcall(function()
		return Players:GetNameFromUserIdAsync(userId)
	end)
	local resolved = if ok and type(name) == "string" then name else "Pilot " .. tostring(userId)
	nameCache[userId] = resolved
	return resolved
end

-- Returns the entries for this session's fallback board (Studio only).
local function sessionEntries(limit: number)
	local entries = {}
	for userId, distance in pairs(sessionBest) do
		table.insert(entries, { UserId = userId, Distance = distance })
	end
	table.sort(entries, function(a, b)
		return a.Distance > b.Distance
	end)
	for index, entry in ipairs(entries) do
		entry.Rank = index
		entry.Name = nameFor(entry.UserId)
		if index >= limit then
			break
		end
	end
	return entries
end

function LeaderboardService.Submit(player: Player, distance: number)
	if distance <= 0 then
		return
	end
	local previous = submitted[player]
	if previous and distance <= previous then
		return
	end
	submitted[player] = distance
	sessionBest[player.UserId] = math.max(sessionBest[player.UserId] or 0, distance)
	cache.at = 0

	local store = ordered()
	if not store then
		return
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		local ok, err = pcall(function()
			store:SetAsync(tostring(player.UserId), math.floor(distance))
		end)
		if not ok then
			warn("[Leaderboard] submit failed:", err)
		end
	end)
end

function LeaderboardService.GetTop(limit: number)
	limit = math.clamp(limit or 10, 1, MAX_ENTRIES)
	if os.time() - cache.at < CACHE_SECONDS and #cache.entries > 0 then
		return cache.entries
	end

	local entries = {}
	local store = ordered()

	if store then
		local ok, pages = pcall(function()
			return store:GetSortedAsync(false, limit)
		end)
		if ok and pages then
			local pageOk, page = pcall(function()
				return pages:GetCurrentPage()
			end)
			if pageOk and page then
				for rank, item in ipairs(page) do
					local userId = tonumber(item.key)
					if userId then
						table.insert(entries, {
							Rank = rank,
							UserId = userId,
							Name = nameFor(userId),
							Distance = item.value,
						})
					end
				end
			end
		else
			warn("[Leaderboard] read failed:", pages)
		end
	end

	-- Studio / offline: fall back to this session's flights.
	if #entries == 0 then
		entries = sessionEntries(limit)
	end

	cache.entries = entries
	cache.at = os.time()
	return entries
end

function LeaderboardService.Forget(player: Player)
	submitted[player] = nil
end

return LeaderboardService

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/server/MonetizationService.luau
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
--!nonstrict
-- MonetizationService: gamepass ownership, perk aggregation and product receipts.
--
-- Ownership is cached per player and refreshed on join and after a purchase.
-- In Studio (or wherever the Marketplace API is unavailable) the pcall simply
-- reports "not owned", so nothing errors and the game stays playable.

local MarketplaceService = game:GetService("MarketplaceService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")
local Monetization = require(Shared:WaitForChild("Monetization"))

local MonetizationService = {}

local perksByPlayer: { [Player]: any } = {}
local ownedByPlayer: { [Player]: { [string]: boolean } } = {}
local testOwned: { [Player]: { [string]: boolean } } = {}

-- Set by GameService before Start(); receives (player, productKey, product).
local productHandler: ((Player, string, any) -> boolean)? = nil

function MonetizationService.SetProductHandler(handler)
	productHandler = handler
end

function MonetizationService.GetPerks(player: Player)
	return perksByPlayer[player] or Monetization.EmptyPerks()
end

-- Keys of the passes this player owns, for the store UI.
function MonetizationService.GetOwnedKeys(player: Player): { [string]: boolean }
	return ownedByPlayer[player] or {}
end

function MonetizationService.OwnsPass(player: Player, key: string): boolean
	local owned = ownedByPlayer[player]
	return owned ~= nil and owned[key] == true
end

local function readOwnership(player: Player)
	local owned = {}
	for key, pass in pairs(Monetization.Gamepasses) do
		local forced = testOwned[player]
		if forced and forced[key] then
			owned[key] = true
		elseif Monetization.IsConfigured(pass.Id) then
			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return MarketplaceService:UserOwnsGamePassAsync(player.UserId, pass.Id)
			end)
			if ok and result then
				owned[key] = true
			end
		end
	end
	return owned
end

-- Returns true when the aggregated perks changed.
function MonetizationService.Refresh(player: Player): boolean
	local owned = readOwnership(player)
	ownedByPlayer[player] = owned

	local perks = Monetization.Aggregate(owned)

	local previous = perksByPlayer[player]
	perksByPlayer[player] = perks
	if not previous then
		return true
	end
	for _, field in ipairs({ "CoinMult", "GemMult", "DamageMult", "HealthMult", "SpeedMult", "MagnetMult", "LuckMult", "Revives", "ExtraAllySlots", "ExtraProjectiles", "AutoCollect", "Vip" }) do
		if previous[field] ~= perks[field] then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

function MonetizationService.GrantTestPass(player: Player, key: string)
	if not Monetization.Gamepasses[key] then
		return false
	end
	testOwned[player] = testOwned[player] or {}
	testOwned[player][key] = true
	MonetizationService.Refresh(player)
	return true
end

-- Client-requested prompts. Failures (unconfigured pass, API blocked) are
-- reported to the client as a toast rather than thrown.
function MonetizationService.PromptPass(player: Player, key: string): (boolean, string)
	local pass = Monetization.Gamepasses[key]
	if not pass then
		return false, "Unknown pass"
	end
	if not Monetization.IsConfigured(pass.Id) then
		return false, pass.Name .. " is not on sale yet"
	end
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		MarketplaceService:PromptGamePassPurchase(player, pass.Id)
	end)
	if not ok then
		warn("[Monetization] prompt failed:", err)
		return false, "Could not open the purchase window"
	end
	return true, ""
end

function MonetizationService.PromptProduct(player: Player, key: string): (boolean, string)
	local product = Monetization.Products[key]
	if not product then
		return false, "Unknown product"
	end
	if not Monetization.IsConfigured(product.Id) then
		return false, product.Name .. " is not on sale yet"
	end
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		MarketplaceService:PromptProductPurchase(player, product.Id)
	end)
	if not ok then
		warn("[Monetization] prompt failed:", err)
		return false, "Could not open the purchase window"
	end
	return true, ""
end

local function processReceipt(receiptInfo)
	local player = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(receiptInfo.PlayerId)
	if not player then
		return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.NotProcessedYet
	end

	local key, product = Monetization.ProductByAssetId(receiptInfo.ProductId)
	if not product then
		warn("[Monetization] unknown product id:", receiptInfo.ProductId)
		return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.NotProcessedYet
	end

	if not productHandler then
		return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.NotProcessedYet
	end

	local ok, granted = pcall(function()
		return productHandler(player, key, product)
	end)
	if ok and granted then
		return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.PurchaseGranted
	end
	warn("[Monetization] product grant failed for", player.Name, key, granted)
	return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.NotProcessedYet
end

function MonetizationService.Start()
	MarketplaceService.ProcessReceipt = function(receiptInfo)
		-- A receipt must never throw; a thrown error makes Roblox retry forever.
		local ok, decision = pcall(processReceipt, receiptInfo)
		if ok then
			return decision
		end
		warn("[Monetization] ProcessReceipt error:", decision)
		return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.NotProcessedYet
	end

	MarketplaceService.PromptGamePassPurchaseFinished:Connect(function(player, passId, wasPurchased)
		if not wasPurchased then
			return
		end
		if MonetizationService.Refresh(player) then
			MonetizationService.OnPerksChanged(player)
		end
		local key = nil
		for passKey, pass in pairs(Monetization.Gamepasses) do
			if pass.Id == passId then
				key = passKey
				break
			end
		end
		if key then
			MonetizationService.OnPassPurchased(player, key)
		end
	end)
end

-- Assigned by GameService to push state after a change.
MonetizationService.OnPerksChanged = function(_player: Player) end
MonetizationService.OnPassPurchased = function(_player: Player, _key: string) end

function MonetizationService.Forget(player: Player)
	perksByPlayer[player] = nil
	ownedByPlayer[player] = nil
	testOwned[player] = nil
end

return MonetizationService

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/server/PlayerData.luau
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
--!nonstrict
-- PlayerData: default schema, derived values, validation and the client snapshot.
--
-- Every value that crosses the network is produced by `PlayerData.Snapshot`, so
-- the client always receives a complete picture and never has to merge partial
-- updates (a partial payload used to blank out `UnlockedPlanes` and friends).

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")

local GameConfig = require(Shared:WaitForChild("GameConfig"))
local Monetization = require(Shared:WaitForChild("Monetization"))
local Quests = require(Shared:WaitForChild("Quests"))
local Stats = require(Shared:WaitForChild("Stats"))

local PlayerData = {}

local SAVE_VERSION = 2

function PlayerData.Default()
	return {
		Version = SAVE_VERSION,
		Coins = 0,
		Gems = 0,
		Upgrades = { Damage = 1, Health = 1, Speed = 1, Magnet = 1, Luck = 1 },
		UnlockedPlanes = { Classic = true },
		UnlockedWeapons = { Dart = true },
		UnlockedAllies = {},
		UnlockedShields = { None = true },
		Eggs = {},
		EquippedPlane = "Classic",
		EquippedCharacter = "Pilot",
		EquippedHangar = "Cardboard",
		EquippedShield = "None",
		EquippedWeapon = "Dart",
		Allies = {},
		Rebirths = 0,
		Prestige = { HeadStart = 0, Coins = 0, Armor = 0, Gems = 0 },
		BestDistance = 0,
		BestKills = 0,
		TotalKills = 0,
		TotalDistance = 0,
		TotalCoinsEarned = 0,
		TotalGemsEarned = 0,
		Runs = 0,
		BossesBeaten = {},
		DailyStreak = 0,
		LastDailyDay = 0,
		ReviveTokens = 0,
		Quests = { Day = 0, List = {} },
		Achievements = {},
		CodesRedeemed = {},
		CreatedAt = os.time(),
		LastSeen = os.time(),
	}
end

function PlayerData.UpgradeLevel(data, kind: string): number
	local v = data.Upgrades and data.Upgrades[kind]
	if type(v) ~= "number" then
		return 1
	end
	return math.max(1, math.floor(v))
end

function PlayerData.GetLevel(data): number
	local totalDistance = tonumber(data.TotalDistance) or 0
	local rebirths = tonumber(data.Rebirths) or 0
	return 1 + math.floor(totalDistance / 150) + rebirths * 4
end

function PlayerData.GetStats(data, perks)
	return Stats.Compute({
		Upgrades = data.Upgrades,
		EquippedPlane = data.EquippedPlane,
		EquippedWeapon = data.EquippedWeapon,
		EquippedShield = data.EquippedShield,
		Allies = data.Allies,
		Rebirths = data.Rebirths,
		TotalDistance = data.TotalDistance,
		Perks = perks,
	})
end

function PlayerData.UpgradeCost(kind: string, level: number): number
	return GameConfig.UpgradeCost(kind, level)
end

function PlayerData.AllyLimit(data, perks): number
	local extra = (perks and perks.ExtraAllySlots) or 0
	return math.min(GameConfig.MaxAllySlots, GameConfig.MaxAllies + extra)
end

function PlayerData.CanRebirth(data): boolean
	if PlayerData.GetLevel(data) >= GameConfig.Rebirth.RequiredLevel then
		return true
	end
	local beaten = 0
	if type(data.BossesBeaten) == "table" then
		for _ in pairs(data.BossesBeaten) do
			beaten += 1
		end
	end
	return beaten >= GameConfig.Rebirth.AltBossCount
end

-- Coerce unknown/garbage saved values into a safe schema.
function PlayerData.Sanitize(data)
	local clean = PlayerData.Default()
	if type(data) ~= "table" then
		return clean
	end

	local function int(value, minimum: number?, maximum: number?): number
		local n = math.floor(tonumber(value) or 0)
		if minimum then
			n = math.max(minimum, n)
		end
		if maximum then
			n = math.min(maximum, n)
		end
		return n
	end

	clean.Coins = int(data.Coins, 0, 1e12)
	clean.Gems = int(data.Gems, 0, 1e9)
	clean.Rebirths = int(data.Rebirths, 0, 500)
	clean.BestDistance = int(data.BestDistance, 0, 1e7)
	clean.BestKills = int(data.BestKills, 0, 1e6)
	clean.TotalKills = int(data.TotalKills, 0, 1e8)
	clean.TotalDistance = int(data.TotalDistance, 0, 1e9)
	clean.TotalCoinsEarned = int(data.TotalCoinsEarned, 0, 1e12)
	clean.TotalGemsEarned = int(data.TotalGemsEarned, 0, 1e9)
	clean.Runs = int(data.Runs, 0, 1e7)
	clean.DailyStreak = int(data.DailyStreak, 0, 365)
	clean.LastDailyDay = int(data.LastDailyDay, 0, 1e9)
	clean.ReviveTokens = int(data.ReviveTokens, 0, 99)
	clean.CreatedAt = int(data.CreatedAt, 0, os.time() + 86400)
	clean.LastSeen = int(data.LastSeen, 0, os.time() + 86400)

	if type(data.Upgrades) == "table" then
		for kind in pairs(GameConfig.Upgrades) do
			local v = tonumber(data.Upgrades[kind])
			if v then
				clean.Upgrades[kind] = int(v, 1, 1e6)
			end
		end
	end

	local function unlocked(source, catalogue, into, always)
		if type(source) == "table" then
			for id in pairs(source) do
				if catalogue[id] then
					into[id] = true
				end
			end
		end
		if always then
			into[always] = true
		end
	end

	unlocked(data.UnlockedPlanes, GameConfig.Planes, clean.UnlockedPlanes, "Classic")
	unlocked(data.UnlockedWeapons, GameConfig.Weapons, clean.UnlockedWeapons, "Dart")
	unlocked(data.UnlockedAllies, GameConfig.Allies, clean.UnlockedAllies)
	unlocked(data.UnlockedShields, GameConfig.Shields, clean.UnlockedShields, "None")
	unlocked(data.BossesBeaten, { [""] = true }, clean.BossesBeaten)

	-- Boss keys are zone ids, not catalogue keys, so validate them directly.
	if type(data.BossesBeaten) == "table" then
		table.clear(clean.BossesBeaten)
		for zoneId in pairs(data.BossesBeaten) do
			if GameConfig.GetZoneById(zoneId) then
				clean.BossesBeaten[zoneId] = true
			end
		end
	end

	if type(data.Eggs) == "table" then
		for _, eggId in ipairs(data.Eggs) do
			if GameConfig.Eggs[eggId] and #clean.Eggs < 50 then
				table.insert(clean.Eggs, eggId)
			end
		end
	end

	local allyLimit = GameConfig.MaxAllySlots
	if type(data.Allies) == "table" then
		for _, id in ipairs(data.Allies) do
			if GameConfig.Allies[id] and #clean.Allies < allyLimit then
				table.insert(clean.Allies, id)
				clean.UnlockedAllies[id] = true
			end
		end
	end

	if GameConfig.Characters[data.EquippedCharacter] then
		clean.EquippedCharacter = data.EquippedCharacter
	end
	if GameConfig.Hangars[data.EquippedHangar] then
		clean.EquippedHangar = data.EquippedHangar
	end
	if GameConfig.Shields[data.EquippedShield] then
		clean.EquippedShield = data.EquippedShield
	end
	if GameConfig.Planes[data.EquippedPlane] and clean.UnlockedPlanes[data.EquippedPlane] then
		clean.EquippedPlane = data.EquippedPlane
	end
	if GameConfig.Weapons[data.EquippedWeapon] and clean.UnlockedWeapons[data.EquippedWeapon] then
		clean.EquippedWeapon = data.EquippedWeapon
	end

	if type(data.Achievements) == "table" then
		for id in pairs(data.Achievements) do
			if id == "list" or type(id) == "string" then
				clean.Achievements[id] = true
			end
		end
	end
	if type(data.CodesRedeemed) == "table" then
		for code in pairs(data.CodesRedeemed) do
			if type(code) == "string" and #clean.CodesRedeemed < 200 then
				clean.CodesRedeemed[string.upper(code)] = true
			end
		end
	end

	if type(data.Prestige) == "table" then
		for kind, entry in pairs(GameConfig.Prestige) do
			local value = tonumber(data.Prestige[kind])
			if value then
				clean.Prestige[kind] = int(value, 0, entry.Max)
			end
		end
	end

	if type(data.Quests) == "table" and type(data.Quests.List) == "table" then
		clean.Quests.Day = int(data.Quests.Day, 0, 1e9)
		for _, entry in ipairs(data.Quests.List) do
			if type(entry) == "table" and type(entry.Id) == "string" and #clean.Quests.List < 12 then
				table.insert(clean.Quests.List, {
					Id = entry.Id,
					Progress = int(entry.Progress, 0, 1e9),
					Claimed = entry.Claimed == true,
				})
			end
		end
	end

	return clean
end

-- Build (or refresh) today's daily quest list. Returns true if it changed.
function PlayerData.EnsureDailyQuests(data): boolean
	local day = Quests.UtcDay()
	if data.Quests.Day == day and #data.Quests.List > 0 then
		return false
	end
	local rotation = Quests.RotationForDay(day)
	local list = {}
	for _, template in ipairs(rotation) do
		table.insert(list, { Id = template.Id, Progress = 0, Claimed = false })
	end
	data.Quests = { Day = day, List = list }
	return true
end

function PlayerData.FindQuestTemplate(id: string)
	for _, template in ipairs(Quests.Templates) do
		if template.Id == id then
			return template
		end
	end
	return nil
end

-- The single payload the client receives, both on load and on every update.
function PlayerData.Snapshot(data, perks, ownedPasses)
	return {
		Coins = data.Coins,
		Gems = data.Gems,
		Upgrades = data.Upgrades,
		UnlockedPlanes = data.UnlockedPlanes,
		UnlockedWeapons = data.UnlockedWeapons,
		UnlockedAllies = data.UnlockedAllies,
		UnlockedShields = data.UnlockedShields,
		Eggs = data.Eggs,
		EquippedPlane = data.EquippedPlane,
		EquippedWeapon = data.EquippedWeapon,
		EquippedShield = data.EquippedShield,
		EquippedCharacter = data.EquippedCharacter,
		EquippedHangar = data.EquippedHangar,
		Allies = data.Allies,
		Rebirths = data.Rebirths,
		Prestige = data.Prestige,
		BestDistance = data.BestDistance,
		BestKills = data.BestKills,
		TotalKills = data.TotalKills,
		TotalDistance = data.TotalDistance,
		TotalCoinsEarned = data.TotalCoinsEarned,
		TotalGemsEarned = data.TotalGemsEarned,
		Runs = data.Runs,
		BossesBeaten = data.BossesBeaten,
		DailyStreak = data.DailyStreak,
		LastDailyDay = data.LastDailyDay,
		ReviveTokens = data.ReviveTokens or 0,
		Quests = data.Quests,
		Achievements = data.Achievements,
		CodesRedeemed = data.CodesRedeemed,
		Level = PlayerData.GetLevel(data),
		CanRebirth = PlayerData.CanRebirth(data),
		Day = Quests.UtcDay(),
		Perks = perks or Monetization.EmptyPerks(),
		OwnedPasses = ownedPasses or {},
		Event = GameConfig.GetActiveEvent(),
		AllyLimit = PlayerData.AllyLimit(data, perks),
	}
end

return PlayerData

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/shared/GameConfig.luau
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- ============================================================================
--!nonstrict
-- GameConfig: every static number, table and tunable in Paper Wings.
--
-- This file is data only. Anything that decides whether a run is legal lives
-- on the server (GameService); anything cosmetic reads from here.
--
-- Naming note: `Mult` on shields is a *damage divisor* (higher = tankier).
-- `CoinMult` on planes/passes is a *payout multiplier*.

local GameConfig = {}

-- Distance / movement -------------------------------------------------------
GameConfig.MetersPerStud = 0.25 -- 4 studs == 1 m
GameConfig.Corridor = {
	HalfWidth = 26,
	MinHeight = 8, -- enemy/coin lower spawn band; aircraft can land below this
	MaxHeight = 44,
	GroundY = 0,
	PlaneGroundClearance = 1.6,
}

GameConfig.BaseSpeed = 56
GameConfig.BaseHealth = 100
GameConfig.BaseDamage = 10

-- Enemy scaling --------------------------------------------------------------
-- The design doc's "+8% per 100 m" is exponential: by 10 km it multiplies
-- health by ~2,200x, which makes every zone past the third impossible. These
-- curves keep the same early feel but stay solvable: health is ~20x at 10 km
-- and ~45x at 20 km, while damage grows far more slowly so runs stay survivable.
GameConfig.EnemyHealthScalePer100m = 0.08 -- kept for tooling/reference
GameConfig.EnemyDamageScalePer100m = 0.04

function GameConfig.EnemyHealthMult(distance: number): number
	local units = math.max(0, distance) / 1000
	-- Linear-in-kilometres. Player damage grows ~5x over 40 upgrade levels and
	-- weapons add 1-2.4x on top, so health must not outrun that: at 20 km this
	-- is 21x, which a well-upgraded player clears in ~2-4 s per enemy.
	return 1 + units
end

function GameConfig.EnemyDamageMult(distance: number): number
	local units = math.max(0, distance) / 2000
	return (1 + units) ^ 0.85
end

GameConfig.FireRange = 62
GameConfig.FireInterval = 0.35
GameConfig.CoinPickupRadius = 8

-- Server-authoritative reward rates, per metre of distance. Zone multipliers
-- (below) make deeper runs worth far more than farming the first zone.
GameConfig.CoinPerMeter = 0.9
GameConfig.GemPerMeter = 0.006
GameConfig.KillCoinFlat = 6

-- Combo ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Killing enemies in quick succession builds a payout multiplier. It decays
-- back to 1x when you stop killing, so it rewards aggression without being a
-- permanent free multiplier.
GameConfig.Combo = {
	Window = 2.6, -- seconds since the last kill before the chain resets
	BonusPerKill = 0.04, -- +4% coins per chain step
	MaxBonus = 1.0, -- capped at +100%
	MaxSteps = 25,
}

-- Boost (speed burst) -------------------------------------------------------
GameConfig.Boost = {
	ChargeMax = 100,
	ChargePerKill = 9,
	DrainPerSecond = 36,
	SpeedMult = 1.85,
	MinChargeToStart = 25,
}

-- Zones ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- `Style` selects the scenery recipe in WorldBuilder. `Enemies` is the spawn

-- Upgrades ------------------------------------------------------------------
-- cost(level) = BaseCost * Growth ^ (level - 1)
GameConfig.Upgrades = {
	Damage = { Name = "Damage", Desc = "More damage per shot", BaseCost = 100, Growth = 1.22, PerLevel = 0.12, Max = math.huge },
	Health = { Name = "Health", Desc = "More max health", BaseCost = 150, Growth = 1.24, PerLevel = 0.10, Max = math.huge },
	Speed = { Name = "Speed", Desc = "Faster flight", BaseCost = 200, Growth = 1.28, PerLevel = 0.03, Max = 0.60 },
	Magnet = { Name = "Magnet", Desc = "Bigger coin pickup range", BaseCost = 250, Growth = 1.30, PerLevel = 0.08, Max = 2.50 },
	Luck = { Name = "Luck", Desc = "Better gem and egg drops", BaseCost = 300, Growth = 1.35, PerLevel = 0.05, Max = 1.00 },
	FireRate = { Name = "Fire Rate", Desc = "Shoot more frequently while holding fire", BaseCost = 140, Growth = 1.28, PerLevel = 0.08, Max = 2.40 },
}
GameConfig.UpgradeOrder = { "Damage", "Health", "Speed", "Magnet", "Luck", "FireRate" }

-- Prestige -------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Permanent, gem-priced boosts. Unlike upgrades these survive rebirth, so they
-- are the long-term sink for gems once the collection is filled.
GameConfig.Prestige = {
	HeadStart = {
		Name = "Head Start",
		Desc = "+1 starting level in every upgrade",
		BaseCost = 50,
		Growth = 1.6,
		Max = 5,
	},
	Coins = {
		Name = "Coin Training",
		Desc = "+5% coins from every flight",
		BaseCost = 80,
		Growth = 1.5,
		Max = 10,
	},
	Armor = {
		Name = "Armor Plating",
		Desc = "+5% max health",
		BaseCost = 80,
		Growth = 1.5,
		Max = 10,
	},
	Gems = {
		Name = "Salvage",
		Desc = "+5% gems from every flight",
		BaseCost = 100,
		Growth = 1.6,
		Max = 10,
	},
}
GameConfig.PrestigeOrder = { "HeadStart", "Coins", "Armor", "Gems" }

function GameConfig.PrestigeCost(kind: string, rank: number): number
	local entry = GameConfig.Prestige[kind]
	if not entry or rank >= entry.Max then
		return math.huge
	end
	return math.floor(entry.BaseCost * entry.Growth ^ math.max(0, rank) + 0.5)
end

	{ Coins = 1400, Gems = 3 },
	{ Coins = 2000, Gems = 5 },
	{ Coins = 3000, Gems = 8 },
	{ Coins = 5000, Gems = 15 },
}

-- Helpers -------------------------------------------------------------------
function GameConfig.GetZoneForDistance(distance: number)
	for _, zone in ipairs(GameConfig.Zones) do
		if distance >= zone.Min and distance < zone.Max then
			return zone
		end
	end
	return GameConfig.Zones[#GameConfig.Zones]
end

function GameConfig.GetZoneIndex(distance: number): number
	for i, zone in ipairs(GameConfig.Zones) do
		if distance >= zone.Min and distance < zone.Max then
			return i
		end
	end
	return #GameConfig.Zones
end

function GameConfig.GetZoneById(id: string)
	for _, zone in ipairs(GameConfig.Zones) do
		if zone.Id == id then
			return zone
		end
	end
	return nil
end

function GameConfig.GetBossForZone(zoneId: string)
	for _, boss in ipairs(GameConfig.Bosses) do
		if boss.Zone == zoneId then
			return boss
		end
	end
	return nil
end

-- Live-ops events ------------------------------------------------------------
-- Day-of-week bonuses give a reason to log in on a specific day. `Days` uses
-- os.date's wday: 1 = Sunday ... 7 = Saturday, in UTC.
GameConfig.Events = {
	{
		Id = "WeekendRush",
		Name = "WEEKEND RUSH",
		Desc = "+50% coins from every flight",
		CoinMult = 1.5,
		Days = { 1, 7 },
	},
	{
		Id = "MidweekGems",
		Name = "MIDWEEK GEMS",
		Desc = "+50% gems from every flight",
		GemMult = 1.5,
		Days = { 4 },
	},
}

function GameConfig.GetActiveEvent(timestamp: number?)
	local ok, date = pcall(function()
		return os.date("!*t", timestamp or os.time())
	end)
	if not ok or type(date) ~= "table" then
		return nil
	end
	for _, event in ipairs(GameConfig.Events) do
		for _, day in ipairs(event.Days) do
			if day == date.wday then
				return event
			end
		end
	end
	return nil
end

function GameConfig.UpgradeCost(kind: string, level: number): number
	local u = GameConfig.Upgrades[kind]
	if not u then
		return math.huge
	end
	return math.floor(u.BaseCost * (u.Growth ^ math.max(0, level - 1)) + 0.5)
end

function GameConfig.PlaneStats(planeId: string, upgradeLevels: { [string]: number })
	local plane = GameConfig.Planes[planeId] or GameConfig.Planes.Classic
	local dmgLevel = math.max(1, upgradeLevels.Damage or 1)
	local hpLevel = math.max(1, upgradeLevels.Health or 1)
	local spLevel = math.max(1, upgradeLevels.Speed or 1)
	local magLevel = math.max(1, upgradeLevels.Magnet or 1)
	local luckLevel = math.max(1, upgradeLevels.Luck or 1)
	local fireRateLevel = math.max(1, upgradeLevels.FireRate or 1)

	local dmgMult = 1 + (dmgLevel - 1) * GameConfig.Upgrades.Damage.PerLevel
	local hpMult = 1 + (hpLevel - 1) * GameConfig.Upgrades.Health.PerLevel
	local spMult = 1 + (spLevel - 1) * GameConfig.Upgrades.Speed.PerLevel
	local magMult = 1 + (magLevel - 1) * GameConfig.Upgrades.Magnet.PerLevel
	local luckMult = 1 + (luckLevel - 1) * GameConfig.Upgrades.Luck.PerLevel
	local fireRateMult = 1 + (fireRateLevel - 1) * GameConfig.Upgrades.FireRate.PerLevel
	fireRateMult = math.min(fireRateMult, 1 + GameConfig.Upgrades.FireRate.Max)

	return {
		Damage = GameConfig.BaseDamage * plane.DamageMult * dmgMult,
		MaxHealth = GameConfig.BaseHealth * plane.HealthMult * hpMult,
		Speed = GameConfig.BaseSpeed * plane.SpeedMult * math.min(spMult, 1 + GameConfig.Upgrades.Speed.Max),
		CoinMult = plane.CoinMult or 1,
		MagnetRadius = GameConfig.CoinPickupRadius * math.min(magMult, 1 + GameConfig.Upgrades.Magnet.Max),
		Luck = math.min(luckMult, 1 + GameConfig.Upgrades.Luck.Max),
		FireRateMultiplier = fireRateMult,
	}
end

return GameConfig

-- Sync probe marker: 2026-10-22 v2
return function()
	print("Hello, world!")
end

--!nonstrict
-- Monetization: gamepass and developer-product catalogue.
--
-- `Id` values are placeholders (0). Replace them with the real asset ids from
-- the Creator Dashboard once the passes/products exist; anything with Id == 0
-- is treated as "not configured": the server ignores it and the client shows an
-- "unavailable" label instead of prompting a purchase that cannot succeed.
--
-- Perk aggregation is multiplicative for multipliers and additive for counts,
-- so passes stack predictably and never fight each other.

-- Empty perk bag. Every field is present so callers never need nil checks.
function Monetization.EmptyPerks()
	return {
		CoinMult = 1,
		GemMult = 1,
		DamageMult = 1,
		HealthMult = 1,
		SpeedMult = 1,
		MagnetMult = 1,
		LuckMult = 1,
		Revives = 0,
		ExtraAllySlots = 0,
		ExtraProjectiles = 0,
		AutoCollect = false,
		Vip = false,
		UnlockPlanes = {},
		UnlockWeapons = {},
	}
end

-- `owned` is a set of gamepass keys: { GoldenWings = true, ... }
function Monetization.Aggregate(owned)
	local perks = Monetization.EmptyPerks()
	for key, pass in pairs(Monetization.Gamepasses) do
		if owned[key] then
			local p = pass.Perks or {}
			perks.CoinMult *= p.CoinMult or 1
			perks.GemMult *= p.GemMult or 1
			perks.DamageMult *= p.DamageMult or 1
			perks.HealthMult *= p.HealthMult or 1
			perks.SpeedMult *= p.SpeedMult or 1
			perks.MagnetMult *= p.MagnetMult or 1
			perks.LuckMult *= p.LuckMult or 1
			perks.Revives += p.Revives or 0
			perks.ExtraAllySlots += p.ExtraAllySlots or 0
			perks.ExtraProjectiles += p.ExtraProjectiles or 0
			perks.AutoCollect = perks.AutoCollect or p.AutoCollect or false
			perks.Vip = perks.Vip or p.Vip or false
			for _, planeId in ipairs(p.UnlockPlanes or {}) do
				perks.UnlockPlanes[planeId] = true
			end
			for _, weaponId in ipairs(p.UnlockWeapons or {}) do
				perks.UnlockWeapons[weaponId] = true
			end
		end
	end
	return perks
end

function Monetization.GetGamepass(key: string)
	return Monetization.Gamepasses[key]
end

function Monetization.PassProductId(key: string)
	local pass = Monetization.Gamepasses[key]
	return pass and pass.Id or 0
end

function Monetization.ProductByAssetId(assetId: number)
	for key, product in pairs(Monetization.Products) do
		if product.Id ~= 0 and product.Id == assetId then
			return key, product
		end
	end
	return nil, nil
end

function Monetization.IsConfigured(id: number?): boolean
	return type(id) == "number" and id > 0
end

return Monetization

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/shared/PlayerData.luau
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- ============================================================================
--!nonstrict
-- PlayerData: default schema, derived values, validation and the client snapshot.
--
-- Every value that crosses the network is produced by `PlayerData.Snapshot`, so
-- the client always receives a complete picture and never has to merge partial
-- updates (a partial payload used to blank out `UnlockedPlanes` and friends).

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Shared = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")

local GameConfig = require(Shared:WaitForChild("GameConfig"))
local Monetization = require(Shared:WaitForChild("Monetization"))
local Quests = require(Shared:WaitForChild("Quests"))
local Stats = require(Shared:WaitForChild("Stats"))

local PlayerData = {}

local SAVE_VERSION = 2

function PlayerData.Default()
	return {
		Version = SAVE_VERSION,
		Coins = 0,
		Gems = 0,
		Upgrades = { Damage = 1, Health = 1, Speed = 1, Magnet = 1, Luck = 1, FireRate = 1 },
		UnlockedPlanes = { Classic = true },
		UnlockedWeapons = { Dart = true },
		UnlockedAllies = {},
		UnlockedShields = { None = true },
		Eggs = {},
		EquippedPlane = "Classic",
		EquippedCharacter = "Pilot",
		EquippedHangar = "Cardboard",
		EquippedShield = "None",
		EquippedWeapon = "Dart",
		Allies = {},
		Rebirths = 0,
		Prestige = { HeadStart = 0, Coins = 0, Armor = 0, Gems = 0 },
		BestDistance = 0,
		BestKills = 0,
		TotalKills = 0,
		TotalDistance = 0,
		TotalCoinsEarned = 0,
		TotalGemsEarned = 0,
		Runs = 0,
		BossesBeaten = {},
		DailyStreak = 0,
		LastDailyDay = 0,
		ReviveTokens = 0,
		Quests = { Day = 0, List = {} },
		Achievements = {},
		CodesRedeemed = {},
		CreatedAt = os.time(),
		LastSeen = os.time(),
	}
end

function PlayerData.UpgradeLevel(data, kind: string): number
	local v = data.Upgrades and data.Upgrades[kind]
	if type(v) ~= "number" then
		return 1
	end
	return math.max(1, math.floor(v))
end

function PlayerData.GetLevel(data): number
	local totalDistance = tonumber(data.TotalDistance) or 0
	local rebirths = tonumber(data.Rebirths) or 0
	return 1 + math.floor(totalDistance / 150) + rebirths * 4
end

function PlayerData.GetStats(data, perks)
	return Stats.Compute({
		Upgrades = data.Upgrades,
		EquippedPlane = data.EquippedPlane,
		EquippedWeapon = data.EquippedWeapon,
		EquippedShield = data.EquippedShield,
		Allies = data.Allies,
		Rebirths = data.Rebirths,
		TotalDistance = data.TotalDistance,
		Perks = perks,
	})
end

function PlayerData.UpgradeCost(kind: string, level: number): number
	return GameConfig.UpgradeCost(kind, level)
end

function PlayerData.AllyLimit(data, perks): number
	local extra = (perks and perks.ExtraAllySlots) or 0
	return math.min(GameConfig.MaxAllySlots, GameConfig.MaxAllies + extra)
end

function PlayerData.CanRebirth(data): boolean
	if PlayerData.GetLevel(data) >= GameConfig.Rebirth.RequiredLevel then
		return true
	end
	local beaten = 0
	if type(data.BossesBeaten) == "table" then
		for _ in pairs(data.BossesBeaten) do
			beaten += 1
		end
	end
	return beaten >= GameConfig.Rebirth.AltBossCount
end

-- Coerce unknown/garbage saved values into a safe schema.
function PlayerData.Sanitize(data)
	local clean = PlayerData.Default()
	if type(data) ~= "table" then
		return clean
	end

	local function int(value, minimum: number?, maximum: number?): number
		local n = math.floor(tonumber(value) or 0)
		if minimum then
			n = math.max(minimum, n)
		end
		if maximum then
			n = math.min(maximum, n)
		end
		return n
	end

	clean.Coins = int(data.Coins, 0, 1e12)
	clean.Gems = int(data.Gems, 0, 1e9)
	clean.Rebirths = int(data.Rebirths, 0, 500)
	clean.BestDistance = int(data.BestDistance, 0, 1e7)
	clean.BestKills = int(data.BestKills, 0, 1e6)
	clean.TotalKills = int(data.TotalKills, 0, 1e8)
	clean.TotalDistance = int(data.TotalDistance, 0, 1e9)
	clean.TotalCoinsEarned = int(data.TotalCoinsEarned, 0, 1e12)
	clean.TotalGemsEarned = int(data.TotalGemsEarned, 0, 1e9)
	clean.Runs = int(data.Runs, 0, 1e7)
	clean.DailyStreak = int(data.DailyStreak, 0, 365)
	clean.LastDailyDay = int(data.LastDailyDay, 0, 1e9)
	clean.ReviveTokens = int(data.ReviveTokens, 0, 99)
	clean.CreatedAt = int(data.CreatedAt, 0, os.time() + 86400)
	clean.LastSeen = int(data.LastSeen, 0, os.time() + 86400)

	if type(data.Upgrades) == "table" then
		for kind in pairs(GameConfig.Upgrades) do
			local v = tonumber(data.Upgrades[kind])
			if v then
				clean.Upgrades[kind] = int(v, 1, 1e6)
			end
		end
	end

	local function unlocked(source, catalogue, into, always)
		if type(source) == "table" then
			for id in pairs(source) do
				if catalogue[id] then
					into[id] = true
				end
			end
		end
		if always then
			into[always] = true
		end
	end

	unlocked(data.UnlockedPlanes, GameConfig.Planes, clean.UnlockedPlanes, "Classic")
	unlocked(data.UnlockedWeapons, GameConfig.Weapons, clean.UnlockedWeapons, "Dart")
	unlocked(data.UnlockedAllies, GameConfig.Allies, clean.UnlockedAllies)
	unlocked(data.UnlockedShields, GameConfig.Shields, clean.UnlockedShields, "None")
	unlocked(data.BossesBeaten, { [""] = true }, clean.BossesBeaten)

	-- Boss keys are zone ids, not catalogue keys, so validate them directly.
	if type(data.BossesBeaten) == "table" then
		table.clear(clean.BossesBeaten)
		for zoneId in pairs(data.BossesBeaten) do
			if GameConfig.GetZoneById(zoneId) then
				clean.BossesBeaten[zoneId] = true
			end
		end
	end

	if type(data.Eggs) == "table" then
		for _, eggId in ipairs(data.Eggs) do
			if GameConfig.Eggs[eggId] and #clean.Eggs < 50 then
				table.insert(clean.Eggs, eggId)
			end
		end
	end

	local allyLimit = GameConfig.MaxAllySlots
	if type(data.Allies) == "table" then
		for _, id in ipairs(data.Allies) do
			if GameConfig.Allies[id] and #clean.Allies < allyLimit then
				table.insert(clean.Allies, id)
				clean.UnlockedAllies[id] = true
			end
		end
	end

	if GameConfig.Characters[data.EquippedCharacter] then
		clean.EquippedCharacter = data.EquippedCharacter
	end
	if GameConfig.Hangars[data.EquippedHangar] then
		clean.EquippedHangar = data.EquippedHangar
	end
	if GameConfig.Shields[data.EquippedShield] then
		clean.EquippedShield = data.EquippedShield
	end
	if GameConfig.Planes[data.EquippedPlane] and clean.UnlockedPlanes[data.EquippedPlane] then
		clean.EquippedPlane = data.EquippedPlane
	end
	if GameConfig.Weapons[data.EquippedWeapon] and clean.UnlockedWeapons[data.EquippedWeapon] then
		clean.EquippedWeapon = data.EquippedWeapon
	end

	if type(data.Achievements) == "table" then
		for id in pairs(data.Achievements) do
			if id == "list" or type(id) == "string" then
				clean.Achievements[id] = true
			end
		end
	end
	if type(data.CodesRedeemed) == "table" then
		for code in pairs(data.CodesRedeemed) do
			if type(code) == "string" and #clean.CodesRedeemed < 200 then
				clean.CodesRedeemed[string.upper(code)] = true
			end
		end
	end

	if type(data.Prestige) == "table" then
		for kind, entry in pairs(GameConfig.Prestige) do
			local value = tonumber(data.Prestige[kind])
			if value then
				clean.Prestige[kind] = int(value, 0, entry.Max)
			end
		end
	end

	if type(data.Quests) == "table" and type(data.Quests.List) == "table" then
		clean.Quests.Day = int(data.Quests.Day, 0, 1e9)
		for _, entry in ipairs(data.Quests.List) do
			if type(entry) == "table" and type(entry.Id) == "string" and #clean.Quests.List < 12 then
				table.insert(clean.Quests.List, {
					Id = entry.Id,
					Progress = int(entry.Progress, 0, 1e9),
					Claimed = entry.Claimed == true,
				})
			end
		end
	end

	return clean
end

-- Build (or refresh) today's daily quest list. Returns true if it changed.
function PlayerData.EnsureDailyQuests(data): boolean
	local day = Quests.UtcDay()
	if data.Quests.Day == day and #data.Quests.List > 0 then
		return false
	end
	local rotation = Quests.RotationForDay(day)
	local list = {}
	for _, template in ipairs(rotation) do
		table.insert(list, { Id = template.Id, Progress = 0, Claimed = false })
	end
	data.Quests = { Day = day, List = list }
	return true
end

function PlayerData.FindQuestTemplate(id: string)
	for _, template in ipairs(Quests.Templates) do
		if template.Id == id then
			return template
		end
	end
	return nil
end

-- The single payload the client receives, both on load and on every update.
function PlayerData.Snapshot(data, perks, ownedPasses)
	return {
		Coins = data.Coins,
		Gems = data.Gems,
		Upgrades = data.Upgrades,
		UnlockedPlanes = data.UnlockedPlanes,
		UnlockedWeapons = data.UnlockedWeapons,
		UnlockedAllies = data.UnlockedAllies,
		UnlockedShields = data.UnlockedShields,
		Eggs = data.Eggs,
		EquippedPlane = data.EquippedPlane,
		EquippedWeapon = data.EquippedWeapon,
		EquippedShield = data.EquippedShield,
		EquippedCharacter = data.EquippedCharacter,
		EquippedHangar = data.EquippedHangar,
		Allies = data.Allies,
		Rebirths = data.Rebirths,
		Prestige = data.Prestige,
		BestDistance = data.BestDistance,
		BestKills = data.BestKills,
		TotalKills = data.TotalKills,
		TotalDistance = data.TotalDistance,
		TotalCoinsEarned = data.TotalCoinsEarned,
		TotalGemsEarned = data.TotalGemsEarned,
		Runs = data.Runs,
		BossesBeaten = data.BossesBeaten,
		DailyStreak = data.DailyStreak,
		LastDailyDay = data.LastDailyDay,
		ReviveTokens = data.ReviveTokens or 0,
		Quests = data.Quests,
		Achievements = data.Achievements,
		CodesRedeemed = data.CodesRedeemed,
		Level = PlayerData.GetLevel(data),
		CanRebirth = PlayerData.CanRebirth(data),
		Day = Quests.UtcDay(),
		Perks = perks or Monetization.EmptyPerks(),
		OwnedPasses = ownedPasses or {},
		Event = GameConfig.GetActiveEvent(),
		AllyLimit = PlayerData.AllyLimit(data, perks),
	}
end

return PlayerData

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/shared/Quests.luau
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
--!nonstrict
-- Quests: daily missions, achievements and redeemable codes.
--
-- Three daily quests are picked deterministically from `Templates` using the
-- UTC day number as the seed, so every server agrees on today's rotation
-- without storing anything extra. Progress accumulates from run results and is
-- persisted per player.

local Quests = {}

Quests.DailyCount = 3

-- `Type` is the counter the server increments:
--   Kills | Distance | Coins | Bosses | Runs | Gems
Quests.Templates = {
	{ Id = "kill20", Type = "Kills", Amount = 20, Coins = 200, Gems = 1, Text = "Destroy %d enemies" },
	{ Id = "kill60", Type = "Kills", Amount = 60, Coins = 450, Gems = 2, Text = "Destroy %d enemies" },

-- Codes are permanent one-off grants. Case-insensitive on redeem.
Quests.Codes = {
	PAPERWINGS = { Coins = 1000, Gems = 3, Note = "Launch code" },
	ORIGAMI = { Coins = 1500, Gems = 5, Note = "Fold it" },
	FIRSTFLIGHT = { Coins = 500, Gems = 1, Note = "Welcome aboard" },
	SCISSORS = { Coins = 2500, Gems = 8, Note = "Snip snip" },
	INKY = { Coins = 2000, Gems = 6, Note = "Mind the ink" },

	THANKS = { Coins = 750, Gems = 2, Note = "Thanks for playing" },
}

function Quests.UtcDay(now: number?): number
	local t = now or os.time()
	return math.floor(t / 86400)
end

-- Stable per-day selection: same day -> same quests on every server.
function Quests.RotationForDay(day: number)
	local pool = {}
	for i, template in ipairs(Quests.Templates) do
		pool[i] = template
	end

	-- Deterministic Fisher-Yates seeded by the day number.
	local seed = day % 2147483647
	local function nextRandom()
		seed = (seed * 1103515245 + 12345) % 2147483648
		return seed / 2147483648
	end
	for i = #pool, 2, -1 do
		local j = math.floor(nextRandom() * i) + 1
		pool[i], pool[j] = pool[j], pool[i]
	end

	local picked = {}
	for i = 1, math.min(Quests.DailyCount, #pool) do
		picked[i] = pool[i]
	end
	return picked
end

function Quests.QuestText(template)
	return string.format(template.Text, template.Amount)
end

return Quests
