--!strict
-- Remote channel names for Paper Wings.
-- The server creates these under ReplicatedStorage.Remotes on startup.
-- The client waits for them by name; nothing here creates instances.

local Remotes = {}

Remotes.Folder = "Remotes"

-- client -> server
Remotes.RunResult = "RunResult" -- run outcome (validated server-side)
Remotes.Purchase = "Purchase" -- buy upgrade / plane / weapon / ally / shield / egg
Remotes.Rebirth = "Rebirth" -- perform rebirth
Remotes.RequestStart = "RequestStart" -- ask to begin a run
Remotes.RequestData = "RequestData" -- ask for the full player snapshot
Remotes.PromptPass = "PromptPass" -- request a gamepass purchase prompt
Remotes.PromptProduct = "PromptProduct" -- request a dev-product purchase prompt
Remotes.ClaimQuest = "ClaimQuest" -- claim a completed daily quest
Remotes.ClaimDaily = "ClaimDaily" -- claim today's login reward
Remotes.RedeemCode = "RedeemCode" -- redeem a promo code
Remotes.RequestLeaderboard = "RequestLeaderboard" -- ask for the global top flights
Remotes.UseRevive = "UseRevive" -- consume a purchased revive token

-- server -> client
Remotes.LeaderboardData = "LeaderboardData" -- leaderboard payload
Remotes.DataLoaded = "DataLoaded" -- full player snapshot
Remotes.DataUpdate = "DataUpdate" -- full player snapshot after a change
Remotes.RunEvent = "RunEvent" -- run lifecycle (ended, zone, etc.)
Remotes.ShopSync = "ShopSync" -- purchase result (success, message)
Remotes.Notify = "Notify" -- transient toast message

function Remotes.GetFolder(): Folder
	return game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild(Remotes.Folder)
end

function Remotes.Get(name: string): RemoteEvent
	return Remotes.GetFolder():WaitForChild(name) :: RemoteEvent
end

return Remotes

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/shared/Stats.luau
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- ============================================================================
--!nonstrict
-- Stats: shared derivation of a player's effective combat numbers.
-- Used by the server (for reward math) and the client (for the HUD), so both
-- sides always agree on what the player's loadout is worth.

local GameConfig = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("GameConfig"))
local Monetization = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("Monetization"))

local Stats = {}

function Stats.Compute(data)
	local upgrades = data.Upgrades or {}
	local levels = {
		Damage = math.max(1, math.floor(tonumber(upgrades.Damage) or 1)),
		Health = math.max(1, math.floor(tonumber(upgrades.Health) or 1)),
		Speed = math.max(1, math.floor(tonumber(upgrades.Speed) or 1)),
		Magnet = math.max(1, math.floor(tonumber(upgrades.Magnet) or 1)),
		Luck = math.max(1, math.floor(tonumber(upgrades.Luck) or 1)),
		FireRate = math.max(1, math.floor(tonumber(upgrades.FireRate) or 1)),
	}

	local planeId = data.EquippedPlane or "Classic"
	if not GameConfig.Planes[planeId] then
		planeId = "Classic"
	end

	-- Prestige head start raises every upgrade level before plane multipliers.
	local prestige = data.Prestige or {}
	local headStart = math.max(0, math.floor(tonumber(prestige.HeadStart) or 0))
	if headStart > 0 then
		for kind in pairs(levels) do
			levels[kind] += headStart
		end
	end

	local base = GameConfig.PlaneStats(planeId, levels)
	local rebirths = math.max(0, math.floor(tonumber(data.Rebirths) or 0))
	local perks = data.Perks or Monetization.EmptyPerks()

	-- Rebirth is additive-then-multiplicative so its value is legible.
	base.Damage *= (1 + GameConfig.Rebirth.DamageBonus) ^ rebirths
	base.MaxHealth *= (1 + GameConfig.Rebirth.HealthBonus) ^ rebirths
	base.CoinMult *= (1 + GameConfig.Rebirth.CoinBonus) ^ rebirths

	-- Gamepass/gamepass-like perks.
	base.Damage *= perks.DamageMult or 1
	base.MaxHealth *= perks.HealthMult or 1
	base.Speed *= perks.SpeedMult or 1
	base.CoinMult *= perks.CoinMult or 1
	base.GemMult = (perks.GemMult or 1) * (1 + 0.10 * rebirths)
	base.MagnetRadius *= perks.MagnetMult or 1
	base.Luck *= perks.LuckMult or 1

	-- Crafted prestige ranks (permanent, survive rebirth).
	base.CoinMult *= 1 + 0.05 * math.max(0, math.floor(tonumber(prestige.Coins) or 0))
	base.MaxHealth *= 1 + 0.05 * math.max(0, math.floor(tonumber(prestige.Armor) or 0))
	base.GemMult *= 1 + 0.05 * math.max(0, math.floor(tonumber(prestige.Gems) or 0))
	base.PrestigeHeadStart = headStart
	base.ExtraProjectiles = perks.ExtraProjectiles or 0
	base.Revives = perks.Revives or 0
	base.AutoCollect = perks.AutoCollect or false
	base.Vip = perks.Vip or false
	base.ExtraAllySlots = perks.ExtraAllySlots or 0

	base.Rebirths = rebirths
	base.PlaneId = planeId
	base.WeaponId = data.EquippedWeapon or "Dart"
	base.ShieldId = data.EquippedShield or "None"
	base.Allies = data.Allies or {}
	base.Pets = data.Allies or {}
	base.Level = 1 + math.floor((tonumber(data.TotalDistance) or 0) / 150) + rebirths * 4
	base.UpgradeLevels = levels

	return base
end

return Stats

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/shared/Utility.luau
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
--!strict
-- Small shared helpers used by both server and client.

local Utility = {}

local GameConfigRef = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("GameConfig"))

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

function Utility.GetShared()
	return ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared")
end

function Utility.Clamp(value: number, min: number, max: number): number
	if value < min then
		return min
	elseif value > max then
		return max
	end
	return value
end

function Utility.Lerp(a: number, b: number, t: number): number
	return a + (b - a) * t
end

function Utility.LerpVector3(a: Vector3, b: Vector3, t: number): Vector3
	return a:Lerp(b, Utility.Clamp(t, 0, 1))
end

local suffixes = { "", "K", "M", "B", "T", "Q" }

function Utility.FormatNumber(value: number): string
	local n = math.floor(value + 0.5)
	if n < 1000 then
		return tostring(n)
	end
	local idx = 1
	local v = n
	while v >= 1000 and idx < #suffixes do
		v = v / 1000
		idx += 1
	end
	return string.format("%.1f%s", v, suffixes[idx])
end

function Utility.Round(value: number, decimals: number): number
	local m = 10 ^ decimals
	return math.floor(value * m + 0.5) / m
end

-- Smooth enemy scaling (see GameConfig.EnemyHealthMult for the reasoning).
function Utility.ScaleHealth(base: number, distance: number): number
	return base * GameConfigRef.EnemyHealthMult(distance)
end

function Utility.ScaleDamage(base: number, distance: number): number
	return base * GameConfigRef.EnemyDamageMult(distance)
end

return Utility
